--- FILE: C:\Users\ADMIN\Documents\Nam\Project\Programmer being\Discord\get-all.ps1 ---
Get-ChildItem -Recurse -File |
    Where-Object {
        $_.FullName -notmatch '\\(node_modules|data|out|dist)\\' -and
        $_.Extension -notin '.db', '.zip', '.db-wal', '.db-shm', '.rar', '.env', '.vsix', '.exe' -and
        $_.Name -notin 'package-lock.json'
    } |
    ForEach-Object {
        "--- FILE: $($_.FullName) ---`n$(Get-Content $_.FullName -Raw)`n`n"
    } |
    Out-File -FilePath "all_contents.txt" -Encoding utf8;
notepad "all_contents.txt"

--- FILE: C:\Users\ADMIN\Documents\Nam\Project\Programmer being\Discord\main.js ---
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//                  INTERACTIVE DISCORD BOT LAUNCHER                  //
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

const { spawn } = require('child_process');
const readline = require('readline');

console.clear();
console.log('='.repeat(70));
console.log('  MrFish Discord Bot - Interactive Control Panel');
console.log('='.repeat(70));
console.log('');

let botProcess = null;
let isRunning = false;

// Setup readline for keyboard input
const rl = readline.createInterface({
    input: process.stdin,
    output: process.stdout,
    prompt: '>> '
});

// Show menu
function showMenu() {
    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log('  🎮 CONTROLS');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log('  [H] + Enter → 🧹 Clear NPM cache');
    console.log('  [S] + Enter → 📺 Clear console output');
    console.log('  [R] + Enter → 🔄 Restart bot');
    console.log('  [V] + Enter →  ℹ  Show info');
    console.log('  [C] + Enter → 🛑 Stop bot');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log('');
}

// Start the bot
function startBot() {
    if (isRunning) {
        console.log('⚠️  Bot is already running!');
        return;
    }

    console.log('🚀 Starting Discord bot...');
    console.log('');

    // Spawn bot process
    botProcess = spawn('node', ['src/index.js'], {
        stdio: ['inherit', 'inherit', 'inherit']
    });

    isRunning = true;

    botProcess.on('close', (code) => {
        isRunning = false;
        if (code !== 0) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(`❌ Bot exited with code ${code}`);
        } else {
            console.log('');
            console.log('✅ Bot stopped gracefully');
        }
        showMenu();
    });

    botProcess.on('error', (err) => {
        console.error('❌ Failed to start bot:', err);
        isRunning = false;
        showMenu();
    });
}

// Stop the bot
function stopBot() {
    if (!isRunning) {
        console.log('⚠️  Bot is not running!');
        return;
    }

    console.log('🛑 Stopping bot...');

    if (botProcess) {
        botProcess.kill('SIGTERM');

        // Force kill if not stopped in 5 seconds
        setTimeout(() => {
            if (isRunning) {
                console.log('⚠️  Forcing shutdown...');
                botProcess.kill('SIGKILL');
            }
        }, 5000);
    }
}

// Restart the bot
function restartBot() {
    console.log('🔄 Restarting bot...');

    if (isRunning) {
        stopBot();
        // Wait for bot to stop, then start again
        setTimeout(() => {
            startBot();
        }, 2000);
    } else {
        startBot();
    }
}

// Clear NPM cache
function clearCache() {
    console.log('🧹 Clearing NPM cache...');

    const { execSync } = require('child_process');

    try {
        execSync('npm cache clean --force', {
            encoding: 'utf-8',
            stdio: 'inherit'
        });
        console.log('✅ Cache cleared successfully!');
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('❌ Failed to clear cache:', err.message);
    }
}

// Clear console
function clearConsole() {
    console.clear();
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log('  MrFish Discord Bot - Interactive Control Panel');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log('');
    console.log('📺 Console cleared!');
    showMenu();
}

/**
 * The function `showInfo` provides detailed information about the bot, including status, Node.js
 * version, OS platform, memory usage, uptime, CPU info, RAM info, project version, author, and file
 * checks.
 */
function showInfo() {
    const os = require('os');
    const fs = require('fs');
    const { execSync } = require('child_process');

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log('  ℹ️  BOT INFORMATION');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    // Bot status
    console.log(`📊 Status: ${isRunning ? '🟢 Running' : '🔴 Stopped'}`);
    console.log(`💻 Node.js: ${process.version}`);

    // Get detailed OS info
    let osInfo = '';
    const platform = os.platform();
    const arch = os.arch() === 'x64' ? '64-bit' : os.arch();

    if (platform === 'win32') {
        try {
            const winVer = execSync('wmic os get Caption', { encoding: 'utf-8' });
            const match = winVer.match(/Windows\s+(\d+|[\w\s]+)/i);
            osInfo = match ? `${match[0].trim()} ${arch}` : `Windows ${arch}`;
        } catch (err) {
            osInfo = `Windows ${arch}`;
        }
    } else if (platform === 'linux') {
        try {
            const osRelease = execSync('cat /etc/os-release', { encoding: 'utf-8' });
            const nameMatch = osRelease.match(/PRETTY_NAME="(.+)"/);
            osInfo = nameMatch ? `${nameMatch[1]} ${arch}` : `Linux ${arch}`;
        } catch (err) {
            osInfo = `Linux ${arch}`;
        }
    } else if (platform === 'darwin') {
        try {
            const macVer = execSync('sw_vers -productVersion', { encoding: 'utf-8' }).trim();
            osInfo = `macOS ${macVer} ${arch}`;
        } catch (err) {
            osInfo = `macOS ${arch}`;
        }
    } else {
        osInfo = `${platform} ${arch}`;
    }

    console.log(`🖥️  Platform: ${osInfo}`);
    console.log(`📦 Memory: ${(process.memoryUsage().heapUsed / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB`);
    console.log(`⏱️  Uptime: ${Math.floor(process.uptime())} seconds`);

    // CPU info (bonus!)
    const cpus = os.cpus();
    if (cpus.length > 0) {
        console.log(`🔧 CPU: ${cpus[0].model}`);
        console.log(`⚡ Cores: ${cpus.length}`);
    }

    // RAM info (bonus!)
    const totalRAM = (os.totalmem() / 1024 / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2);
    const freeRAM = (os.freemem() / 1024 / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2);
    console.log(`💾 RAM: ${freeRAM}GB free / ${totalRAM}GB total`);

    // Project info
    try {
        const pkg = require('./package.json');
        console.log(`📌 Version: ${pkg.version}`);
        console.log(`👤 Author: ${pkg.author}`);
    } catch (err) {
        // Ignore
    }

    // Check files
    const hasEnv = fs.existsSync('.env');
    const hasNodeModules = fs.existsSync('node_modules');

    console.log(`📄 .env file: ${hasEnv ? '✅ Found' : '❌ Missing'}`);
    console.log(`📦 node_modules: ${hasNodeModules ? '✅ Found' : '❌ Missing'}`);

    console.log('='.repeat(70));
}

// Handle keyboard input
rl.on('line', (input) => {
    const cmd = input.trim().toLowerCase();

    switch (cmd) {
        case 'm':
            showMenu();
            break
        case 'h':
            clearCache();
            break;

        case 's':
            clearConsole();
            break;

        case 'r':
            restartBot();
            break;

        case 'v':
            showInfo();
            break;

        case 'c':
            stopBot();
            break;

        case 'exit':
        case 'quit':
        case 'q':
            console.log('👋 Goodbye!');
            if (isRunning) {
                stopBot();
            }
            setTimeout(() => {
                process.exit(0);
            }, 1000);
            break;

        default:
            if (cmd !== '') {
                console.log(`❌ Unknown command: ${cmd}`);
            }
    }
});

// Handle Ctrl+C
process.on('SIGINT', () => {
    console.log('');
    console.log('👋 Shutting down...');

    if (isRunning) {
        stopBot();
        setTimeout(() => {
            process.exit(0);
        }, 2000);
    } else {
        process.exit(0);
    }
});

// Show menu and auto-start bot
console.log('🤖 Auto-starting bot...');
console.log('');
console.log('');
startBot();

--- FILE: C:\Users\ADMIN\Documents\Nam\Project\Programmer being\Discord\minecraft_bots\bot.js ---
// Mineflayer Bot for Python Bridge
// Supports Minecraft 1.21.1 and all versions

const mineflayer = require('mineflayer');
const fs = require('fs');

// Read config from command line argument
const configFile = process.argv[2];

if (!configFile) {
    console.error('Usage: node bot.js <config.json>');
    process.exit(1);
}
// Load config
let config;
try {
    config = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(configFile, 'utf8'));
} catch (err) {
    console.error(`Failed to load config: ${err.message}`);
    process.exit(1);
}

console.log(`[${config.username}] Starting bot...`);
console.log(`[${config.username}] Server: ${config.host}:${config.port}`);
console.log(`[${config.username}] Version: ${config.version}`);

// Create bot
const bot = mineflayer.createBot({
    host: config.host,
    port: config.port,
    username: config.username,
    version: config.version,
    auth: 'offline',
    hideErrors: false,
    checkTimeoutInterval: 60000, // Check for timeout every 60 seconds instead of 30
    keepAlive: true
});

let isRegistered = config.registered || false;
let isLoggedIn = false;

// Bot spawned
bot.once('spawn', () => {
    console.log(`[${config.username}] ✅ Connected and spawned!`);
    // Send login commands after spawn
    setTimeout(() => {
        if (!isRegistered) {
            bot.chat(`/register ${config.password} ${config.password}`);
            console.log(`[${config.username}] 📝 Sent /register command`);
            isRegistered = true;
            
            setTimeout(() => {
                bot.chat(`/login ${config.password}`);
                console.log(`[${config.username}] 🔐 Sent /login command`);
                isLoggedIn = true;
            }, 1000);
        } else {
            bot.chat(`/login ${config.password}`);
            console.log(`[${config.username}] 🔐 Sent /login command`);
            isLoggedIn = true;
        }
    }, 2000);
});

// Chat messages
bot.on('message', (message) => {
    const msg = message.toString().toLowerCase();
    
    // Detect login prompts
    if ((msg.includes('/login') || msg.includes('log in')) && !isLoggedIn) {
        setTimeout(() => {
            bot.chat(`/login ${config.password}`);
            console.log(`[${config.username}] 🔐 Auto-login triggered by prompt`);
            isLoggedIn = true;
        }, 1000);
    }
    
    // Detect register prompts
    if ((msg.includes('/register') || msg.includes('register')) && !isRegistered) {
        setTimeout(() => {
            bot.chat(`/register ${config.password} ${config.password}`);
            console.log(`[${config.username}] 📝 Auto-register triggered by prompt`);
            isRegistered = true;
        }, 1000);
    }
    
    // Detect successful login
    if (msg.includes('logged in') || msg.includes('successfully') || msg.includes('welcome')) {
        console.log(`[${config.username}] ✅ Login confirmed!`);
        isLoggedIn = true;
    }
});

// Anti-AFK (random movements every 10 seconds)
setInterval(() => {
    if (bot.entity) {
        const movements = ['forward', 'back', 'left', 'right', 'jump'];
        const move = movements[Math.floor(Math.random() * movements.length)];
        
        bot.setControlState(move, true);
        setTimeout(() => {
            bot.clearControlStates();
        }, 500);
    }
}, 10000);

// Error handling
bot.on('error', (err) => {
    console.error(`[${config.username}] ❌ Error: ${err.message}`);
    // Don't exit on timeout errors, try to reconnect
    if (err.message.includes('timed out') || err.message.includes('timeout')) {
        console.log(`[${config.username}] ⏱️ Connection timeout, bot will attempt to reconnect...`);
        setTimeout(() => {
            console.log(`[${config.username}] 🔄 Reconnecting...`);
            process.exit(1); // Let PM2 or your process manager restart it
        }, 5000);
    }
});

bot.on('kicked', (reason) => {
    console.log(`[${config.username}] ⚠️ Kicked: ${reason}`);
    // Wait before exiting to allow for reconnection
    setTimeout(() => {
        process.exit(1);
    }, 5000);
});

bot.on('end', (reason) => {
    console.log(`[${config.username}] 🔌 Disconnected: ${reason || 'Unknown'}`);
    // Wait before exiting to allow for reconnection
    setTimeout(() => {
        process.exit(1);
    }, 5000);
});

// Handle process termination
process.on('SIGINT', () => {
    console.log(`[${config.username}] 🛑 Stopping bot...`);
    bot.quit();
    process.exit(0);
});

process.on('SIGTERM', () => {
    console.log(`[${config.username}] 🛑 Stopping bot...`);
    bot.quit();
    process.exit(0);
});

console.log(`[${config.username}] 🤖 Bot is running...`);

async function deployCommands() {
    const rest = new REST({ version: '10' }).setToken(config.DISCORD_TOKEN);

    try {
        console.log('🔄 Registering slash commands...');

        commands.push(
            gemsCommands.gems.data,
            gemsCommands.gemshop.data,
            xpCommands.xp.data,
            xpCommands.xptop.data,
            xpCommands.xpglobal.data
        );

        // Register commands globally
        await rest.put(
            Routes.applicationCommands(config.CLIENT_ID),
            { body: commands }
        );


        console.log('✅ Successfully registered slash commands globally!');
        console.log(`📝 Registered ${commands.length} commands`);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Error registering slash commands:', error);
    }
}

module.exports = { deployCommands, commands };

///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//                    DISCORD BOT v1.0.4 - NODE.JS VERSION           //

// Developed by @Orange225673
// Contributors: Huỳnh Gia Kiệt, Hoàng Thanh Trúc

// GITHUB: @orange225673

// Note: Only change if got permission by Owner!

// Main import
require('dotenv').config();
const { Client, GatewayIntentBits, Collection, ActivityType, EmbedBuilder } = require('discord.js');


// Import configs
const config = require('./config');
const DatabaseManager = require('./utils/DatabaseManager');
const { checkSpam } = require('./utils/spamDetector');
const { setupSlashCommands } = require('./slashHandler');
const { deployCommands } = require('./deployCommands');

// Import systems
const economySystem = require('./systems/economySystem');
const mathSystem = require('./systems/mathSystem');
const pollSystem = require('./systems/pollSystem');
const reminderSystem = require('./systems/reminderSystem');
const aiSystem = require('./systems/aiSystem');
const xpSystem = require('./systems/xpSystem');

// Import command

const funCommands = require('./commands/fun');
const moderationCommands = require('./commands/moderation');
const statsCommands = require('./commands/stats');
const adminCommands = require('./commands/admin');
const reminderCommands = require('./commands/reminder');
const pollCommands = require('./commands/poll');
const aiCommands = require('./commands/ai');
const economyCommands = require('./commands/economy');
const chessCommands = require('./commands/chess');
const minecraftCommands = require('./commands/minecraft');
const permissionCommands = require('./commands/permissions');
const codeCommands = require('./commands/code');
const mathCommands = require('./commands/math');
const gemsCommands = require('./commands/gems');
const xpCommands = require('./commands/xp');
const enablexpCommand = require('./commands/enableXP');
const levelupCommand = require('./commands/levelup');
const calculatorCommand = require('./commands/calculator');


// Initialize bot
const client = new Client({
    intents: [
        GatewayIntentBits.Guilds,
        GatewayIntentBits.GuildMessages,
        GatewayIntentBits.MessageContent,
        GatewayIntentBits.GuildMembers,
        GatewayIntentBits.GuildMessageReactions,
        GatewayIntentBits.GuildScheduledEvents,
        GatewayIntentBits.GuildPresences
    ]
});

// Initialize data manager
const dataManager = new DatabaseManager();

// Global variables
const afkUsers = new Map();
client.commands = new Collection();
client.dataManager = dataManager;
client.config = config;
client.afkUsers = afkUsers;

console.log('⚙️  Loading systems...');

// Setup slash command handler
setupSlashCommands(client);

// Load systems
economySystem.setup(client);
mathSystem.setup(client);
pollSystem.setup(client);
reminderSystem.setup(client);
aiSystem.setup(client, config);
xpSystem.setup(client);

console.log('⚙️  Loading commands...');

// Load commands
funCommands.setup(client, afkUsers);
moderationCommands.setup(client);
statsCommands.setup(client);
adminCommands.setup(client);
reminderCommands.setup = () => { client.commands.set('remindme', reminderCommands); client.commands.set('remind', reminderCommands); client.commands.set('reminder', reminderCommands); };
reminderCommands.setup();
pollCommands.setup(client);
aiCommands.setup(client);
economyCommands.setup(client);
chessCommands.setup(client);
minecraftCommands.setup(client);
codeCommands.setup(client);
permissionCommands.setup(client);
mathCommands.setup(client);
gemsCommands.setup(client);
xpCommands.setup(client);
client.commands.set('enablexp', enablexpCommand);
client.commands.set('xpenable', enablexpCommand);
client.commands.set('levelup', levelupCommand);
client.commands.set('lvlup', levelupCommand);
client.commands.set('levelconfig', levelupCommand);
calculatorCommand.setup(client);


console.log('✅ All systems and commands loaded');

// ============ ON_READY EVENT ============
client.once('ready', async () => {
    console.log('='.repeat(50));
    console.log(`✅ Logged in as ${client.user.tag}`);
    console.log(`📊 Connected to ${client.guilds.cache.size} server(s)`);
    console.log('='.repeat(50));

    // Load afk data
    const afkData = dataManager.getAllAFK();
    afkData.forEach(data => {
        // Key có thể là userId hoặc kết hợp serverId_userId tùy nhu cầu của bạn
        // Ở đây tôi dùng userId cho đơn giản giống logic cũ của bạn
        client.afkUsers.set(data.user_id, {
            reason: data.reason,
            time: data.timestamp,
            server: data.server_id
        });
    });
    console.log(`✅ Loaded {${afkData.length}} AFK users from Database`);

    // Preload chess piece images
    const { preloadPieceImages } = require('./systems/boardRenderer');
    await preloadPieceImages();

    // Generate invite link
    if (config.CLIENT_ID) {
        const permissions_interger = 8;
        const inviteLink = `https://discord.com/api/oauth2/authorize?client_id=${config.CLIENT_ID}&permissions=${permissions_interger}&scope=bot%20applications.commands`;

        console.log('');
        console.log('🔗 Bot Invite Link:');
        console.log('   Click this link to invite the bot to your server:');
        console.log(`   ${inviteLink}`);
        console.log('');
    }

    // Deploy slash commands if CLIENT_ID is set
    if (config.CLIENT_ID) {
        await deployCommands();
    }

    // Set bot status
    client.user.setActivity(`Watching ${client.guilds.cache.size} server(s)`,
        { type: ActivityType.Watching });

    // Ensure data folders for all guilds
    client.guilds.cache.forEach(guild => {
        dataManager.ensureServer(guild.id);
    });

    setInterval(() => {
        dataManager.cleanExpiredBoosts();
        console.log('Cleaned expired gem shop boosts');
    }, 3600000);

});

// ============ ON_GUILD_JOIN EVENT ============
client.on('guildCreate', async (guild) => {
    console.log(`👋 Joined new server: ${guild.name} (${guild.id})`);
    dataManager.ensureServer(guild.id);

    // Send DM to the server owner
    try {
        const owner = guild.members.cache.get(guild.ownerId) || await guild.fetchOwner();
        if (owner) {

            const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                .setTitle('Bot Added to Your Server!')
                .setDescription(`Thanks for inviting me to **${guild.name}**!`)
                .setColor(0xe94560)

            await owner.send({ embeds: [embed] });
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Could not DM owner:`, error.message);
    }
});

// ============ ON_MESSAGE EVENT ============
client.on('messageCreate', async (message) => {
    // Ignore bot messages
    if (message.author.bot) return;


    // 1. Kiểm tra người gửi đang AFK để gỡ bỏ
    if (client.afkUsers.has(message.author.id)) {
        const data = client.afkUsers.get(message.author.id);
        if (Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - data.time > 5) {

            client.afkUsers.delete(message.author.id); // Xóa khỏi Map
            dataManager.removeAFK(message.guild.id, message.author.id); // Xóa khỏi SQL

            const welcomeEmbed = new EmbedBuilder()
                .setColor('#2ecc71')
                .setDescription(`👋 Welcome back ${message.author}! You were AFK <t:${data.time}:R>.`);

            message.reply({ embeds: [welcomeEmbed] }).then(m => setTimeout(() => m.delete().catch(() => { }), 10000));
        }
    }

    // 2. Thông báo khi có người bị nhắc tên (mention) đang AFK
    if (message.mentions.users.size > 0) {
        message.mentions.users.forEach(user => {
            if (user.id !== message.author.id && client.afkUsers.has(user.id)) {
                const data = client.afkUsers.get(user.id);

                const notifyEmbed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setColor('#f1c40f')
                    .setAuthor({ name: `${user.username} is AFK`, iconURL: user.displayAvatarURL() })
                    .setDescription(`💬 **Reason:** ${data.reason}\n⏰ **Since:** <t:${data.time}:R>`)

                message.reply({
                    embeds: [notifyEmbed],
                    allowedMentions: { repliedUser: false }
                });
            }
        });
    }

    // Anti-spam check
    const spamWarning = await checkSpam(message, client);
    if (spamWarning) return;

    // Record message in database (per guild)
    const dbManager = new DatabaseManager();
    dbManager.recordUserMessage(message.guild.id, message.author.id);

    // Economy - earn money per message
    await economySystem.onMessageEarnMoney(message);
    // XP - earn XP per message
    await xpSystem.onMessageXPGain(message, client);


    // Process commands
    if (!message.content.startsWith(config.COMMAND_PREFIX)) return;

    const args = message.content.slice(config.COMMAND_PREFIX.length).trim().split(/ +/);
    const commandName = args.shift().toLowerCase();

    const command = client.commands.get(commandName);
    if (!command) return;



    // Check if command is enabled
    if (!dataManager.isCommandEnabled(message.guild.id, commandName)) {
        return message.reply('❌ This command is disabled on this server.');
    }

    // Execute command
    try {        // Use 'run' function for prefix commands if it exists, otherwise use 'execute'
        if (command.run && typeof command.run === 'function') {
            await command.run(message, args, client, config);
        } else if (command.execute && typeof command.execute === 'function') {
            await command.execute(message, args, client);
        } else {
            console.error(`Command ${commandName} has no run or execute function`);
            return message.reply('❌ Command not properly implemented.');
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(`❌ Error executing command ${commandName}:`, error);
        message.reply('❌ An error occurred while executing that command.');
    }
});

// Handle chess moves outside commands
client.on('messageCreate', async (message) => {
    if (message.author.bot || !message.guild || message.content.startsWith(config.COMMAND_PREFIX)) return;

    try {
        const { handleChessMove } = require('./commands/chess');
        await handleChessMove(message, client);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Chess move error:', error.message);
    }
});
// ======================== MESSAGE_DELETE
client.snipes = new Map();
client.on('messageDelete', (message) => {
    if (message.partial || message.author?.bot) return; // Bỏ qua tin nhắn cũ hoặc của bot

    let snipes = client.snipes.get(message.channel.id) || [];

    // Thêm tin nhắn mới vào đầu mảng
    snipes.unshift({
        content: message.content,
        author: message.author,
        image: message.attachments.first()?.proxyURL,
        timestamp: message.createdAt
    });

    // Giới hạn chỉ lưu 99 tin nhắn gần nhất
    if (snipes.length > 99) snipes.pop();

    client.snipes.set(message.channel.id, snipes);
});

// ============ ON_MEMBER_JOIN EVENT ============
client.on('guildMemberAdd', (member) => {
    dataManager.updateAnalytics(member.guild.id, 'join', member.guild.memberCount)
    console.log(`👋 ${member.user.tag} joined ${member.guild.name}`);
});

// ============ ON_MEMBER_REMOVE EVENT ============
client.on('guildMemberRemove', (member) => {
    dataManager.updateAnalytics(member.guild.id, 'leave', member.guild.memberCount);
    console.log(`👋 ${member.user.tag} left ${member.guild.name}`);
});

// ============ ON_REACTION_ADD EVENT ============
client.on('messageReactionAdd', async (reaction, user) => {
    if (user.bot) return;

    // Poll system reactions
    await pollSystem.onReactionAdd(reaction, user);
});

// ============ ON_INTERACTION_CREATE EVENT ============
client.on('interactionCreate', async (interaction) => {
    // Handle chess button interactions
    if (interaction.isButton() && interaction.customId.startsWith('chess_')) {
        const { handleInteraction } = require('./commands/chess');
        await handleInteraction(interaction, client);
    }

});

// ============ ERROR HANDLERS ============
process.on('unhandledRejection', (error) => {
    console.error('❌ Unhandled promise rejection:', error);
});

process.on('uncaughtException', (error) => {
    console.error('❌ Uncaught exception:', error);
    process.exit(1);
});

// ============ START BOT ============

const startBot = () => {
    console.log('Starting Discord Bot...');

    setTimeout(async () => {
        try {
            await client.login(config.DISCORD_TOKEN);
            console.log("Login request sent successfully!");
        } catch (err) {
            console.error("Login failed:", err.message);
            process.exit(1);
        }
    }, 1000);
};

startBot();

///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//                    SLASH COMMAND HANDLER                           //
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

const { EmbedBuilder, PermissionFlagsBits } = require('discord.js');
const { formatNumber, parseTime, formatTime, isOwner, randomInt } = require('./utils/helpers');
const DataManager = require('./utils/dataManager');
const DatabaseManager = require('./utils/DatabaseManager');
const { Easy, Hard } = require('./systems/mathProblems');

const dataManager = new DataManager();
const dbManager = new DatabaseManager();

function setupSlashCommands(client) {
    client.on('interactionCreate', async (interaction) => {
        if (!interaction.isChatInputCommand()) return;

        const { commandName, options } = interaction;

        try {
            // ============ DAILY COMMAND ============
            if (commandName === 'daily') {
                const lastDaily = dbManager.getDailyCooldown(interaction.guild.id, interaction.user.id);
                const now = Date.now();
                const cooldownTime = 86400000; // 24 hours

                if (lastDaily && (now - lastDaily) < cooldownTime) {
                    const timeLeft = cooldownTime - (now - lastDaily);
                    const hours = Math.floor(timeLeft / 3600000);
                    const minutes = Math.floor((timeLeft % 3600000) / 60000);
                    return interaction.reply(`⏰ You already claimed your daily! Try again in ${hours}h ${minutes}m`);
                }

                // Give reward
                const reward = randomInt(10000, 500000);
                dbManager.addUserBalance(interaction.guild.id, interaction.user.id, reward);
                dbManager.setDailyCooldown(interaction.guild.id, interaction.user.id, now);

                await interaction.reply(`🎁 You claimed your daily reward of **$${reward.toLocaleString()}**!`);
            }

            // ============ WORK COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'work') {
                const jobs = [
                    'mowed lawns', 'washed dishes', 'delivered pizzas',
                    'coded websites', 'wrote articles', 'taught classes'
                ];
                const job = jobs[Math.floor(Math.random() * jobs.length)];
                const earned = randomInt(5000, 2500000);

                dataManager.addUserBalance(interaction.guild.id, interaction.user.id, earned);

                await interaction.reply(`💼 You ${job} and earned **$${formatNumber(earned)}**!`);
            }

            // ============ GAMBLE COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'gamble') {
                const amountStr = options.getString('amount');
                const balance = dataManager.getUserBalance(interaction.guild.id, interaction.user.id);

                let betAmount = amountStr.toLowerCase() === 'all' ? balance : parseInt(amountStr);

                if (isNaN(betAmount) || betAmount < 10000) {
                    return await interaction.reply('❌ Minimum bet is **$10,000**!');
                }

                if (betAmount > balance) {
                    return await interaction.reply(`❌ You only have **$${formatNumber(balance)}**!`);
                }

                // Simple gambling
                const win = Math.random() > 0.5;
                const multiplier = win ? 2 : -1;
                const winAmount = Math.floor(betAmount * multiplier);

                dataManager.setUserBalance(
                    interaction.guild.id,
                    interaction.user.id,
                    balance + winAmount
                );

                const emoji = win ? '✨' : '💸';
                const result = win ? 'You won!' : 'You lost!';

                await interaction.reply(
                    `${emoji} **Gambling Result** ${emoji}\n\n` +
                    `${result}\n` +
                    `Bet: **$${formatNumber(betAmount)}**\n` +
                    `Result: **${multiplier > 0 ? '+' : ''}$${formatNumber(winAmount)}**\n` +
                    `New Balance: **$${formatNumber(balance + winAmount)}**`
                );
            }

            // ============ GIVE COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'give') {
                const target = options.getUser('user');
                const amount = options.getInteger('amount');

                if (target.id === interaction.user.id) {
                    return await interaction.reply('❌ You can\'t give money to yourself!');
                }

                if (target.bot) {
                    return await interaction.reply('❌ You can\'t give money to bots!');
                }

                const balance = dataManager.getUserBalance(interaction.guild.id, interaction.user.id);
                if (amount > balance) {
                    return await interaction.reply(`❌ You only have **$${formatNumber(balance)}**!`);
                }

                dataManager.addUserBalance(interaction.guild.id, interaction.user.id, -amount);
                dataManager.addUserBalance(interaction.guild.id, target.id, amount);

                await interaction.reply(`💸 You gave **$${formatNumber(amount)}** to **${target.username}**!`);
            }

            // ============ LEADERBOARD COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'leaderboard') {
                const economy = dataManager.getEconomy(interaction.guild.id);
                const sorted = Object.entries(economy).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 10);

                if (sorted.length === 0) {
                    return await interaction.reply('📭 No one has any money yet!');
                }

                let text = '**💰 Richest Users**\n\n';
                for (let i = 0; i < sorted.length; i++) {
                    const [userId, balance] = sorted[i];
                    const user = await client.users.fetch(userId).catch(() => null);
                    const name = user ? user.username : 'Unknown User';
                    text += `\`#${i + 1}\` **${name}**: $${formatNumber(balance)}\n`;
                }

                await interaction.reply(text);
            }

            // ============ ME COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'me') {
                const target = options.getUser('user') || interaction.user;
                const stats = dbManager.getUserStats(interaction.guild.id, target.id);

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle(`📊 Stats: ${target.username}`)
                    .setThumbnail(target.displayAvatarURL())
                    .setColor(0x00FFFF)
                    .addFields(
                        { name: 'Daily Messages', value: `\`${formatNumber(stats.daily_messages || 0)}\``, inline: true },
                        { name: 'Total Messages', value: `\`${formatNumber(stats.total_messages || 0)}\``, inline: true }
                    )
                    .setFooter({ text: 'Keep chatting to increase your giveaway chances!' });

                await interaction.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
            }

            // ============ TOPTOTAL COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'toptotal') {
                const topUsers = dbManager.getTopMessageSenders(interaction.guild.id, 10);

                if (topUsers.length === 0) {
                    return await interaction.reply('💭 Leaderboard is empty!');
                }

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('🏆 All-Time Leaderboard')
                    .setColor(0xFFD700);

                let description = '';
                for (let i = 0; i < topUsers.length; i++) {
                    const { user_id, total_messages } = topUsers[i];
                    const user = await client.users.fetch(user_id).catch(() => null);
                    const name = user ? user.username : 'Unknown User';
                    description += `\`#${i + 1}\` **${name}**: ${formatNumber(total_messages)} msgs\n`;
                }

                embed.setDescription(description);
                await interaction.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
            }

            // ============ TOPDAILY COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'topdaily') {
                const topDaily = dbManager.getTopDailyMessageSenders(interaction.guild.id, 10);

                if (topDaily.length === 0) {
                    return await interaction.reply('💭 Leaderboard is empty!');
                }

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('🔥 Daily Leaderboard')
                    .setColor(0xFF4500);

                let description = '';
                for (let i = 0; i < topDaily.length; i++) {
                    const { user_id, daily_messages } = topDaily[i];
                    const user = await client.users.fetch(user_id).catch(() => null);
                    const name = user ? user.username : 'Unknown User';
                    description += `\`#${i + 1}\` **${name}**: ${formatNumber(daily_messages)} msgs\n`;
                }

                embed.setDescription(description);
                await interaction.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
            }
            // ============ MATH COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'math') {

            }

            // ============ POLL COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'poll') {
                const question = options.getString('question');
                const opts = [];

                for (let i = 1; i <= 5; i++) {
                    const opt = options.getString(`option${i}`);
                    if (opt) opts.push(opt);
                }

                const reactions = ['1️⃣', '2️⃣', '3️⃣', '4️⃣', '5️⃣'];
                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle(`📊 ${question}`)
                    .setColor(0x00D4FF)
                    .setFooter({ text: `Poll by ${interaction.user.username}` })
                    .setTimestamp();

                let description = '';
                for (let i = 0; i < opts.length; i++) {
                    description += `${reactions[i]} ${opts[i]}\n`;
                }
                embed.setDescription(description);

                const msg = await interaction.reply({ embeds: [embed], fetchReply: true });

                for (let i = 0; i < opts.length; i++) {
                    await msg.react(reactions[i]);
                }
            }

            // ============ REMIND COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'remind') {
                const time = options.getString('time');
                const message = options.getString('message');

                const timeMs = parseTime(time);
                if (!timeMs) {
                    return await interaction.reply('❌ Invalid time format! Use: 10s, 5m, 1h, 2d');
                }

                await interaction.reply(`⏰ Reminder set! I'll remind you in **${formatTime(timeMs)}**`);

                setTimeout(async () => {
                    try {
                        await interaction.user.send(
                            `⏰ **Reminder**\n\n${message}\n\n` +
                            `Set in **${interaction.guild.name}**`
                        );
                    } catch {
                        interaction.channel.send(`⏰ ${interaction.user}, reminder: ${message}`);
                    }
                }, timeMs);
            }

            // ============ PING COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'ping') {
                const latency = Date.now() - interaction.createdTimestamp;
                const apiLatency = Math.round(client.ws.ping);

                await interaction.reply(
                    `🏓 Pong!\n` +
                    `📡 Latency: **${latency}ms**\n` +
                    `💓 API Latency: **${apiLatency}ms**`
                );
            }

            // ============ AFK COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'afk') {
                const reason = options.getString('reason') || 'AFK';
                const timestamp = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

                // Get afkUsers from client (passed from index.js)
                if (!client.afkUsers) {
                    client.afkUsers = new Map();
                }

                client.afkUsers.set(interaction.user.id, {
                    reason: reason,
                    time: timestamp
                });

                try {
                    await interaction.member.setNickname(`[AFK] ${interaction.member.displayName}`);
                } catch (error) {
                    // No permission to change nickname
                }

                await interaction.reply(
                    `✅ You are now AFK: **${reason}**\n` +
                    `I'll notify others when they mention you!`
                );
            }

            // ============ GIVEAWAY COMMANDS ============
            else if (commandName === 'giveaway-start') {
                // Check permissions
                const hasPermission =
                    interaction.user.id === '972306366599397427' || // Full access user
                    interaction.user.id === client.config.OWNER_ID || // Bot owner
                    interaction.member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.ManageGuild) || // Manage Server
                    (interaction.guild.id === '1360887797417902310' && // Special server
                        (interaction.member.roles.cache.has('1389454897669214358') ||
                            interaction.member.roles.cache.has('1389470511381348352'))); // Required roles

                if (!hasPermission) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Admin/Owner only!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const duration = options.getString('duration');
                const claimTime = options.getString('claim_time');
                const winners = options.getInteger('winners');
                const host = options.getUser('host');
                const prize = options.getString('prize');
                const totalMsgs = options.getInteger('total_messages') || 0;
                const dailyMsgs = options.getInteger('daily_messages') || 0;
                const requiredRole = options.getRole('required_role');
                const bypassRole = options.getRole('bypass_role');

                const { parseTime, formatTime } = require('./utils/helpers');
                const { getExtraEntries, createGiveawayEmbed } = require('./systems/giveaway/core');
                const { activeGiveaways } = require('./systems/giveaway/core');

                const durationMs = parseTime(duration);
                if (!durationMs || durationMs < 60000) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Invalid duration! Min 1m', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const claimMs = parseTime(claimTime);
                if (!claimMs || claimMs < 60000) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Invalid claim time! Min 1m', ephemeral: true });
                }

                if (winners < 1 || winners > 20) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Winners must be 1-20!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const endTime = new Date(Date.now() + durationMs);
                const extraEntries = getExtraEntries(interaction.guild.id);

                const giveawayData = {
                    prize,
                    winners,
                    host_id: host.id,
                    channel_id: interaction.channel.id,
                    guild_id: interaction.guild.id,
                    end_time: endTime.toISOString(),
                    claim_time: Math.floor(claimMs / 1000),
                    requirements: {
                        role: requiredRole ? requiredRole.id : 'None',
                        bypass: bypassRole ? bypassRole.id : 'None',
                        total_messages: totalMsgs,
                        daily_messages: dailyMsgs
                    },
                    extra_entries: extraEntries,
                    message_id: null
                };

                const embed = await createGiveawayEmbed(giveawayData, 'ACTIVE');
                const giveawayMessage = await interaction.channel.send({ embeds: [embed] });
                await giveawayMessage.react('🎉');

                giveawayData.message_id = giveawayMessage.id;

                // Save to SQLite database
                dbManager.createGiveaway(
                    interaction.guild.id,
                    giveawayMessage.id,
                    interaction.channel.id,
                    prize,
                    winners,
                    new Date(endTime).getTime(),
                    host.id,
                    Math.floor(claimMs / 1000),
                    giveawayData.requirements,
                    giveawayData.extra_entries
                );

                if (!activeGiveaways.has(interaction.guild.id.toString())) {
                    activeGiveaways.set(interaction.guild.id.toString(), new Map());
                }
                activeGiveaways.get(interaction.guild.id.toString()).set(giveawayMessage.id, giveawayData);

                await interaction.reply({ content: `✅ Giveaway started! Ends in **${formatTime(durationMs)}**`, ephemeral: true });

                // Schedule giveaway end
                const { endGiveaway } = require('./systems/giveaway/core');
                setTimeout(async () => {
                    await endGiveaway(client, interaction.guild.id, giveawayMessage.id);
                }, durationMs);
            }

            else if (commandName === 'giveaway-delete') {
                // Check permissions
                const hasPermission =
                    interaction.user.id === '972306366599397427' ||
                    interaction.user.id === client.config.OWNER_ID ||
                    interaction.member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.ManageGuild) ||
                    (interaction.guild.id === '1360887797417902310' &&
                        (interaction.member.roles.cache.has('1389454897669214358') ||
                            interaction.member.roles.cache.has('1389470511381348352')));

                if (!hasPermission) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Admin/Owner only!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const messageId = options.getString('message_id');
                const reason = options.getString('reason');

                const data = dataManager.loadFile(interaction.guild.id, 'giveaway_data.json');

                if (!data.giveaways || !data.giveaways[messageId]) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Giveaway not found!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const giveaway = data.giveaways[messageId];

                try {
                    const channel = interaction.guild.channels.cache.get(giveaway.channel_id.toString());
                    const giveawayMsg = await channel.messages.fetch(messageId);
                    await giveawayMsg.delete();
                } catch (error) {
                    // Message already deleted
                }

                delete data.giveaways[messageId];
                dataManager.saveFile(interaction.guild.id, 'giveaway_data.json', data);

                const { activeGiveaways } = require('./systems/giveaway/core');
                if (activeGiveaways.has(interaction.guild.id.toString())) {
                    activeGiveaways.get(interaction.guild.id.toString()).delete(parseInt(messageId));
                }

                await interaction.reply(`✅ Giveaway deleted! Reason: ${reason}`);
            }

            else if (commandName === 'giveaway-done') {
                // Check permissions
                const hasPermission =
                    interaction.user.id === '972306366599397427' ||
                    interaction.user.id === client.config.OWNER_ID ||
                    interaction.member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.ManageGuild) ||
                    (interaction.guild.id === '1360887797417902310' &&
                        (interaction.member.roles.cache.has('1389454897669214358') ||
                            interaction.member.roles.cache.has('1389470511381348352')));

                if (!hasPermission) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Admin/Owner only!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const messageId = options.getString('message_id');
                const winner = options.getUser('winner');

                const data = dataManager.loadFile(interaction.guild.id, 'giveaway_data.json');

                if (!data.giveaways || !data.giveaways[messageId]) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Giveaway not found!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const giveaway = data.giveaways[messageId];
                giveaway.claimed = true;
                giveaway.reroll_enabled = false;
                giveaway.claimed_by = winner.id;
                giveaway.claimed_at = new Date().toISOString();

                data.giveaways[messageId] = giveaway;
                dataManager.saveFile(interaction.guild.id, 'giveaway_data.json', data);

                await interaction.reply(`✅ Giveaway marked as claimed by ${winner}!`);
            }

            else if (commandName === 'giveaway-reroll') {
                // Check permissions
                const hasPermission =
                    interaction.user.id === '972306366599397427' ||
                    interaction.user.id === client.config.OWNER_ID ||
                    interaction.member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.ManageGuild) ||
                    (interaction.guild.id === '1360887797417902310' &&
                        (interaction.member.roles.cache.has('1389454897669214358') ||
                            interaction.member.roles.cache.has('1389470511381348352')));

                if (!hasPermission) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Admin/Owner only!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const messageId = parseInt(options.getString('message_id'));
                const { rerollGiveaway } = require('./systems/giveaway/core');

                const result = await rerollGiveaway(client, interaction.guild.id, messageId);

                if (result.success) {
                    await interaction.reply(`✅ Rerolled! New winner: ${result.message}`);
                } else {
                    await interaction.reply(`❌ Failed: ${result.message}`);
                }
            }

            else if (commandName === 'giveaway-set-ext') {
                // Check permissions
                const hasPermission =
                    interaction.user.id === '972306366599397427' ||
                    interaction.user.id === client.config.OWNER_ID ||
                    interaction.member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.ManageGuild) ||
                    (interaction.guild.id === '1360887797417902310' &&
                        (interaction.member.roles.cache.has('1389454897669214358') ||
                            interaction.member.roles.cache.has('1389470511381348352')));

                if (!hasPermission) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Admin/Owner only!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const role = options.getRole('role');
                const multiplier = options.getInteger('multiplier');

                if (multiplier < 1 || multiplier > 10) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Multiplier must be 1-10!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const data = dataManager.loadFile(interaction.guild.id, 'giveaway_data.json');

                if (!data.guild_config) data.guild_config = {};
                if (!data.guild_config.extra_entry_roles) data.guild_config.extra_entry_roles = {};

                data.guild_config.extra_entry_roles[role.id] = multiplier;
                dataManager.saveFile(interaction.guild.id, 'giveaway_data.json', data);

                await interaction.reply(`✅ ${role} now gets **${multiplier}x** entries in giveaways!`);
            }

            else if (commandName === 'giveaway-remove-ext') {
                // Check permissions
                const hasPermission =
                    interaction.user.id === '972306366599397427' ||
                    interaction.user.id === client.config.OWNER_ID ||
                    interaction.member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.ManageGuild) ||
                    (interaction.guild.id === '1360887797417902310' &&
                        (interaction.member.roles.cache.has('1389454897669214358') ||
                            interaction.member.roles.cache.has('1389470511381348352')));

                if (!hasPermission) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Admin/Owner only!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const role = options.getRole('role');

                const data = dataManager.loadFile(interaction.guild.id, 'giveaway_data.json');

                if (!data.guild_config?.extra_entry_roles?.[role.id]) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ This role doesn\'t have extra entries!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                delete data.guild_config.extra_entry_roles[role.id];
                dataManager.saveFile(interaction.guild.id, 'giveaway_data.json', data);

                await interaction.reply(`✅ Removed extra entries from ${role}`);
            }

            else if (commandName === 'giveaway-list-ext') {
                const { getExtraEntries } = require('./systems/giveaway/core');
                const extraEntries = getExtraEntries(interaction.guild.id);

                if (Object.keys(extraEntries).length === 0) {
                    return await interaction.reply('📭 No extra entry roles configured!');
                }

                let text = '**🎁 Extra Entry Roles**\n\n';
                for (const [roleId, multiplier] of Object.entries(extraEntries)) {
                    const role = interaction.guild.roles.cache.get(roleId);
                    if (role) {
                        text += `${role}: ${multiplier}x entries\n`;
                    }
                }

                await interaction.reply(text);
            }

            // ============ 8BALL COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === '8ball') {
                const responses = [
                    'Yes, definitely!', 'It is certain.', 'Without a doubt.',
                    'You may rely on it.', 'As I see it, yes.', 'Most likely.',
                    'Outlook good.', 'Signs point to yes.', 'Reply hazy, try again.',
                    'Ask again later.', 'Better not tell you now.', 'Cannot predict now.',
                    'Concentrate and ask again.', 'Don\'t count on it.', 'My reply is no.',
                    'My sources say no.', 'Outlook not so good.', 'Very doubtful.'
                ];

                const answer = responses[Math.floor(Math.random() * responses.length)];
                await interaction.reply(`🎱 ${answer}`);
            }

            // ============ ROLL COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'roll') {
                const sides = options.getInteger('sides') || 6;
                const result = Math.floor(Math.random() * sides) + 1;
                await interaction.reply(`🎲 You rolled a **${result}** (1-${sides})`);

            // ============ FLIP COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'flip') {
                const result = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'Heads' : 'Tails';
                await interaction.reply(`🪙 ${result}!`);

            // ============ MODERATION COMMANDS ============
            else if (commandName === 'kick') {
                if (!interaction.member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.KickMembers)) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ You don\'t have permission!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const target = options.getMember('user');
                const reason = options.getString('reason') || 'No reason';

                await target.kick(reason);
                await interaction.reply(`✅ Kicked **${target.user.tag}** | Reason: ${reason}`);
            }

            else if (commandName === 'ban') {
                if (!interaction.member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.BanMembers)) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ You don\'t have permission!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const target = options.getMember('user');
                const reason = options.getString('reason') || 'No reason';

                await target.ban({ reason });
                await interaction.reply(`✅ Banned **${target.user.tag}** | Reason: ${reason}`);
            }

            else if (commandName === 'timeout') {
                if (!interaction.member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.ModerateMembers)) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ You don\'t have permission!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const target = options.getMember('user');
                const duration = options.getString('duration');
                const reason = options.getString('reason') || 'No reason';

                const durationMs = parseTime(duration);
                if (!durationMs) {
                    return await interaction.reply('❌ Invalid duration!');
                }

                await target.timeout(durationMs, reason);
                await interaction.reply(`✅ Timed out **${target.user.tag}** for ${duration}`);
            }

            else if (commandName === 'clear') {

                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ You don\'t have permission!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                await interaction.channel.bulkDelete(amount, true);
                await interaction.reply({ content: `✅ Deleted **${amount}** messages!`, ephemeral: true });
            }

            else if (commandName === 'minecraft') {

            else if (commandName === 'gbl') {
                const economyCommands = require('./commands/economy');
                await economyCommands.gamble.execute(interaction, client, client.config);

            else if (commandName === 'bal') {

            // ============ CHESS COMMANDS ============
            else if (commandName === 'chess') {
                const opponent = options.getUser('opponent');
                const minutes = options.getInteger('minutes') || 10;

                if (opponent.id === interaction.user.id) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ You cannot play against yourself!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                if (opponent.bot) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ You cannot play against bots!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                if (minutes < 1 || minutes > 60) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Time must be between 1-60 minutes!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const { createPendingGame } = require('./systems/chessManager');
                const durationMs = minutes * 60000;
                createPendingGame(interaction.user.id, opponent.id, durationMs);

                await interaction.reply(`♟️ Chess challenge sent to ${opponent}!\nThey should type: \`%chess confirm @${interaction.user.username}\``);
            }

            else if (commandName === 'board') {
                const { getGameByPlayer } = require('./systems/chessManager');
                const { renderBoard } = require('./systems/boardRenderer');
                const { formatTime } = require('./systems/chessManager');
                const { EmbedBuilder, ActionRowBuilder, ButtonBuilder, ButtonStyle } = require('discord.js');

                const game = getGameByPlayer(interaction.user.id);
                if (!game) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ You are not in an active chess game!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const flipped = game.chess.turn() === 'b';
                const boardImage = await renderBoard(game.chess.fen(), game.lastMove, flipped);
                const turn = game.chess.turn() === 'w' ? 'White' : 'Black';
                const currentPlayer = game.chess.turn() === 'w' ? game.players.white : game.players.black;

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('♟️ Current Board')
                    .setColor(game.chess.turn() === 'w' ? 0xFFFFFF : 0x000000)
                    .addFields(
                        { name: '⚪ White Time', value: formatTime(game.clocks.white), inline: true },
                        { name: '⚫ Black Time', value: formatTime(game.clocks.black), inline: true },
                        { name: '🎯 Turn', value: `${turn} <@${currentPlayer}>`, inline: true }
                    )
                    .setImage('attachment://board.png');

                const buttons = new ActionRowBuilder()
                    .addComponents(
                        new ButtonBuilder()
                            .setCustomId('chess_resign')
                            .setLabel('Resign')
                            .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Danger),
                        new ButtonBuilder()
                            .setCustomId('chess_draw')
                            .setLabel('Offer Draw')
                            .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary)
                    );

                await interaction.reply({
                    embeds: [embed],
                    components: [buttons],
                    files: [{ attachment: boardImage, name: 'board.png' }]
                });

            else if (commandName === 'chesstotal') {

                }

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle(`♟️ ${interaction.guild.name} - Chess Leaderboard`)
                    .setColor(0xFFD700)
                    .setDescription('*Top 10 players (minimum 5 games)*')
                    .setTimestamp();

                leaderboard.forEach((player, index) => {
                    const medal = index === 0 ? '🥇' : index === 1 ? '🥈' : index === 2 ? '🥉' : `${index + 1}.`;
                    embed.addFields({
                        name: `${medal} <@${player.userId}>`,
                        value: `**ELO:** ${player.elo} | **W/L/D:** ${player.wins}/${player.losses}/${player.draws} | **WR:** ${player.winRate}%`,

                    .setTitle('🌍 Global Chess Leaderboard')
                    .setColor(0x5865F2)
                    .setDescription('*Top 10 players worldwide (minimum 5 games)*')
