                leaderboard.forEach((player, index) => {
                    const medal = index === 0 ? '🥇' : index === 1 ? '🥈' : index === 2 ? '🥉' : `${index + 1}.`;
                    embed.addFields({
                        name: `${medal} <@${player.userId}>`,
                        value: `**ELO:** ${player.elo} | **Games:** ${player.games} | **Servers:** ${player.servers} | **WR:** ${player.winRate}%`,
                        inline: false
                    });
                });

                await interaction.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
            }

            else if (commandName === 'chesselo') {
                const { getPlayerStats, PROVISIONAL_GAMES } = require('./systems/chessElo');
                const userId = options.getUser('user')?.id || interaction.user.id;
                const stats = getPlayerStats(interaction.guild.id, userId);

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle(`♟️ Chess Stats - <@${userId}>`)
                    .setColor(0x00FF00)
                    .setTimestamp();

                // Server stats
                if (stats.server.games < PROVISIONAL_GAMES) {
                    embed.addFields({
                        name: `📊 ${interaction.guild.name} Stats`,
                        value: `**Status:** Provisional (${stats.server.games}/${PROVISIONAL_GAMES} games)\n**Games:** ${stats.server.games} | **W/L/D:** ${stats.server.wins}/${stats.server.losses}/${stats.server.draws}\n*Play ${PROVISIONAL_GAMES - stats.server.games} more games to get rated!*`,
                        inline: false
                    });
                } else {
                    embed.addFields({
                        name: `📊 ${interaction.guild.name} Stats`,
                        value: `**ELO:** ${stats.server.elo}\n**Games:** ${stats.server.games} | **W/L/D:** ${stats.server.wins}/${stats.server.losses}/${stats.server.draws}\n**Win Rate:** ${stats.server.winRate}%`,
                        inline: false
                    });
                }

                // Global stats
                if (stats.global.games < PROVISIONAL_GAMES) {
                    embed.addFields({
                        name: '🌍 Global Stats',
                        value: `**Status:** Provisional (${stats.global.games}/${PROVISIONAL_GAMES} games)\n**Games:** ${stats.global.games} | **Servers:** ${stats.global.servers}\n*Play ${PROVISIONAL_GAMES - stats.global.games} more games to get rated!*`,
                        inline: false
                    });
                } else {
                    embed.addFields({
                        name: '🌍 Global Stats',
                        value: `**ELO:** ${stats.global.elo}\n**Games:** ${stats.global.games} | **Servers:** ${stats.global.servers}\n**Win Rate:** ${stats.global.winRate}%`,
                        inline: false
                    });
                }

                await interaction.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
            }

            // ============ PERMISSION COMMANDS ============
            else if (commandName === 'membercount') {
                // Permission check (optional - remove if everyone should use it)
                if (!interaction.member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.ManageGuild)) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ No permission!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                try {
                    const guild = interaction.guild; // ✅ Fixed: was message.guild

                    // Get member counts
                    const totalMembers = guild.memberCount;
                    const humanMembers = guild.members.cache.filter(member => !member.user.bot).size;
                    const botMembers = guild.members.cache.filter(member => member.user.bot).size;

                    // Get online members (requires presence intent)
                    const onlineMembers = guild.members.cache.filter(
                        member => member.presence?.status === 'online' ||
                            member.presence?.status === 'idle' ||
                            member.presence?.status === 'dnd'
                    ).size;

                    // Get role with most members
                    const topRole = guild.roles.cache
                        .filter(role => role.id !== guild.id) // Exclude @everyone
                        .sort((a, b) => b.members.size - a.members.size)
                        .first();

                    // Create embed
                    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                        .setTitle(`👥 ${guild.name} - Member Statistics`)
                        .setColor(0x5865F2)
                        .setThumbnail(guild.iconURL({ dynamic: true }))
                        .addFields(
                            {
                                name: '📊 Total Members',
                                value: `\`${totalMembers}\``,
                                inline: true
                            },
                            {
                                name: '👤 Humans',
                                value: `\`${humanMembers}\``,
                                inline: true
                            },
                            {
                                name: '🤖 Bots',
                                value: `\`${botMembers}\``,
                                inline: true
                            },
                            {
                                name: '🟢 Online',
                                value: `\`${onlineMembers || 'N/A'}\``,
                                inline: true
                            },
                            {
                                name: '📈 Bot %',
                                value: `\`${((botMembers / totalMembers) * 100).toFixed(1)}%\``,
                                inline: true
                            },
                            {
                                name: '👑 Top Role',
                                value: topRole ? `${topRole} (${topRole.members.size} members)` : 'None',
                                inline: true
                            }
                        )
                        .setFooter({
                            text: `Server created • ${guild.createdAt.toLocaleDateString()}`
                        })
                        .setTimestamp();

                    await interaction.reply({ embeds: [embed] }); // ✅ Fixed: was message.reply

                } catch (error) {
                    console.error('Error in membercount command:', error);
                    await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Failed to fetch member statistics!', ephemeral: true }); // ✅ Fixed
                }
            }

            else if (commandName === 'whitelist-add') {
                if (!interaction.member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.ManageGuild)) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Admin/Owner only!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const role = options.getRole('role');
                const PermissionManager = require('./systems/permissionManager');
                const permManager = new PermissionManager();

                permManager.addWhitelistedRole(interaction.guild.id, role.id);
                await interaction.reply(`✅ ${role} can now use admin commands!`);
            }

            else if (commandName === 'whitelist-remove') {
                if (!interaction.member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.ManageGuild)) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Admin/Owner only!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const role = options.getRole('role');
                const PermissionManager = require('./systems/permissionManager');
                const permManager = new PermissionManager();

                permManager.removeWhitelistedRole(interaction.guild.id, role.id);
                await interaction.reply(`✅ ${role} removed from whitelist!`);
            }

            else if (commandName === 'whitelist-list') {
                const PermissionManager = require('./systems/permissionManager');
                const permManager = new PermissionManager();
                const roles = await permManager.listWhitelistedRoles(interaction.guild);

                if (roles.length === 0) {
                    return await interaction.reply('📝 No whitelisted roles!');
                }

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('🔓 Whitelisted Roles')
                    .setDescription(roles.map(r => `<@&${r.id}> - ${r.name}`).join('\n'))
                    .setColor(0x00FF00);

                await interaction.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
            }

            else if (commandName === 'command-toggle') {
                if (!interaction.member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.ManageGuild)) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Admin/Owner only!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                const command = options.getString('command');
                const commandStates = dataManager.loadFile(interaction.guild.id, 'command_states.json');

                commandStates[command] = !commandStates[command];
                dataManager.saveFile(interaction.guild.id, 'command_states.json', commandStates);

                const status = commandStates[command] ? '✅ Enabled' : '❌ Disabled';
                await interaction.reply(`${status} command: \`${command}\``);
            }

            else if (commandName === 'command-list') {
                const commandStates = dataManager.loadFile(interaction.guild.id, 'command_states.json');

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('📜 Command Status')
                    .setColor(0x5865F2);

                let enabled = [];
                let disabled = [];

                for (const [cmd, state] of Object.entries(commandStates)) {
                    if (state) {
                        enabled.push(cmd);
                    } else {
                        disabled.push(cmd);
                    }
                }

                if (enabled.length > 0) {
                    embed.addFields({ name: '✅ Enabled', value: enabled.join(', '), inline: false });
                }
                if (disabled.length > 0) {
                    embed.addFields({ name: '❌ Disabled', value: disabled.join(', '), inline: false });
                }

                await interaction.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
            }

            // ============ MATH QUIZ COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'math') {
                const mathSystem = require('./systems/mathSystem');
                const difficulty = options.getString('difficulty') || 'easy';

                // Check if difficulty is valid
                if (!['easy', 'hard'].includes(difficulty)) {
                    return await interaction.reply('❌ Difficulty must be "easy" or "hard"!');
                }

                // Initialize problem classes
                const easyProbs = new Easy();
                const hardProbs = new Hard();

                // Get problem generators based on difficulty
                const easyProblems = [
                    () => easyProbs.addition(),
                    () => easyProbs.subtraction(),
                    () => easyProbs.multiplication(),
                    () => easyProbs.division(),
                ];

                const hardProblemsArray = [
                    () => hardProbs.addition(),
                    () => hardProbs.subtraction(),
                    () => hardProbs.multiplication(),
                    () => hardProbs.division(),
                    // TODO: Add more hard problems when implemented
                ];

                const problemArray = difficulty === 'hard' ? hardProblemsArray : easyProblems;

                // Select 5 random problems
                const selectedProblems = [];
                for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
                    const randomIndex = mathSystem.randomInt(0, problemArray.length - 1);
                    selectedProblems.push(problemArray[randomIndex]);
                }

                // Reset active players
                mathSystem.activePlayers.clear();

                // Defer the interaction
                await interaction.deferReply();

                // Wait for join reactions (30 seconds)
                try {
                    await mathSystem.waitForJoinReactions(interaction, 30000);

                    // Check if anyone joined
                    if (mathSystem.activePlayers.size === 0) {
                        return await interaction.followUp('❌ No one joined the quiz. Game cancelled.');
                    }

                    // Announce quiz start
                    const playerList = Array.from(mathSystem.activePlayers.keys())
                        .map(userId => `<@${userId}>`)
                        .join(', ');

                    await interaction.channel.send(
                        `🎮 **Quiz Started!** Players: ${playerList}\n\n**${difficulty.toUpperCase()} Difficulty - 5 Questions**`
                    );

                    // Run the quiz
                    await mathSystem.runQuiz(interaction, selectedProblems);
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error('Error in math quiz:', error);
                    await interaction.followUp(`❌ Error: ${error.message}`);
                }
            }

            // ============ ADMIN COMMANDS ============
            else if (commandName === 'killbot') {
                if (interaction.user.id !== client.config.OWNER_ID) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Owner only!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                await interaction.reply('🛑 Shutting down...');
                process.exit(0);
            }

            else if (commandName === 'restart') {
                if (interaction.user.id !== client.config.OWNER_ID) {
                    return await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Owner only!', ephemeral: true });
                }

                await interaction.reply('🔄 Restarting bot...');
                process.exit(1); // PM2 will restart it
            }

            // ============ CAT/DOG COMMANDS ============
            else if (commandName === 'cat') {
                const funCommands = require('./commands/fun');
                // Call the cat command if it exists
                if (funCommands.cat && funCommands.cat.execute) {
                    await funCommands.cat.execute(interaction, [], client);
                } else {
                    await interaction.reply('🐱 Meow!');
                }
            }

            else if (commandName === 'dog') {
                const funCommands = require('./commands/fun');
                // Call the dog command if it exists
                if (funCommands.dog && funCommands.dog.execute) {
                    await funCommands.dog.execute(interaction, [], client);
                } else {
                    await interaction.reply('🐶 Woof!');
                }
            }
            // ==================== SAY COMMANDS =======
            else if (commandName === 'say') {
                const funCommands = require('./commands/fun');

                if (funCommands.say && funCommands.say.execute) {
                    await funCommands.say.execute(interaction, [], client);
                }
            }
            //======================================================================
            //          SLASH COMMAND HANDLERS - ADD TO slashHandler.js
            //======================================================================

            // ============ ADD THESE HANDLERS AFTER THE EXISTING COMMANDS ============

            // ============ GEMS COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'gems') {
                const gemsCommands = require('./commands/gems');
                await gemsCommands.gems.execute(interaction, client, client.config);
            }

            // ============ GEMSHOP COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'gemshop') {
                const gemsCommands = require('./commands/gems');
                await gemsCommands.gemshop.execute(interaction, client, client.config);
            }

            // ============ XP COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'xp') {
                const xpCommands = require('./commands/xp');
                await xpCommands.xp.execute(interaction, client, client.config);
            }

            // ============ XPTOP COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'xptop') {
                const xpCommands = require('./commands/xp');
                await xpCommands.xptop.execute(interaction, client, client.config);
            }

            // ============ XPGLOBAL COMMAND ============
            else if (commandName === 'xpglobal') {
                const xpCommands = require('./commands/xp');
                await xpCommands.xpglobal.execute(interaction, client, client.config);
            }

            // ==================== INSTRUCTIONS ====================
            // 1. Find the section in your slashHandler.js where commands are handled
            //    (around line 800+ where you have "else if (commandName === 'say')")
            //
            // 2. Add the above handlers AFTER your existing commands
            //
            // 3. Make sure they're inside the try-catch block
            //
            // 4. Make sure they're before the final catch block


        } catch (error) {
            console.error(`❌ Error handling slash command ${commandName}:`, error);

            if (interaction.replied || interaction.deferred) {
                await interaction.followUp({ content: '❌ An error occurred!', ephemeral: true });
            } else {
                await interaction.reply({ content: '❌ An error occurred!', ephemeral: true });
            }
        }
    });

    console.log('✅ Slash command handler loaded');
}

module.exports = { setupSlashCommands };

--- FILE: C:\Users\ADMIN\Documents\Nam\Project\Programmer being\Discord\src\commands\admin.js ---
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//                         ADMIN COMMANDS                             //
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

const { EmbedBuilder } = require('discord.js');
const { isOwner } = require('../utils/helpers');
const crypto = require('crypto');

// Store pending confirmations: userId -> { code, attempts, timestamp }
const pendingKills = new Map();

function setup(client) {

    // ============ EVAL COMMAND ==================
    client.commands.set('eval', {
        name: 'eval',
        description: '⚠️ Execute JavaScript code (OWNER ONLY - DANGEROUS)',
        aliases: ['ev', 'execute'],

        async execute(message, args, client) {
            // CRITICAL: Owner check
            if (!isOwner(message.author.id, client.config.OWNER_ID)) {
                return message.reply('❌ Owner only');
            }

            // Check if code provided
            if (args.length === 0) {
                return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%eval <code>`');
            }

            // Get code
            let code = args.join(' ');

            // Remove code blocks if present
            if (code.startsWith('```js') || code.startsWith('```javascript')) {
                code = code.replace(/```(?:js|javascript)\n?/, '').replace(/```$/, '');
            } else if (code.startsWith('```')) {
                code = code.replace(/```\n?/, '').replace(/```$/, '');
            }

            // Trim whitespace
            code = code.trim();

            // Warning embed
            const warningEmbed = new EmbedBuilder()
                .setTitle('⚠️ Executing Code...')
                .setDescription('```js\n' + code.substring(0, 1900) + '\n```')
                .setColor(0xFFA500)
                .setFooter({ text: '⚠️ Be careful! This can crash the bot!' })
                .setTimestamp();

            await message.reply({ embeds: [warningEmbed] });

            try {
                // Execute code
                const startTime = Date.now();
                let result = eval(code);
                const executionTime = Date.now() - startTime;

                // Handle promises
                if (result instanceof Promise) {
                    result = await result;
                }

                // Convert result to string
                let output = result;

                if (typeof result === 'object') {
                    try {
                        output = JSON.stringify(result, null, 2);
                    } catch (err) {
                        output = String(result);
                    }
                } else {
                    output = String(result);
                }

                // Truncate if too long
                if (output.length > 1900) {
                    output = output.substring(0, 1900) + '...';
                }

                // Success embed
                const successEmbed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('✅ Execution Successful')
                    .setDescription('```js\n' + output + '\n```')
                    .setColor(0x00FF00)
                    .addFields(
                        { name: '⏱️ Execution Time', value: `${executionTime}ms`, inline: true },
                        { name: '📊 Type', value: typeof result, inline: true }
                    )
                    .setTimestamp();

                await message.reply({ embeds: [successEmbed] });

            } catch (error) {
                // Error embed
                const errorEmbed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('❌ Execution Failed')
                    .setDescription('```js\n' + error.toString().substring(0, 1900) + '\n```')
                    .setColor(0xFF0000)
                    .addFields(
                        { name: 'Error Name', value: error.name || 'Unknown', inline: true },
                        { name: 'Error Type', value: typeof error, inline: true }
                    )
                    .setTimestamp();

                await message.reply({ embeds: [errorEmbed] });
            }
        }
    });

    // Aliases
    client.commands.set('ev', client.commands.get('eval'));
    client.commands.set('execute', client.commands.get('eval'));

    // ============ (BOOL) IS ONLINE COMMAND ======
    client.commands.set('isonline', {
        name: 'isonline',
        description: 'check bot status',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            if (!isOwner(message.author.id, client.config.OWNER_ID)) return;
            await message.reply("Yes I'm online my boss");
        }
    });

    // ============ SET NICKNAME COMMAND =======
    client.commands.set('setname', {
        name: 'setname',
        description: 'Set new name for the bot (Owner only)',
        aliases: ['botname'],
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            // Owner check first
            if (!isOwner(message.author.id, client.config.OWNER_ID)) {
                return message.reply('❌ Owner only!');
            }

            // Get new name
            const newName = args.join(' ').trim();

            // Validation checks
            if (!newName) {
                return message.reply('❌ Please provide a new name!\nUsage: `%setname <new name>`');
            }

            if (newName.length < 2) {
                return message.reply('❌ Name must be at least 2 characters long!');
            }

            if (newName.length > 32) {
                return message.reply('❌ Name must be 32 characters or less!');
            }

            // Check for invalid characters (optional - Discord allows most characters)
            if (newName.includes('@') || newName.includes('#')) {
                return message.reply('❌ Name cannot contain @ or # symbols!');
            }

            // Try to change username
            try {
                const oldName = client.user.username;
                await client.user.setUsername(newName);

                return message.reply(
                    `✅ **Bot username updated!**\n` +
                    `Old name: \`${oldName}\`\n` +
                    `New name: \`${newName}\``
                );
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error changing bot username:', error);

                // Handle specific error cases
                if (error.code === 50035) {
                    return message.reply('❌ Invalid username format!');
                } else if (error.message.includes('rate limit')) {
                    return message.reply(
                        `⏰ **Rate Limited!**\n` +
                        `Discord only allows **2 username changes per hour**.\n`
                    );
                } else {
                    console.error(error);
                    return message.reply(
                        `❌ **Failed to change username!**\n`
                    );

                }
            }
        }
    });
    // ============ SERVERS COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('servers', {
        name: 'servers',
        aliases: ['guilds'],
        description: 'List all servers',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            if (!isOwner(message.author.id, client.config.OWNER_ID)) {
                return message.reply('❌ Owner only!');
            }

            const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                .setTitle('📊 Bot Servers')
                .setColor(0x00D4FF)
                .setDescription(`Connected to **${client.guilds.cache.size}** servers`);

            let text = '';
            client.guilds.cache.forEach(guild => {
                text += `**${guild.name}** (${guild.id}) - ${guild.memberCount} members\n`;
            });

            embed.addFields({ name: 'Servers', value: text || 'None' });

            await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
        }
    });

    client.commands.set('guilds', client.commands.get('servers'));

    // ============ SHUTDOWN COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('shutdown', {
        name: 'shutdown',
        aliases: ['stop'],
        description: 'Shutdown the bot',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            if (!isOwner(message.author.id, client.config.OWNER_ID)) {
                return message.reply('❌ Owner only!');
            }

            await message.reply('👋 Shutting down...');

            setTimeout(() => {
                process.exit(0);
            }, 1000);
        }
    });

    client.commands.set('stop', client.commands.get('shutdown'));

    // ============ KILLBOT COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('killbot', {
        name: 'killbot',
        description: 'Shutdown bot with confirmation (Owner only)',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            if (!isOwner(message.author.id, client.config.OWNER_ID)) {
                return message.reply('❌ Owner only!');
            }

            // Generate random 60-character string
            const confirmCode = crypto.randomBytes(30).toString('hex'); // 60 hex chars

            // Store confirmation code
            pendingKills.set(message.author.id, {
                code: confirmCode,
                attempts: 0,
                timestamp: Date.now()
            });

            // Auto-expire after 5 minutes
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (pendingKills.has(message.author.id)) {
                    pendingKills.delete(message.author.id);
                }
            }, 300000);

            // Send DM
            try {
                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('⚠️ Bot Shutdown Confirmation')
                    .setDescription('To confirm bot shutdown, use the command:')
                    .setColor(0xFF0000)
                    .addFields(
                        { name: 'Confirmation Code', value: `\`${confirmCode}\``, inline: false },
                        { name: 'Command', value: `\`%confirm ${confirmCode}\``, inline: false },
                        { name: '⚠️ Warning', value: 'You have **2 attempts** to enter the correct code.\nCode expires in 5 minutes.', inline: false }
                    )
                    .setTimestamp();

                await message.author.send({ embeds: [embed] });
            } catch (error) {
                pendingKills.delete(message.author.id);
                await message.reply('❌ Could not send DM! Make sure your DMs are open.');
            }
        }
    });

    // ============ CONFIRM COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('confirm', {
        name: 'confirm',
        description: 'Confirm bot shutdown',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            if (!isOwner(message.author.id, client.config.OWNER_ID)) {
                return message.reply('❌ Owner only!');
            }

            if (!pendingKills.has(message.author.id)) {
                return message.reply('❌ No pending shutdown confirmation. Use `%killbot` first.');
            }

            if (args.length === 0) {
                return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%confirm <code>`');
            }

            const userCode = args[0];
            const pending = pendingKills.get(message.author.id);

            // Check if code matches
            if (userCode === pending.code) {
                pendingKills.delete(message.author.id);

                await message.reply('✅ Confirmation successful! Shutting down bot in 3 seconds...');

                setTimeout(() => {
                    console.log('🛑 Bot shutdown by owner');
                    process.exit(0);
                }, 3000);
            } else {
                pending.attempts++;

                if (pending.attempts >= 2) {
                    pendingKills.delete(message.author.id);
                    return message.reply('❌ Incorrect code! Maximum attempts reached. Confirmation cancelled.');
                } else {
                    const attemptsLeft = 2 - pending.attempts;
                    return message.reply(`❌ Incorrect code! You have **${attemptsLeft}** attempt(s) left.`);
                }
            }
        }
    });

    // ============ %SIZECHECK COMMAND ============
    const { execSync } = require('child_process');
    const fs = require('fs');
    const path = require('path');

    client.commands.set('sizecheck', {
        name: 'sizecheck',
        description: '📊 Check project size (Owner only)',
        aliases: ['size', 'disksize'],

        async execute(message, args, client) {
            if (!isOwner(message.author.id, client.config.OWNER_ID)) {
                return message.reply('❌ Owner only! ');
            }

            const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                .setTitle('📊 Analyzing Project Size...')
                .setDescription('Scanning folders...')
                .setColor(0x3498DB);

            const msg = await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });

            try {
                const folders = fs.readdirSync('.', { withFileTypes: true })
                    .filter(dirent => dirent.isDirectory())
                    .map(dirent => dirent.name);

                let folderSizes = [];

                for (const folder of folders) {
                    const size = getFolderSize(folder);
                    folderSizes.push({
                        name: folder,
                        size: size,
                        sizeMB: (size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)
                    });
                }

                folderSizes.sort((a, b) => b.size - a.size);

                const totalSize = folderSizes.reduce((sum, f) => sum + f.size, 0);
                const totalMB = (totalSize / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2);

                const resultEmbed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('📊 Project Size Analysis')
                    .setColor(0x2ECC71)
                    .setTimestamp();

                let folderList = '';
                for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(10, folderSizes.length); i++) {
                    const folder = folderSizes[i];
                    const emoji = folder.sizeMB > 100 ? '🔴' : folder.sizeMB > 10 ? '🟡' : '🟢';
                    folderList += `${emoji} **${folder.name}**: ${folder.sizeMB} MB\n`;
                }

                resultEmbed.addFields(
                    { name: '📁 Folder Sizes', value: folderList || 'No folders', inline: false },
                    { name: '💾 Total Size', value: `**${totalMB} MB**`, inline: true },
                    { name: '📈 Folders', value: `${folderSizes.length}`, inline: true }
                );

                await msg.edit({ embeds: [resultEmbed] });

            } catch (error) {
                await msg.edit({ content: `❌ Error: ${error.message}` });
            }
        }
    });

    // ============ %CLEANUP COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('cleanup', {
        name: 'cleanup',
        description: '🧹 Clean NPM cache (Owner only)',
        aliases: ['clean', 'cleancache'],

        async execute(message, args, client) {
            if (!isOwner(message.author.id, client.config.OWNER_ID)) {
                return message.reply('❌ Owner only!');
            }

            const msg = await message.reply('🧹 Cleaning...');

            try {
                let results = [];

                // Clean NPM cache
                try {
                    execSync('npm cache clean --force', { encoding: 'utf-8' });
                    results.push('✅ NPM cache cleaned');
                } catch (err) {
                    results.push('❌ NPM cache clean failed');
                }

                // Delete cache folders
                const foldersToDelete = ['.npm', '.cache', '.node-gyp'];
                let deleted = [];

                for (const folder of foldersToDelete) {
                    if (fs.existsSync(folder)) {
                        try {
                            fs.rmSync(folder, { recursive: true, force: true });
                            deleted.push(folder);
                        } catch (err) {
                            // Ignore errors
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (deleted.length > 0) {
                    results.push(`✅ Deleted: ${deleted.join(', ')}`);
                }

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('🧹 Cleanup Complete!')
                    .setDescription(results.join('\n'))
                    .setColor(0x2ECC71)
                    .setFooter({ text: ' Owner Command' })
                    .setTimestamp();

                await msg.edit({ embeds: [embed] });

            } catch (error) {
                await msg.edit({ content: `❌ Error: ${error.message}` });
            }
        }
    });

    // Helper function for folder size
    function getFolderSize(folderPath) {
        let totalSize = 0;

        function calculateSize(dirPath) {
            try {
                const files = fs.readdirSync(dirPath);
                for (const file of files) {
                    const filePath = path.join(dirPath, file);
                    try {
                        const stats = fs.statSync(filePath);
                        if (stats.isDirectory()) {
                            calculateSize(filePath); // ✅ Now includes ALL folders
                        } else {
                            totalSize += stats.size;
                        }
                    } catch (err) { }
                }
            } catch (err) { }
        }

        calculateSize(folderPath);
        return totalSize;
    }


    console.log('✅ Admin commands loaded');
}

module.exports = { setup };

//======================================================================
//                      AI COMMANDS
//======================================================================

const { SlashCommandBuilder } = require('discord.js');
const { generateResponse, getPrompts } = require('../systems/aiSystem');
const DataManager = require('../utils/dataManager');

const dataManager = new DataManager();

// Cooldowns
const cooldowns = new Map();

function checkCooldown(userId, commandName, seconds) {
  const key = `${userId}-${commandName}`;
  const now = Date.now();

  if (cooldowns.has(key)) {
    const expirationTime = cooldowns.get(key) + seconds * 1000;

    if (now < expirationTime) {
      const timeLeft = (expirationTime - now) / 1000;
      return { onCooldown: true, timeLeft };
    }
  }

  cooldowns.set(key, now);
  setTimeout(() => cooldowns.delete(key), seconds * 1000);

  return { onCooldown: false };
}

// Helper function to send long messages as multiple messages
async function sendLongMessage(target, text) {
  const MAX_LENGTH = 2000;

  if (text.length <= MAX_LENGTH) {
    if (target.editReply) {
      await target.editReply(text);
    } else {
      await target.reply(text);
    }
    return;
  }

  // Split text into chunks of 2000 characters
  const chunks = [];
  let currentChunk = '';

  const lines = text.split('\n');
  for (const line of lines) {
    if ((currentChunk + line + '\n').length > MAX_LENGTH) {
      if (currentChunk) chunks.push(currentChunk);
      currentChunk = line + '\n';
    } else {
      currentChunk += line + '\n';
    }
  }
  if (currentChunk) chunks.push(currentChunk);

  // Send first message using editReply (if available) or reply
  if (target.editReply) {
    await target.editReply(chunks[0]);
    // Send remaining as follow-ups for interactions
    for (let i = 1; i < chunks.length; i++) {
      await target.followUp(chunks[i]);
    }
  } else {
    // For regular messages, just reply multiple times
    for (const chunk of chunks) {
      await target.reply(chunk);
    }
  }
}

// ============ MINECRAFT AI ============
const minecraft = {
  name: 'minecraft',
  category: 'AI',
  description: 'Minecraft expert AI',
  aliases: ['mc'],

  data: new SlashCommandBuilder()
    .setName('minecraft')
    .setDescription('Ask a Minecraft expert AI')
    .addStringOption(opt => opt
      .setName('question')
      .setDescription('Your Minecraft question')
      .setRequired(true)
    ),

  async execute(interaction, client, config) {
    // Check if this is a valid interaction with options
    if (!interaction || !interaction.options || !interaction.options.getString) {
      console.error('minecraft: Invalid interaction object');
      return;
    }
    const question = interaction.options.getString('question');

    const serverConfig = dataManager.loadFile(interaction.guild.id, 'server_config.json');

    if (serverConfig.features && serverConfig.features.minecraft_enabled === false) {
      return interaction.reply({ content: '🔒 The Minecraft command is currently locked!', ephemeral: true });
    }

    const cooldown = checkCooldown(interaction.user.id, 'minecraft', 15);
    if (cooldown.onCooldown) {
      return interaction.reply({
        content: `⏰ Cooldown! Wait ${Math.ceil(cooldown.timeLeft)} seconds.`,
        ephemeral: true
      });
    }

    await interaction.deferReply();

    try {
      const prompts = getPrompts();
      const systemInstruction = prompts.minecraft;

      const answer = await generateResponse(question, systemInstruction);

      await sendLongMessage(interaction, answer);
    } catch (error) {
      await interaction.editReply(`❌ **Error:** ${error.message}`);
    }
  },

  async run(message, args, client, config) {
    if (args.length === 0) {
      return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%minecraft <question>`');
    }

    const question = args.join(' ');

    const serverConfig = dataManager.loadFile(message.guild.id, 'server_config.json');

    if (serverConfig.features && serverConfig.features.minecraft_enabled === false) {
      return message.reply('🔒 The Minecraft command is currently locked!');
    }

    const cooldown = checkCooldown(message.author.id, 'minecraft', 15);
    if (cooldown.onCooldown) {
      return message.reply(`⏰ Cooldown! Wait ${Math.ceil(cooldown.timeLeft)} seconds.`);
    }

    const typingInterval = setInterval(() => {
      message.channel.sendTyping();
    }, 5000);

    await message.channel.sendTyping();

    try {
      const prompts = getPrompts();
      const systemInstruction = prompts.minecraft;

      const answer = await generateResponse(question, systemInstruction);

      clearInterval(typingInterval);
      await sendLongMessage(message, answer);
    } catch (error) {
      clearInterval(typingInterval);
      await message.reply(`❌ **Error:** ${error.message}`);
    }
  }
};

// ============ LOCK/UNLOCK MINECRAFT ============
const minecraftlock = {
  name: 'minecraftlock',
  category: 'Admin',
  description: 'Lock minecraft command',

  async execute(message, args, client) {
    // Get config from client
    const config = client.config || require('../config');
    if (message.author.id !== config.OWNER_ID && !message.member.permissions.has('ManageGuild')) {
      return message.reply('❌ You need to be server owner or have Manage Server permission!');
    }

    const serverConfig = dataManager.loadFile(message.guild.id, 'server_config.json');

    if (!serverConfig.features) serverConfig.features = {};
    serverConfig.features.minecraft_enabled = false;

    dataManager.saveFile(message.guild.id, 'server_config.json', serverConfig);

    await message.reply('🔒 **Minecraft command has been LOCKED**');
  }
};

const minecraftunlock = {
  name: 'minecraftunlock',
  category: 'Admin',
  description: 'Unlock minecraft command',

  async execute(message, args, client) {
    // Get config from client
    const config = client.config || require('../config');
    if (message.author.id !== config.OWNER_ID && !message.member.permissions.has('ManageGuild')) {
      return message.reply('❌ You need to be server owner or have Manage Server permission!');
    }

    const serverConfig = dataManager.loadFile(message.guild.id, 'server_config.json');

    if (!serverConfig.features) serverConfig.features = {};
    serverConfig.features.minecraft_enabled = true;

    dataManager.saveFile(message.guild.id, 'server_config.json', serverConfig);

    await message.reply('🔓 **Minecraft command has been UNLOCKED**');
  }
};

  // Minecraft commands
  client.commands.set('minecraft', minecraft);
  client.commands.set('mc', minecraft);
  client.commands.set('minecraftlock', minecraftlock);
  client.commands.set('minecraftunlock', minecraftunlock);

  // Math Helper
  client.commands.set('mathhelper', mathhelper);
  client.commands.set('mathhelp', mathhelper);
  client.commands.set('mh', mathhelper);

  // Physics Helper
  client.commands.set('physicshelper', physicshelper);
  client.commands.set('physicshelp', physicshelper);
  client.commands.set('ph', physicshelper);

  // Chemistry Helper
  client.commands.set('chemistryhelper', chemistryhelper);
  client.commands.set('chemhelp', chemistryhelper);
  client.commands.set('ch', chemistryhelper);

  // Coding Helper
  client.commands.set('codinghelper', codinghelper);
  client.commands.set('codehelp', codinghelper);

  console.log('✅ AI commands registered');
}

///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//                    CALCULATOR COMMAND                             //
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

const math = require('mathjs');
const { EmbedBuilder } = require('discord.js');

function setup(client) {
    client.commands.set('calculator', {
        name: 'calculator',
        aliases: ['calc', 'math', 'calculate'],
        description: 'Advanced calculator with error handling',

        async execute(message, args, client) {
            // ============ VALIDATION ============

            // Check if equation provided
            if (!args.length) {
                return message.reply({
                    content: '❌ **Usage:** `%calc <expression>`\n' +
                        '**Examples:**\n' +
                        '• `%calc 2 + 2`\n' +
                        '• `%calc sqrt(144)`\n' +
                        '• `%calc sin(pi/2)`\n' +
                        '• `%calc (5 + 3) * 2`'
                });
            }

            // Get full equation
            let equation = args.join(' ');

            // Check length limit
            if (equation.length > 200) {
                return message.reply('❌ **Error:** Equation too long! (Max 200 characters)');
            }

            // ============ PRE-PROCESSING ============

            try {
                // Clean and normalize equation
                const cleanEquation = equation
                    .replace(/–/g, '-')           // Em dash to minus
                    .replace(/×/g, '*')           // Multiplication symbol
                    .replace(/÷/g, '/')           // Division symbol
                    .replace(/x/gi, '*')          // 'x' as multiplication
                    .replace(/:/g, '/')           // Colon as division
                    .replace(/(\d+)n/g, '$1')     // Remove BigInt suffix
                    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')         // Normalize spaces
                    .trim();

                // ============ EVALUATION ============

                let result = math.evaluate(cleanEquation);

                // ============ POST-PROCESSING ============

                // Handle numeric results
                if (typeof result === 'number') {
                    // Check for division by zero or invalid operations
                    if (!isFinite(result)) {
                        return message.reply('❌ **Math Error:** Division by zero or invalid operation!');
                    }

                    // Format result (max 10 decimal places)
                    result = math.format(result, { precision: 14 });

                    // Remove trailing zeros
                    if (result.includes('.')) {
                        result = parseFloat(result).toString();
                    }
                }

                // ============ SUCCESS RESPONSE ============

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('🔢 Calculator')
                    .setColor(0x00D4FF)
                    .addFields(
                        {
                            name: '📝 Input',
                            value: `\`\`\`${equation}\`\`\``,
                            inline: false
                        },
                        {
                            name: '✅ Result',
                            value: `\`\`\`${result.toString()}\`\`\``,
                            inline: false
                        }
                    )
                    .setTimestamp();

                return message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });

            } catch (error) {
                // ============ ERROR HANDLING ============

                const errMsg = error.message.toLowerCase();
                let errorMessage = '❌ **Syntax Error:** Invalid expression!';

                // Specific error messages
                if (errMsg.includes('unexpected end')) {
                    errorMessage = '❌ **Syntax Error:** Equation is incomplete!';
                }
                else if (errMsg.includes('undefined symbol') || errMsg.includes('undefined variable')) {
                    errorMessage = '❌ **Syntax Error:** Unknown symbol or variable!';
                }
                else if (errMsg.includes('units do not match')) {
                    errorMessage = '❌ **Math Error:** Incompatible units!';
                }
                else if (errMsg.includes('parenthesis')) {
                    errorMessage = '❌ **Syntax Error:** Mismatched parentheses!';
                }
                else if (errMsg.includes('too many arguments')) {
                    errorMessage = '❌ **Syntax Error:** Too many arguments for function!';
                }
                else if (errMsg.includes('too few arguments')) {
                    errorMessage = '❌ **Syntax Error:** Not enough arguments for function!';
                }
                else if (errMsg.includes('dimension')) {
                    errorMessage = '❌ **Math Error:** Dimension mismatch!';
                }

                // Send error embed
                const errorEmbed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('❌ Calculator Error')
                    .setColor(0xFF0000)
                    .addFields(
                        {
                            name: '📝 Your Input',
                            value: `\`\`\`${equation}\`\`\``,
                            inline: false
                        },
                        {
                            name: '⚠️ Error',
                            value: errorMessage,
                            inline: false
                        }
                    )
                    .setFooter({ text: 'Tip: Check your syntax and try again!' })
                    .setTimestamp();

                return message.reply({ embeds: [errorEmbed] });
            }
        }
    });

    // Set aliases
    client.commands.set('calc', client.commands.get('calculator'));
    client.commands.set('calculate', client.commands.get('calculator'));
}

module.exports = { setup };

//======================================================================
//                         CHESS COMMANDS
//======================================================================

const { EmbedBuilder, ActionRowBuilder, ButtonBuilder, ButtonStyle } = require('discord.js');
const {
    createPendingGame,
    confirmGame,
    pendingGames,
    tryMove,
    resign,
    offerDraw,
    acceptDraw,
    getGameByPlayer,
    formatTime
} = require('../systems/chessManager');
const { renderBoard } = require('../systems/boardRenderer');
const {
    updateElo,
    getServerLeaderboard,
    getGlobalLeaderboard,
    getPlayerStats,
    PROVISIONAL_GAMES
} = require('../systems/chessElo');

function setup(client) {
    // ============ CHESS START COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('chess', {
        name: 'chess',
        aliases: ['startchess'],
        description: 'Start a chess game',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            // Check if confirming a game
            if (args[0] === 'confirm') {
                const p1 = message.mentions.users.first();
                if (!p1) {
                    return message.reply('❌ Please mention the player who challenged you!');
                }

                const game = confirmGame(message.author.id, p1.id, message.channel.id, message.guild.id);
                if (!game) {
                    return message.reply('❌ No pending chess game from that player!');
                }

                // Start automatic timeout checker
                const { startTimeoutChecker } = require('../systems/chessManager');
                startTimeoutChecker(client, game);

                const boardImage = await renderBoard(game.chess.fen(), null, false);

                // Determine color assignment method
                let assignmentNote = '';
                if (game.colorMethod === 'streak_balance') {
                    assignmentNote = '\n*Colors balanced due to recent streak*';
                } else if (game.colorMethod === 'total_balance') {
                    assignmentNote = '\n*Colors balanced based on game history*';
                } else {
                    assignmentNote = '\n*Colors assigned randomly*';
                }

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('♟️ Chess Game Started!')
                    .setColor(0x000000)
                    .addFields(
                        { name: '⚪ White', value: `<@${game.players.white}>${assignmentNote}`, inline: true },
                        { name: '⚫ Black', value: `<@${game.players.black}>`, inline: true },
                        { name: '⏱️ Time', value: `${formatTime(game.clocks.white)}`, inline: true }
                    )
                    .setImage('attachment://board.png')
                    .setFooter({ text: 'White to move! Use chess notation (e.g., e2e4, e4, Nf3)' });

                const buttons = new ActionRowBuilder()
                    .addComponents(
                        new ButtonBuilder()
                            .setCustomId('chess_resign')
                            .setLabel('Resign')
                            .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Danger),
                        new ButtonBuilder()
                            .setCustomId('chess_draw')
                            .setLabel('Offer Draw')
                            .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary)
                    );

                return message.channel.send({
                    embeds: [embed],
                    components: [buttons],
                    files: [{ attachment: boardImage, name: 'board.png' }]
                });
            }

            // Start new game
            const p2 = message.mentions.users.first();
            if (!p2) {
                return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%chess @opponent <minutes>` or `%chess confirm @challenger`');
            }

            if (p2.id === message.author.id) {
                return message.reply('❌ You cannot play against yourself!');
            }

            if (p2.bot) {
                return message.reply('❌ You cannot play against bots!');
            }

            const minutes = parseInt(args[1]) || 10;
            if (minutes < 1 || minutes > 60) {
                return message.reply('❌ Time must be between 1-60 minutes!');
            }

            const durationMs = minutes * 60000;
            createPendingGame(message.author.id, p2.id, durationMs);

            return message.reply(`♟️ Chess challenge sent to ${p2}!\nThey should type: \`%chess confirm @${message.author.username}\``);
        }
    });

    client.commands.set('startchess', client.commands.get('chess'));

    // ============ CHESS BOARD COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('board', {
        name: 'board',
        description: 'Show current chess board',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const game = getGameByPlayer(message.author.id);
            if (!game) {
                return message.reply('❌ You are not in an active chess game!');
            }

            // Determine if board should be flipped based on whose turn it is
            const flipped = game.chess.turn() === 'b';
            const boardImage = await renderBoard(game.chess.fen(), game.lastMove, flipped);
            const turn = game.chess.turn() === 'w' ? 'White' : 'Black';
            const currentPlayer = game.chess.turn() === 'w' ? game.players.white : game.players.black;

            const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                .setTitle('♟️ Current Board')
                .setColor(game.chess.turn() === 'w' ? 0xFFFFFF : 0x000000)
                .addFields(
                    { name: '⚪ White Time', value: formatTime(game.clocks.white), inline: true },
                    { name: '⚫ Black Time', value: formatTime(game.clocks.black), inline: true },
                    { name: '🎯 Turn', value: `${turn} <@${currentPlayer}>`, inline: true }
                )
                .setImage('attachment://board.png');

            const buttons = new ActionRowBuilder()
                .addComponents(
                    new ButtonBuilder()
                        .setCustomId('chess_resign')
                        .setLabel('Resign')
                        .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Danger),
                    new ButtonBuilder()
                        .setCustomId('chess_draw')
                        .setLabel('Offer Draw')
                        .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary)
                );

            return message.channel.send({
                embeds: [embed],
                components: [buttons],
                files: [{ attachment: boardImage, name: 'board.png' }]
            });
        }
    });

    // ============ CHESS ELO/STATS COMMANDS ============
    client.commands.set('chesstotal', {
        name: 'chesstotal',
        description: 'Show server chess leaderboard',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const leaderboard = getServerLeaderboard(message.guild.id, 10);

            if (leaderboard.length === 0) {
                return message.reply('📊 No rated players yet! Play 5 games to get rated.');
            }

            const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                .setTitle(`♟️ ${message.guild.name} - Chess Leaderboard`)
                .setColor(0xFFD700)
                .setDescription('*Top 10 players (minimum 5 games)*')
                .setTimestamp();

            leaderboard.forEach((player, index) => {
                const medal = index === 0 ? '🥇' : index === 1 ? '🥈' : index === 2 ? '🥉' : `${index + 1}.`;
                embed.addFields({
                    name: `${medal} <@${player.userId}>`,
                    value: `**ELO:** ${player.elo} | **W/L/D:** ${player.wins}/${player.losses}/${player.draws} | **WR:** ${player.winRate}%`,
                    inline: false
                });
            });

            return message.channel.send({ embeds: [embed] });
        }
    });

    client.commands.set('chessglobal', {
        name: 'chessglobal',
        description: 'Show global chess leaderboard',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const leaderboard = getGlobalLeaderboard(10);

            if (leaderboard.length === 0) {
                return message.reply('📊 No rated players yet! Play 5 games to get rated.');
            }

            const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                .setTitle('🌍 Global Chess Leaderboard')
                .setColor(0x5865F2)
                .setDescription('*Top 10 players worldwide (minimum 5 games)*')
                .setTimestamp();

            leaderboard.forEach((player, index) => {
                const medal = index === 0 ? '🥇' : index === 1 ? '🥈' : index === 2 ? '🥉' : `${index + 1}.`;
                embed.addFields({
                    name: `${medal} <@${player.userId}>`,
                    value: `**ELO:** ${player.elo} | **Games:** ${player.games} | **Servers:** ${player.servers} | **WR:** ${player.winRate}%`,
                    inline: false
                });
            });

            return message.channel.send({ embeds: [embed] });
        }
    });

    client.commands.set('chesselo', {
        name: 'chesselo',
        aliases: ['chessstats', 'chessrating'],
        description: 'Show your chess stats and ELO',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const userId = message.mentions.users.first()?.id || message.author.id;
            const stats = getPlayerStats(message.guild.id, userId);

            const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                .setTitle(`♟️ Chess Stats - <@${userId}>`)
                .setColor(0x00FF00)
                .setTimestamp();

            // Server stats
            if (stats.server.games < PROVISIONAL_GAMES) {
                embed.addFields({

                    value: `**Status:** Provisional (${stats.server.games}/${PROVISIONAL_GAMES} games)\n**Games:** ${stats.server.games} | **W/L/D:** ${stats.server.wins}/${stats.server.losses}/${stats.server.draws}\n*Play ${PROVISIONAL_GAMES - stats.server.games} more games to get rated!*`,
                    inline: false
                });
            } else {
                embed.addFields({
                    name: `📊 ${message.guild.name} Stats`,
                    value: `**ELO:** ${stats.server.elo}\n**Games:** ${stats.server.games} | **W/L/D:** ${stats.server.wins}/${stats.server.losses}/${stats.server.draws}\n**Win Rate:** ${stats.server.winRate}%`,

// Handle button interactions
async function handleInteraction(interaction, client) {
    if (!interaction.isButton()) return;

    const userId = interaction.user.id;
    const guildId = interaction.guild.id;

    if (interaction.customId === 'chess_resign') {
        const result = resign(userId, guildId);
        if (!result) {
            return interaction.reply({
                content: '❌ You are not in this game!',
                ephemeral: true
            });
        }

        // Update ELO
        const eloUpdate = updateElo(guildId, result.winnerId, result.loserId, false);

        const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
            .setTitle('🏳️ Game Over - Resignation')
            .setDescription(`<@${userId}> resigned!`)
            .setColor(0xFF0000)
            .addFields(
                { name: '🏆 Winner', value: `<@${result.winnerId}>`, inline: false },
                { name: '📈 ELO Changes', value: `<@${result.winnerId}>: **${eloUpdate.winner.oldElo}** → **${eloUpdate.winner.newElo}** (${eloUpdate.winner.change >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${eloUpdate.winner.change})\n<@${result.loserId}>: **${eloUpdate.loser.oldElo}** → **${eloUpdate.loser.newElo}** (${eloUpdate.loser.change >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${eloUpdate.loser.change})`, inline: false }
            );

        if (eloUpdate.winner.games < PROVISIONAL_GAMES || eloUpdate.loser.games < PROVISIONAL_GAMES) {
            embed.setFooter({ text: `Ratings show after ${PROVISIONAL_GAMES} games` });
        }

        return interaction.channel.send({ embeds: [embed] });
    }

    if (interaction.customId === 'chess_draw') {
        const offered = offerDraw(userId);
        if (!offered) {
            return interaction.reply({
                content: '❌ You are not in this game!',
                ephemeral: true
            });
        }

        const game = getGameByPlayer(userId);
        const opponentId = game.players.white === userId ? game.players.black : game.players.white;

        const buttons = new ActionRowBuilder()
            .addComponents(
                new ButtonBuilder()
                    .setCustomId('chess_draw_accept')
                    .setLabel('Accept Draw')
                    .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Success),
                new ButtonBuilder()
                    .setCustomId('chess_resign')
                    .setLabel('Resign')
                    .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Danger)
            );

        return interaction.reply({
            content: `🤝 <@${userId}> offered a draw to <@${opponentId}>!`,
            components: [buttons]
        });
    }

    if (interaction.customId === 'chess_draw_accept') {
        const result = acceptDraw(userId, guildId);
        if (!result) {
            return interaction.reply({
                content: '❌ No draw offer available!',
                ephemeral: true
            });
        }

        // Update ELO for draw
        const eloUpdate = updateElo(guildId, result.player1, result.player2, true);

        const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
            .setTitle('🤝 Game Over - Draw')
            .setDescription(result.drawReason)
            .setColor(0x808080)
            .addFields({
                name: '📊 ELO Changes',
                value: `<@${result.player1}>: **${eloUpdate.winner.oldElo}** → **${eloUpdate.winner.newElo}** (${eloUpdate.winner.change >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${eloUpdate.winner.change})\n<@${result.player2}>: **${eloUpdate.loser.oldElo}** → **${eloUpdate.loser.newElo}** (${eloUpdate.loser.change >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${eloUpdate.loser.change})`,
                inline: false
            });

        if (eloUpdate.winner.games < PROVISIONAL_GAMES || eloUpdate.loser.games < PROVISIONAL_GAMES) {
            embed.setFooter({ text: `Ratings show after ${PROVISIONAL_GAMES} games` });
        }

        return interaction.channel.send({ embeds: [embed] });
    }
}

// Handle chess moves in messages
async function handleChessMove(message, client) {
    if (message.author.bot) return;

    // First check if user is in a game
    const game = getGameByPlayer(message.author.id);
    if (!game) return; // Not in a game, ignore message

    // Check if message is in the game channel
    if (message.channel.id !== game.channelId) {
        return; // Wrong channel, ignore
    }

    const content = message.content.trim();
    const guildId = message.guild.id;

    // Try to make the move (chess.js handles case-insensitivity)
    const moveResult = tryMove(message.author.id, content);

    // If move failed and user is in a game, tell them it's illegal
    if (!moveResult) {
        return message.reply('❌ Illegal move! Please use valid chess notation (e.g., e2e4, Nf3, Ke2)');
    }

    if (moveResult.timeout) {
        // Update ELO for timeout
        const eloUpdate = updateElo(guildId, moveResult.winnerId, moveResult.loserId, false);

        const winnerColor = moveResult.timeout.winner === 'white' ? '⚪ White' : '⚫ Black';
        const loserColor = moveResult.timeout.loser === 'white' ? '⚪ White' : '⚫ Black';

        const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
            .setTitle('⏱️ Game Over - Timeout')
            .setDescription(`${winnerColor} wins by timeout!`)
            .setColor(0xFF0000)
            .addFields(
                { name: '🏆 Winner', value: `<@${moveResult.winnerId}>`, inline: true },
                { name: '⏱️ Time Out', value: `<@${moveResult.loserId}>`, inline: true },
                { name: '\u200b', value: '\u200b', inline: true },
                {
                    name: '📈 ELO Changes',
                    value: `<@${moveResult.winnerId}>: **${eloUpdate.winner.oldElo}** → **${eloUpdate.winner.newElo}** (${eloUpdate.winner.change >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${eloUpdate.winner.change})\n<@${moveResult.loserId}>: **${eloUpdate.loser.oldElo}** → **${eloUpdate.loser.newElo}** (${eloUpdate.loser.change >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${eloUpdate.loser.change})`,
                    inline: false
                }
            );

        if (eloUpdate.winner.games < PROVISIONAL_GAMES || eloUpdate.loser.games < PROVISIONAL_GAMES) {
            embed.setFooter({ text: `Ratings show after ${PROVISIONAL_GAMES} games` });
        }

        return message.channel.send({ embeds: [embed] });
    }

    // Flip board after move (show from perspective of player to move next)
    const flipped = moveResult.move.color === 'w'; // If white just moved, flip for black
    const boardImage = await renderBoard(moveResult.fen, moveResult.move, flipped);

    const turn = moveResult.gameOver ? 'Game Over' : (moveResult.move.color === 'w' ? 'Black' : 'White') + ' to move';

    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
        .setTitle(`♟️ Chess - ${moveResult.move.san}`)
        .setColor(moveResult.check ? 0xFF0000 : 0x00FF00)
        .addFields(
            { name: '⚪ White Time', value: moveResult.clocks.white, inline: true },
            { name: '⚫ Black Time', value: moveResult.clocks.black, inline: true },
            { name: '🎯 Status', value: turn, inline: true }
        )
        .setImage('attachment://board.png');

    if (moveResult.check && !moveResult.checkmate) {
        embed.setFooter({ text: '⚠️ CHECK!' });
    }

    // Handle checkmate
    if (moveResult.checkmate) {
        // Update ELO
        const eloUpdate = updateElo(guildId, moveResult.winnerId, moveResult.loserId, false);

        embed.setTitle('🏆 CHECKMATE!');
        embed.setDescription(`${moveResult.move.color === 'w' ? '⚪ White' : '⚫ Black'} wins!`);
        embed.setColor(0xFFD700);
        embed.addFields({
            name: '📈 ELO Changes',
            value: `<@${moveResult.winnerId}>: **${eloUpdate.winner.oldElo}** → **${eloUpdate.winner.newElo}** (${eloUpdate.winner.change >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${eloUpdate.winner.change})\n<@${moveResult.loserId}>: **${eloUpdate.loser.oldElo}** → **${eloUpdate.loser.newElo}** (${eloUpdate.loser.change >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${eloUpdate.loser.change})`,
            inline: false
        });

        if (eloUpdate.winner.games < PROVISIONAL_GAMES || eloUpdate.loser.games < PROVISIONAL_GAMES) {
            embed.setFooter({ text: `Ratings show after ${PROVISIONAL_GAMES} games` });
        } else {
            embed.setFooter({ text: `${moveResult.move.color === 'w' ? 'White' : 'Black'} delivers checkmate!` });
        }
    }

    // Handle draws
    if (moveResult.draw) {
        // Update ELO for draw
        const eloUpdate = updateElo(guildId, moveResult.whiteId, moveResult.blackId, true);

        embed.setTitle('🤝 DRAW!');
        embed.setDescription(moveResult.drawReason);
        embed.setColor(0x808080);
        embed.addFields({
            name: '📊 ELO Changes',
            value: `<@${moveResult.whiteId}>: **${eloUpdate.winner.oldElo}** → **${eloUpdate.winner.newElo}** (${eloUpdate.winner.change >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${eloUpdate.winner.change})\n<@${moveResult.blackId}>: **${eloUpdate.loser.oldElo}** → **${eloUpdate.loser.newElo}** (${eloUpdate.loser.change >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${eloUpdate.loser.change})`,
            inline: false
        });

        // Add specific draw type info
        if (moveResult.drawType === 'stalemate') {
            embed.setFooter({ text: 'The king has no legal moves but is not in check.' });
        } else if (moveResult.drawType === 'threefold') {
            embed.setFooter({ text: 'The same position occurred three times.' });
        } else if (moveResult.drawType === 'insufficient') {
            embed.setFooter({ text: 'Neither player has enough material to checkmate.' });
        } else if (moveResult.drawType === 'fifty') {
            embed.setFooter({ text: '50 moves without captures or pawn moves.' });
        }

        if (eloUpdate.winner.games < PROVISIONAL_GAMES || eloUpdate.loser.games < PROVISIONAL_GAMES) {
            embed.setFooter({ text: `Ratings show after ${PROVISIONAL_GAMES} games` });
        }
    }

    // Check for potential draw claims
    if (!moveResult.gameOver && !moveResult.draw) {
        if (moveResult.threefoldRepetition) {
            embed.addFields({ name: '🔄 Notice', value: 'Threefold repetition - players can claim a draw!', inline: false });
        }
        if (moveResult.insufficientMaterial) {
            embed.addFields({ name: '🎭 Notice', value: 'Insufficient material - draw is automatic!', inline: false });
        }
    }

    const buttons = new ActionRowBuilder()
        .addComponents(
            new ButtonBuilder()
                .setCustomId('chess_resign')
                .setLabel('Resign')
                .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Danger),
            new ButtonBuilder()
                .setCustomId('chess_draw')
                .setLabel('Offer Draw')
                .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary)
        );

    return message.channel.send({
        embeds: [embed],
        components: moveResult.gameOver ? [] : [buttons],
        files: [{ attachment: boardImage, name: 'board.png' }]
    });
}

module.exports = { setup, handleInteraction, handleChessMove };

///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//                         CODE COMMAND                               //
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

const CodeValidator = require('../utils/codeValidator');
const CodeExecutor = require('../systems/codeExecutor');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const { EmbedBuilder } = require('discord.js');

// Cooldowns per user
const cooldowns = new Map();
const COOLDOWN_TIME = 30 * 1000; // 30 seconds

function setup(client) {
    client.commands.set('code', {
        name: 'code',
        description: 'Execute code (JavaScript)',
        async run(message, args, client, config) {
            // Check cooldown
            const userId = message.author.id;
            const now = Date.now();

            if (cooldowns.has(userId)) {
                const expirationTime = cooldowns.get(userId) + COOLDOWN_TIME;
                if (now < expirationTime) {
                    const remainingTime = ((expirationTime - now) / 1000).toFixed(1);
                    return message.reply(`⏱️ Command on cooldown. Try again in **${remainingTime}s**`);
                }
            }

            // Set cooldown
            cooldowns.set(userId, now);

            // Parse mode from message (handles newlines)
            const modeMatch = message.content.match(/^%code\s+([123])\s*(?:\n|$)/);
            const mode = modeMatch ? modeMatch[1] : null;

            if (!mode) {
                return message.reply(
                    '❌ Invalid mode! Usage: `%code <mode> ```<language>\ncode\n```\n\n' +
                    '**Modes:**\n1️⃣ Basic output\n2️⃣ Output + modules\n3️⃣ All info'
                );
            }

            // Extract code block from message content
            const validator = new CodeValidator();
            const codeBlock = validator.extractCodeBlock(message.content);

            if (!codeBlock.found) {
                return message.reply(
                    '❌ No code block found! Usage: `%code <mode> ```<language>\ncode\n```\n\n'
                );
            }

            const { code, language } = codeBlock;

            // Show "thinking" reaction
            await message.react('⏳');

            // Validate code
            const validation = validator.validateCode(code, language);
            if (!validation.valid) {
                await message.reactions.removeAll().catch(() => { });
                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('❌ Code Validation Failed')
                    .setDescription(validation.errors.join('\n'))
                    .setColor(0xFF0000)
                    .setFooter({ text: `Language: ${language}` });
                return message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
            }

            try {
                // Execute code
                const executor = new CodeExecutor();
                const result = await executor.execute(code, language);

                await message.reactions.removeAll().catch(() => { });

                if (!result.success) {
                    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                        .setTitle('❌ Execution Error')
                        .setDescription(`\`\`\`${language}\n${result.error}\n\`\`\``)
                        .setColor(0xFF0000)
                        .setFooter({ text: `Language: ${language}` });
                    return message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
                }

                // Format output based on mode
                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle(`✅ Code Executed (${language})`)
                    .setColor(0x00FF00)
                    .setFooter({ text: message.author.username });

                // Mode 1: Basic output
                if (mode === '1') {
                    const output = result.output.length > 1024
                        ? result.output.substring(0, 1021) + '...'
                        : result.output;
                    embed.addFields({
                        name: '📤 Output',
                        value: `\`\`\`\n${output}\n\`\`\``,
                        inline: false
                    });
                }
                // Mode 2: Output + modules
                else if (mode === '2') {
                    const output = result.output.length > 800
                        ? result.output.substring(0, 797) + '...'
                        : result.output;
                    embed.addFields({
                        name: '📤 Output',
                        value: `\`\`\`\n${output}\n\`\`\``,
                        inline: false
                    });

                    if (result.modules.length > 0) {
                        const modules = result.modules.join(', ');
                        const modulesField = modules.length > 1024
                            ? modules.substring(0, 1021) + '...'
                            : modules;
                        embed.addFields({
                            name: '📚 Modules Used',
                            value: modulesField,
                            inline: false
                        });
                    } else {
                        embed.addFields({
                            name: '📚 Modules Used',
                            value: 'None',
                            inline: false
                        });
                    }
                }
                // Mode 3: All info
                else if (mode === '3') {
                    const output = result.output.length > 700
                        ? result.output.substring(0, 697) + '...'
                        : result.output;
                    embed.addFields({
                        name: '📤 Output',
                        value: `\`\`\`\n${output}\n\`\`\``,
                        inline: false
                    });

                    if (result.modules.length > 0) {
                        const modules = result.modules.join(', ');
                        const modulesField = modules.length > 512
                            ? modules.substring(0, 509) + '...'
                            : modules;
                        embed.addFields({
                            name: '📚 Modules',
                            value: modulesField,
                            inline: true
                        });
                    } else {
                        embed.addFields({
                            name: '📚 Modules',
                            value: 'None',
                            inline: true
                        });
                    }

                    embed.addFields({
                        name: '⏱️ Execution Time',
                        value: `${result.executionTime}ms`,
                        inline: true
                    });

                    embed.addFields({
                        name: '💬 Language',
                        value: language,
                        inline: true
                    });
                }

                await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });

            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Code execution error:', error);
                await message.reactions.removeAll().catch(() => { });
                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('❌ Unexpected Error')
                    .setDescription(`\`\`\`${error.message}\n\`\`\``)
                    .setColor(0xFF0000);
                message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
            }
        }
    });
}

console.log("✅ Code command started!")
module.exports = {
    setup
};
