//======================================================================
//                   ECONOMY COMMANDS (DATABASE VERSION)
//======================================================================

const { EmbedBuilder, SlashCommandBuilder } = require('discord.js');
const { ECONOMY_CONFIG, checkCooldown } = require('../systems/economySystem');
const DatabaseManager = require('../utils/DatabaseManager');

const dbManager = new DatabaseManager();

function weightedRandom(outcomes) {
  const items = Object.keys(outcomes);
  const weights = items.map(item => outcomes[item].weight);

  const totalWeight = weights.reduce((sum, weight) => sum + weight, 0);
  let random = Math.random() * totalWeight;

  for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
    random -= weights[i];
    if (random <= 0) {
      return items[i];
    }
  }

  return items[items.length - 1];
}

// ============ GAMBLE COMMAND ============
const gamble = {
  name: 'gbl',
  category: 'Economy',
  description: 'Gamble your money with various outcomes',
  aliases: ['gamble'],

  data: new SlashCommandBuilder()
    .setName('gbl')
    .setDescription('Gamble your money')
    .addStringOption(opt => opt
      .setName('amount')
      .setDescription('Amount to gamble (or "all")')
      .setRequired(true)
    ),

  async execute(interaction, client, config) {
    if (!interaction || !interaction.guild) return;
    const cooldown = checkCooldown(interaction.user.id, 'gbl', 2);
    if (cooldown.onCooldown) {
      return interaction.reply({
        content: `⏰ Cooldown! Wait ${Math.ceil(cooldown.timeLeft)} seconds.`,
        ephemeral: true
      });
    }

    const amount = interaction.options.getString('amount');
    let userBalance = dbManager.getBalance(interaction.guild.id, interaction.user.id);

    let betAmount;
    let isAllIn = false;

    if (amount.toLowerCase() === 'all') {
      if (userBalance < ECONOMY_CONFIG.MIN_BET) {
        return interaction.reply(`You need at least **$${ECONOMY_CONFIG.MIN_BET.toLocaleString()}** to gamble all!`);
      }
      betAmount = userBalance;
      isAllIn = true;
    } else {
      betAmount = parseInt(amount);

      if (isNaN(betAmount)) {
        return interaction.reply('Invalid amount! Use a number or `all`');
      }

      if (betAmount < ECONOMY_CONFIG.MIN_BET) {
        return interaction.reply(`Minimum bet is **$${ECONOMY_CONFIG.MIN_BET.toLocaleString()}**!`);
      }

      if (betAmount > ECONOMY_CONFIG.MAX_BET) {
        return interaction.reply(`Maximum bet is **$${ECONOMY_CONFIG.MAX_BET.toLocaleString()}**! Use \`/gbl all\` to bet everything.`);
      }
    }

    if (betAmount > userBalance) {
      return interaction.reply(`You don't have enough balance! You only have **$${userBalance.toLocaleString()}**`);
    }

    // Gambling outcomes
    const outcome = weightedRandom(ECONOMY_CONFIG.GAMBLING_OUTCOMES);
    const multiplier = ECONOMY_CONFIG.GAMBLING_OUTCOMES[outcome].multiplier;

    let resultText;
    let resultColor;

    if (multiplier < 0) {
      userBalance -= betAmount;
      resultText = `💸 You lost **$${betAmount.toLocaleString()}**!`;
      resultColor = 0xFF0000; // Red
    } else {
      const winnings = betAmount * multiplier;
      userBalance += winnings;

      if (outcome === 'win') {
        resultText = `✅ You won **$${winnings.toLocaleString()}**!`;
        resultColor = 0x00FF00; // Green
      } else if (outcome === 'big_win') {
        resultText = `🎉 Big Win! You won **$${winnings.toLocaleString()}**!`;
        resultColor = 0x0000FF; // Blue
      } else if (outcome === 'very_high_win') {
        resultText = `🌟 Very High Win! You won **$${winnings.toLocaleString()}**!`;
        resultColor = 0x800080; // Purple
      } else if (outcome === 'jackpot_win') {
        resultText = `💰 JACKPOT WIN! You won **$${winnings.toLocaleString()}**!`;
        resultColor = 0xFFD700; // Gold
      } else if (outcome === 'LEGENDARY') {
        resultText = `🔥💎 **LEGENDARY WIN!!** 💎🔥\n**YOU WON $${winnings.toLocaleString()}!!**\n✨ You're a LEGEND! ✨`;
        resultColor = 0xFFD700; // Gold
      }
    }

    // Save balance to database
    dbManager.setBalance(interaction.guild.id, interaction.user.id, userBalance);

    // Create progress bar
    const progressBarLength = 30;
    const maxBalance = 1e50; // Match Python's huge number
    const filledLength = Math.min(
      Math.floor(progressBarLength * (userBalance / maxBalance)),
      progressBarLength
    );
    const bar = '█'.repeat(filledLength) + '░'.repeat(progressBarLength - filledLength);

    // Create embed
    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
      .setTitle('🎰 Gambling Results')
      .setColor(resultColor)
      .setAuthor({
        name: interaction.user.username,
        iconURL: interaction.user.displayAvatarURL()
      })
      .addFields(
        {
          name: '💵 Bet Amount',
          value: isAllIn ? `**ALL IN:** $${betAmount.toLocaleString()}` : `**$${betAmount.toLocaleString()}**`,
          inline: false
        },
        { name: '🎲 Result', value: resultText, inline: false },
        { name: '💰 New Balance', value: `**$${userBalance.toLocaleString()}**`, inline: false },
        { name: '📊 Balance Progress', value: `\`[${bar}]\``, inline: false }
      )
      .setFooter({ text: 'Gamble responsibly! Use /gbl all to bet everything!' });

    await interaction.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
  },

  async run(message, args, client, config) {
    const cooldown = checkCooldown(message.author.id, 'gbl', 2);
    if (cooldown.onCooldown) {
      return message.reply(`⏰ Cooldown! Wait ${Math.ceil(cooldown.timeLeft)} seconds.`);
    }

    if (args.length === 0) {
      return message.reply('Usage: `%gbl <amount>` or `%gbl all`');
    }

    const amount = args[0];
    let userBalance = dbManager.getBalance(message.guild.id, message.author.id);

    let betAmount;
    let isAllIn = false;

    if (amount.toLowerCase() === 'all') {
      if (userBalance < ECONOMY_CONFIG.MIN_BET) {
        return message.reply(`You need at least **$${ECONOMY_CONFIG.MIN_BET.toLocaleString()}** to gamble all!`);

    } else {

      }

        return message.reply(`Minimum bet is **$${ECONOMY_CONFIG.MIN_BET.toLocaleString()}**!`);
      }

      if (betAmount > ECONOMY_CONFIG.MAX_BET) {
        return message.reply(`Maximum bet is **$${ECONOMY_CONFIG.MAX_BET.toLocaleString()}**! Use \`%gbl all\` to bet everything.`);
      }
    }

    if (betAmount > userBalance) {
      return message.reply(`You don't have enough balance! You only have **$${userBalance.toLocaleString()}**`);
    }

    // Gambling outcomes
    const outcome = weightedRandom(ECONOMY_CONFIG.GAMBLING_OUTCOMES);
    const multiplier = ECONOMY_CONFIG.GAMBLING_OUTCOMES[outcome].multiplier;

    let resultText;
    let resultColor;

    if (multiplier < 0) {
      userBalance -= betAmount;
      resultText = `💸 You lost **$${betAmount.toLocaleString()}**!`;
      resultColor = 0xFF0000;
    } else {

        resultColor = 0x00FF00;

// ============ ADD MONEY (OWNER ONLY) ============
const addmoney = {
  name: 'addmoney',
  category: 'Economy',
  description: 'Add money to a user (Owner only)',

  async execute(message, args, client) {
    const config = client.config || require('../config');
    if (message.author.id !== config.OWNER_ID && !message.member.permissions.has('ManageGuild')) {
      return message.reply({ content: '❌ You need Manage Server permission!', ephemeral: true });
    }

    if (args.length < 2) {
      return message.reply('Usage: `%addmoney <@user> <amount>`');
    }

    const member = message.mentions.members.first();
    const amount = parseInt(args[1]);

    if (!member) {
      return message.reply('❌ Please mention a user');
    }

    if (isNaN(amount) || amount <= 0) {
      return message.reply('❌ Amount must be positive');
    }

    dbManager.addUserBalance(message.guild.id, member.id, amount);

    await message.reply(`✅ Added **$${amount.toLocaleString()}** to ${member.user.tag}'s balance`);
  }
};

// ============ REMOVE MONEY (OWNER ONLY) ============
const removemoney = {
  name: 'removemoney',
  category: 'Economy',
  description: 'Remove money from a user (Owner only)',

  async execute(message, args, client) {
    const config = client.config || require('../config');
    if (message.author.id !== config.OWNER_ID && !message.member.permissions.has('ManageGuild')) {
      return message.reply('❌ You need Manage Server permission!');
    }

    if (args.length < 2) {
      return message.reply('Usage: `%removemoney <@user> <amount>`');
    }

    const member = message.mentions.members.first();
    const amount = parseInt(args[1]);

    if (!member) {
      return message.reply('❌ Please mention a user');
    }

    if (isNaN(amount) || amount <= 0) {
      return message.reply('❌ Amount must be positive');
    }

    const current = dbManager.getBalance(message.guild.id, member.id);
    const newBalance = Math.max(0, current - amount);
    dbManager.setBalance(message.guild.id, member.id, newBalance);

    await message.reply(`✅ Removed **$${amount.toLocaleString()}** from ${member.user.tag}'s balance`);
  }
};

      return message.reply('❌ You need Manage Server permission');
    }

    if (args.length < 2) {
      return message.reply('Usage: `%setmoney <@user> <amount>`');
    }

    const member = message.mentions.members.first();
    const amount = parseInt(args[1]);

    if (!member) {
      return message.reply('❌ Please mention a user');
    }

    if (isNaN(amount) || amount < 0) {
      return message.reply('❌ Amount cannot be negative');
    }

    dbManager.setBalance(message.guild.id, member.id, amount);

    await message.reply(`✅ Set ${member.user.tag}'s balance to **$${amount.toLocaleString()}**`);
  }
};

// ============ BALTOP / BALANCETOP (PER SERVER) ============
const baltop = {
  name: 'baltop',
  category: 'Economy',
  description: 'Show top 10 richest users in this server',
  aliases: ['balancetop', 'leaderboard'],

  data: new SlashCommandBuilder()
    .setName('baltop')
    .setDescription('Show top 10 richest users in this server'),

  async execute(interaction, client, config) {
    if (!interaction || !interaction.guild) return;

    const topUsers = dbManager.getTopBalances(interaction.guild.id, 10);

    if (topUsers.length === 0) {
      return interaction.reply('💭 No one has any money yet!');
    }

    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
      .setTitle(`💰 ${interaction.guild.name} - Top 10 Richest`)
      .setColor(0xFFD700)
      .setThumbnail(interaction.guild.iconURL())
      .setTimestamp();

    let description = '';

    for (let i = 0; i < topUsers.length; i++) {
      const { user_id, balance } = topUsers[i];
      const user = await client.users.fetch(user_id).catch(() => null);
      const name = user ? user.tag : 'Unknown User';

      const medal = i === 0 ? '🥇' : i === 1 ? '🥈' : i === 2 ? '🥉' : `#${i + 1}`;
      description += `${medal} **${name}**: $${balance.toLocaleString()}\n`;
    }

    embed.setDescription(description);

    await interaction.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
  },

  async run(message, args, client, config) {
    const topUsers = dbManager.getTopBalances(message.guild.id, 10);

    if (topUsers.length === 0) {
      return message.reply('💭 No one has any money yet!');
    }

    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
      .setTitle(`💰 ${message.guild.name} - Top 10 Richest`)
      .setColor(0xFFD700)
      .setThumbnail(message.guild.iconURL())
      .setTimestamp();

    let description = '';

    for (let i = 0; i < topUsers.length; i++) {
      const { user_id, balance } = topUsers[i];
      const user = await client.users.fetch(user_id).catch(() => null);
      const name = user ? user.tag : 'Unknown User';

      const medal = i === 0 ? '🥇' : i === 1 ? '🥈' : i === 2 ? '🥉' : `#${i + 1}`;
      description += `${medal} **${name}**: $${balance.toLocaleString()}\n`;
    }

    embed.setDescription(description);

    await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
  }
};

// ============ TOPGLOBAL (ALL SERVERS) ============
const topglobal = {
  name: 'topglobal',
  category: 'Economy',
  description: 'Show top 10 richest users across ALL servers',
  aliases: ['globaltop', 'globalleaderboard'],

  data: new SlashCommandBuilder()
    .setName('topglobal')
    .setDescription('Show top 10 richest users across ALL servers'),

  async execute(interaction, client, config) {
    if (!interaction || !interaction.deferReply) {
      console.error('topglobal: This function is for slash commands only');
      return;
    }
    await interaction.deferReply();

    const topUsers = dbManager.getGlobalTopBalances(10);

    if (topUsers.length === 0) {
      return interaction.editReply('💭 No one has any money yet!');
    }

    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
      .setTitle('🌎 Global Top 10 Richest Users')
      .setColor(0xFFD700)
      .setThumbnail(client.user.displayAvatarURL())
      .setTimestamp();

    let description = '';

    for (let i = 0; i < topUsers.length; i++) {
      const { user_id, total_balance } = topUsers[i];
      const user = await client.users.fetch(user_id).catch(() => null);

      if (user) {
        const medal = i === 0 ? '🥇' : i === 1 ? '🥈' : i === 2 ? '🥉' : `#${i + 1}`;
        description += `${medal} **${user.tag}**: $${total_balance.toLocaleString()}\n`;
      }
    }

    embed.setDescription(description);

    // Add top user's avatar
    if (topUsers.length > 0) {
      const topUser = await client.users.fetch(topUsers[0].user_id).catch(() => null);
      if (topUser) {
        embed.setThumbnail(topUser.displayAvatarURL({ size: 256 }));
      }
    }

    await interaction.editReply({ embeds: [embed] });
  },

  async run(message, args, client, config) {
    const loadingMsg = await message.reply('🔄 Loading global leaderboard...');

    const topUsers = dbManager.getGlobalTopBalances(10);

    if (topUsers.length === 0) {
      return loadingMsg.edit('💭 No one has any money yet!');
    }

    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
      .setTitle('🌎 Global Top 10 Richest Users')
      .setColor(0xFFD700)
      .setThumbnail(client.user.displayAvatarURL())
      .setTimestamp();

    let description = '';

    for (let i = 0; i < topUsers.length; i++) {
      const { user_id, total_balance } = topUsers[i];
      const user = await client.users.fetch(user_id).catch(() => null);

      if (user) {
        const medal = i === 0 ? '🥇' : i === 1 ? '🥈' : i === 2 ? '🥉' : `#${i + 1}`;
        description += `${medal} **${user.tag}**: $${total_balance.toLocaleString()}\n`;
      }
    }

    embed.setDescription(description);

    // Add top user's avatar
    if (topUsers.length > 0) {
      const topUser = await client.users.fetch(topUsers[0].user_id).catch(() => null);
      if (topUser) {
        embed.setThumbnail(topUser.displayAvatarURL({ size: 256 }));
      }
    }

    await loadingMsg.edit({ content: null, embeds: [embed] });
  }
};

function setup(client) {
  client.commands.set('gbl', gamble);
  client.commands.set('gamble', gamble);
  client.commands.set('bal', balance);
  client.commands.set('balance', balance);
  client.commands.set('money', balance);
  client.commands.set('addmoney', addmoney);
  client.commands.set('removemoney', removemoney);
  client.commands.set('setmoney', setmoney);
  client.commands.set('baltop', baltop);
  client.commands.set('balancetop', baltop);
  client.commands.set('leaderboard', baltop);
  client.commands.set('topglobal', topglobal);
  client.commands.set('globaltop', topglobal);
  client.commands.set('globalleaderboard', topglobal);

  console.log('✅ Economy commands registered (DATABASE VERSION)');
}

module.exports = {
  gamble,
  balance,
  addmoney,
  removemoney,
  setmoney,
  baltop,
  topglobal,
  setup
};

//======================================================================
//                   ENABLE XP COMMAND (ADMIN ONLY)
//======================================================================

const { EmbedBuilder, PermissionFlagsBits } = require('discord.js');
const DatabaseManager = require('../utils/DatabaseManager');

const dbManager = new DatabaseManager();

module.exports = {
    name: 'enablexp',
    description: 'Enable XP system for this server',
    aliases: ['xpenable'],

    async execute(message, args, client) {
        // Check permissions
        if (!message.member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.ManageGuild) &&
            message.author.id !== '972306366599397427') {
            return message.reply('❌ You need Manage Server permission!');
        }

        try {
            // Get current config
            const config = dbManager.getServerConfig(message.guild.id);

            // Enable XP
            config.xp_enabled = true;
            if (!config.xp_multiplier) {
                config.xp_multiplier = 1.0;
            }

            // Save config
            dbManager.saveServerConfig(message.guild.id, config);

            const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                .setTitle('✅ XP System Enabled!')
                .setDescription(`The XP system is now **enabled** for **${message.guild.name}**`)
                .setColor(0x00FF00)
                .addFields(
                    { name: 'XP Per Message', value: '15-25 XP (1 min cooldown)', inline: true },
                    { name: 'Server Multiplier', value: `${config.xp_multiplier}x`, inline: true },
                    { name: 'Booster Bonus', value: '2x XP', inline: true }
                )
                .setFooter({ text: 'Users will now gain XP from messages!' })
                .setTimestamp();

            await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });

            console.log(`✅ XP enabled for server: ${message.guild.name} (${message.guild.id})`);

        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Error enabling XP:', error);
            await message.reply(`❌ Error: ${error.message}`);
        }
    },

    async run(message, args, client) {
        return this.execute(message, args, client);
    }
};

///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//                         FUN COMMANDS                               //

const { description } = require('./math');
const databaseManager = require('../utils/DatabaseManager');
const { EmbedBuilder } = require('discord.js');

const dataManager = new databaseManager();
function setup(client, afkUsers) {
    // ============ PING COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('ping', {
        name: 'ping',
        description: 'Check bot latency',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const sent = await message.reply('🏓 Pinging...');
            const latency = sent.createdTimestamp - message.createdTimestamp;
            const apiLatency = Math.round(client.ws.ping);

            await sent.edit(
                `🏓 Pong!\n` +
                `📡 Latency: **${latency}ms**\n` +
                `💓 API Latency: **${apiLatency}ms**`
            );
        }
    });

    // ============ AFK COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('afk', {
        name: 'afk',
        description: 'Set AFK status',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const reason = args.join(' ') || 'AFK';
            const timestamp = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

            // Lưu vào Map (để truy xuất nhanh)
            client.afkUsers.set(message.author.id, { reason, time: timestamp });

            // Lưu vào SQL (để lưu vĩnh viễn)
            dataManager.setAFK(message.guild.id, message.author.id, reason, timestamp);

            const embed = new EmbedBuilder().setColor('#3498db').setDescription(`✅ You are now AFK: **${reason}**`);
            await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
        }
    });


    // ============ 8BALL COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('8ball', {
        name: '8ball',
        description: 'Ask the magic 8ball',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            if (args.length === 0) {
                return message.reply('❌ Ask me a question!');
            }

            const responses = [
                'Yes, definitely!', 'It is certain.', 'Without a doubt.',
                'You may rely on it.', 'As I see it, yes.', 'Most likely.',
                'Outlook good.', 'Signs point to yes.', 'Reply hazy, try again.',
                'Ask again later.', 'Better not tell you now.', 'Cannot predict now.',
                'Concentrate and ask again.', 'Don\'t count on it.', 'My reply is no.',
                'My sources say no.', 'Outlook not so good.', 'Very doubtful.'
            ];

            const answer = responses[Math.floor(Math.random() * responses.length)];
            await message.reply(`🎱 ${answer}`);
        }
    });

    // ============ ROLL COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('roll', {
        name: 'roll',
        aliases: ['dice'],
        description: 'Roll a dice',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const sides = parseInt(args[0]) || 6;

            if (sides < 2 || sides > 100) {
                return message.reply('❌ Dice must have 2-100 sides!');
            }

            const result = Math.floor(Math.random() * sides) + 1;
            await message.reply(`🎲 You rolled a **${result}** (1-${sides})`);
        }
    });

    client.commands.set('dice', client.commands.get('roll'));

    // ============ FLIP COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('flip', {
        name: 'flip',
        aliases: ['coin', 'coinflip'],
        description: 'Flip a coin',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const result = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'Heads' : 'Tails';
            await message.reply(`🪙 ${result}!`);
        }
    });

    client.commands.set('coin', client.commands.get('flip'));
    client.commands.set('coinflip', client.commands.get('flip'));

    // ============ SAY COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('say', {
        name: 'say',
        aliases: ['echo'],
        description: 'Make the bot say something, optionally in a specific channel',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            if (args.length === 0) return

            const logIndex = args.indexOf('-c');
            let targetChannel = message.channel;
            let contentArgs = [...args];

            if (logIndex !== -1) {
                const channelMention = args[logIndex + 1];
                const foundChannel = message.mentions.channels.first() ||
                    message.guild.channels.cache.get(channelMention);

                if (foundChannel) {
                    targetChannel = foundChannel;

                    contentArgs.splice(logIndex, 2);
                } else {
                    return
                }
            }

            const text = contentArgs.join(' ');
            if (!text) return message.reply('❌ What should i say?');

            try {
                await message.delete();
            } catch (error) {

            }

            try {
                await targetChannel.send(text);
            } catch (error) {

            }
        }
    });

    client.commands.set('echo', client.commands.get('say'));

    // ============ CAT COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('cat', {
        name: 'cat',
        description: 'Get a random cat image',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const CAT_API_KEY = process.env.CAT_API_KEY;

            if (!CAT_API_KEY) {
                return message.reply('❌ Cat API key is not configured! Please add CAT_API_KEY to .env file.');
            }

            try {
                const { EmbedBuilder } = require('discord.js');
                const fetch = require('undici').fetch;

                const response = await fetch('https://api.thecatapi.com/v1/images/search', {
                    headers: {
                        'x-api-key': CAT_API_KEY
                    }
                });

                if (!response.ok) {
                    return message.reply('❌ Failed to fetch cat image. API returned an error.');
                }

                const data = await response.json();

                if (!data || data.length === 0) {
                    return message.reply('❌ No cat images found!');
                }

                const catImage = data[0].url;

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('🐱 Random Cat')
                    .setImage(catImage)
                    .setColor(0xFF9500)
                    .setFooter({ text: `Requested by ${message.author.username}` });

                await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });

            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Cat command error:', error);
                await message.reply('❌ Failed to fetch cat image. Please try again later.');
            }
        }
    });

    // ============ DOG COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('dog', {
        name: 'dog',
        description: 'Get a random dog image',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const DOG_API_KEY = process.env.DOG_API_KEY;

            if (!DOG_API_KEY) {
                return message.reply('❌ Dog API key is not configured! Please add DOG_API_KEY to .env file.');
            }

            try {
                const { EmbedBuilder } = require('discord.js');
                const fetch = require('undici').fetch;

                const response = await fetch('https://api.thedogapi.com/v1/images/search', {
                    headers: {
                        'x-api-key': DOG_API_KEY
                    }
                });

                if (!response.ok) {
                    return message.reply('❌ Failed to fetch dog image. API returned an error.');
                }

                const data = await response.json();

                if (!data || data.length === 0) {
                    return message.reply('❌ No dog images found!');
                }

                const dogImage = data[0].url;

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('🐶 Random Dog')
                    .setImage(dogImage)
                    .setColor(0xD2691E)
                    .setFooter({ text: `Requested by ${message.author.username}` });

                await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });

            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Dog command error:', error);
                await message.reply('❌ Failed to fetch dog image. Please try again later.');
            }
        }
    });
    // ============ GAYRATE COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('gayrate', {
        name: 'gayrate',
        description: 'Calculate gay rate',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const user = message.mentions.users.first() || message.author;
            const rate = Math.floor(Math.random() * 101);

            let emoji = '🏳️‍🌈';
            if (rate < 30) emoji = '😐';
            else if (rate < 70) emoji = '🏳️‍🌈';
            else emoji = '🌈✨';

            await message.reply(`${emoji} **${user.username}** is **${rate}%** gay!`);
        }
    });

    // ============ IQTEST COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('iqtest', {
        name: 'iqtest',
        description: 'Test IQ',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const user = message.mentions.users.first() || message.author;
            const iq = Math.floor(Math.random() * 151) + 50; // 50-200

            let emoji = '🧠';
            let comment = '';

            if (iq < 70) {
                emoji = '🤔';
                comment = '(Below Average)';
            } else if (iq < 90) {
                emoji = '😐';
                comment = '(Average)';
            } else if (iq < 110) {
                emoji = '🙂';
                comment = '(Good)';
            } else if (iq < 130) {
                emoji = '🧠';
                comment = '(Very Good)';
            } else if (iq < 150) {
                emoji = '🤓';
                comment = '(Superior)';
            } else {
                emoji = '🧙‍♂️';
                comment = '(Genius!)';
            }

            await message.reply(`${emoji} **${user.username}**'s IQ is **${iq}** ${comment}`);
        }
    });

    // ============ SHIP COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('ship', {
        name: 'ship',
        description: 'Ship two users',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const mentions = message.mentions.users;

            if (mentions.size < 2) {
                return message.reply('❌ Please mention 2 users to ship! Example: `%ship @user1 @user2`');
            }

            const users = Array.from(mentions.values()).slice(0, 2);
            const compatibility = Math.floor(Math.random() * 101);

            let emoji = '💔';
            let comment = 'Not compatible...';

            if (compatibility < 30) {
                emoji = '💔';
                comment = 'Not meant to be...';
            } else if (compatibility < 50) {
                emoji = '😐';
                comment = 'Could work...';
            } else if (compatibility < 70) {
                emoji = '❤️';
                comment = 'Good match!';
            } else if (compatibility < 90) {
                emoji = '💕';
                comment = 'Great couple!';
            } else {
                emoji = '💖✨';
                comment = 'Perfect match!';
            }

            await message.reply(
                `${emoji} **${users[0].username}** x **${users[1].username}**\n` +
                `💘 Compatibility: **${compatibility}%**\n` +
                `${comment}`
            );
        }
    });


    // ============ HELP COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('help', {
        name: 'help',
        aliases: ['commands'],
        description: 'Show all commands',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const { EmbedBuilder } = require('discord.js');

            const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                .setTitle('📚 Bot Commands')
                .setDescription(`Prefix: \`${client.config.COMMAND_PREFIX}\``)
                .setColor(0x00D4FF)
                .addFields(
                    {
                        name: '💰 Economy',

                        inline: false
                    },
                    {
                        name: '📊 Stats',
                        value: '`me`, `toptotal`, `topdaily`',
                        inline: false
                    },
                    {
                        name: '🧮 Math',
                        value: '`math <easy|medium|hard>`',
                        inline: false
                    },
                    {
                        name: '📊 Polls',
                        value: '`poll <question> | <option1> | <option2> ...`',
                        inline: false
                    },
                    {
                        name: '🎉 Fun',
                        value: '`ping`, `8ball`, `roll`, `flip`, `say`, `cat`, `dog`, `gayrate`, `iqtest`, `ship`',
                        inline: false
                    },
                    {
                        name: '⏰ Utility',
                        value: '`remind <time> <message>`, `afk <reason>',
                        inline: false
                    },
                    {
                        name: '🔨 Moderation',
                        value: '`kick`, `ban`, `timeout`, `clear`',
                        inline: false
                    }
                )
                .setFooter({ text: `Requested by ${message.author.username}` });

            await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
        }
    });

    client.commands.set('commands', client.commands.get('help'));

    console.log('✅ Fun commands loaded');
}

const { EmbedBuilder, SlashCommandBuilder } = require('discord.js');
const DatabaseManager = require('../utils/DatabaseManager');

const dbManager = new DatabaseManager();

// 💎 GEM SHOP ITEMS
const GEM_SHOP = {
    'profile_color': {
        name: 'Custom Profile Color',
        cost: 50,
        emoji: '🎨',
        type: 'cosmetic',
        description: 'Customize your XP card color!'
    },
    'xp_boost_1h': {
        name: '2x XP Boost (1 hour)',
        cost: 5,
        emoji: '⚡',
        type: 'boost',
        duration: 3600000, // 1 hour in ms
        description: 'Double XP from messages for 1 hour'
    },
    'xp_boost_24h': {
        name: '2x XP Boost (24 hours)',
        cost: 20,
        emoji: '⚡',
        type: 'boost',
        duration: 86400000, // 24 hours in ms
        description: 'Double XP from messages for 24 hours'
    },
    'money_boost_1h': {
        name: '2x Money Boost (1 hour)',
        cost: 5,
        emoji: '💰',
        type: 'boost',
        duration: 3600000,
        description: 'Double money from messages for 1 hour'
    },
    'giveaway_boost': {
        name: 'Giveaway Luck Boost',
        cost: 10,
        emoji: '🎁',
        type: 'boost',
        duration: 604800000, // 7 days
        description: '+50% extra entries in giveaways for 7 days'
    }
};

// ============ %GEMS COMMAND ============
const gems = {
    name: 'gems',
    category: 'Economy',
    description: 'Check your gem balance',
    aliases: ['gem', 'balgem'],

    data: new SlashCommandBuilder()
        .setName('gems')
        .setDescription('Check your gem balance')
        .addUserOption(opt => opt
            .setName('user')
            .setDescription('Check another user\'s gems')
            .setRequired(false)
        ),

    async execute(interaction, client, config) {
        const targetUser = interaction.options.getUser('user') || interaction.user;
        const gemBalance = dbManager.getUserGems(interaction.guild.id, targetUser.id);

        const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
            .setTitle(`💎 ${targetUser.username}'s Gems`)
            .setColor(0x9B59B6)
            .setThumbnail(targetUser.displayAvatarURL())
            .addFields(
                { name: 'Gem Balance', value: `**${gemBalance}** 💎`, inline: false },
                { name: 'How to Earn Gems', value: '• Win math quizzes\n• Daily streak bonuses\n• Special events', inline: false }
            )
            .setFooter({ text: 'Use /gemshop to spend gems!' });

        await interaction.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
    },

    async run(message, args, client, config) {
        let targetUser = message.author;

        if (message.mentions.users.size > 0) {
            targetUser = message.mentions.users.first();
        }

        const gemBalance = dbManager.getUserGems(message.guild.id, targetUser.id);

        const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
            .setTitle(`💎 ${targetUser.username}'s Gems`)
            .setColor(0x9B59B6)
            .setThumbnail(targetUser.displayAvatarURL())
            .addFields(
                { name: 'Gem Balance', value: `**${gemBalance}** 💎`, inline: false },
                { name: 'How to Earn Gems', value: '• Win math quizzes\n• Daily streak bonuses\n• Special events', inline: false }
            )
            .setFooter({ text: 'Use %gemshop to spend gems!' });

        await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
    }
};

// ============ %GEMSHOP COMMAND ============
const gemshop = {
    name: 'gemshop',
    category: 'Economy',
    description: 'Browse and purchase items with gems',
    aliases: ['gemsshop'],

    data: new SlashCommandBuilder()
        .setName('gemshop')
        .setDescription('Browse the gem shop')
        .addStringOption(opt => opt
            .setName('item')
            .setDescription('Item to purchase')
            .setRequired(false)
            .addChoices(
                { name: '🎨 Custom Profile Color - 50💎', value: 'profile_color' },
                { name: '⚡ 2x XP Boost (1h) - 5💎', value: 'xp_boost_1h' },
                { name: '⚡ 2x XP Boost (24h) - 20💎', value: 'xp_boost_24h' },
                { name: '💰 2x Money Boost (1h) - 5💎', value: 'money_boost_1h' },
                { name: '🎁 Giveaway Luck Boost - 10💎', value: 'giveaway_boost' }
            )
        )
        .addStringOption(opt => opt
            .setName('color')
            .setDescription('Hex color code (for profile color only, e.g., #FF0000)')
            .setRequired(false)
        ),

    async execute(interaction, client, config) {
        const itemId = interaction.options.getString('item');
        const colorHex = interaction.options.getString('color');

        // If no item selected, show shop
        if (!itemId) {
            return showShop(interaction, client);
        }

        // Purchase item
        return purchaseItem(interaction, client, itemId, colorHex);
    },

    async run(message, args, client, config) {
        // If no args, show shop
        if (args.length === 0) {
            return showShop(message, client);
        }

        // Purchase item: %gemshop <item_id> [color]
        const itemId = args[0].toLowerCase();
        const colorHex = args[1];

        return purchaseItem(message, client, itemId, colorHex);
    }
};

// ============ %ADDGEMS COMMAND (OWNER ONLY) ============
const addgems = {
    name: 'addgems',
    category: 'Owner',
    description: '👑 Add gems to a user (Owner only)',
    aliases: ['givegems'],
    ownerOnly: true,

    async run(message, args, client, config) {
        // Check if owner
        if (message.author.id !== '972306366599397427') {
            return message.reply('❌ This command is in beta!');
        }

        // Usage: %addgems @user <amount>
        if (args.length < 2) {
            return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%addgems @user <amount>`');
        }

        // Get target user
        const targetUser = message.mentions.users.first();
        if (!targetUser) {
            return message.reply('❌ Please mention a user! Example: `%addgems @Orange225673 100`');
        }

        // Get amount
        const amount = parseInt(args[1]);
        if (isNaN(amount)) {
            return message.reply('❌ Please provide a valid positive number!');
        }

        // Add gems
        dbManager.addGems(message.guild.id, targetUser.id, amount);
        const newBalance = dbManager.getUserGems(message.guild.id, targetUser.id);

        const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
            .setTitle('💎 Gems Added!')
            .setDescription(`Added **${amount}** 💎 to ${targetUser}`)
            .setColor(0x9B59B6)
            .addFields(
                { name: 'Amount Added', value: `+${amount} 💎`, inline: true },
                { name: 'New Balance', value: `${newBalance} 💎`, inline: true }
            )
            .setThumbnail(targetUser.displayAvatarURL())
            .setFooter({ text: '👑 Owner Command' })
            .setTimestamp();

        await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });

        console.log(`👑 ${message.author.tag} added ${amount} gems to ${targetUser.tag}`);
    }
};

// ============ %REMOVEGEMS COMMAND (OWNER ONLY) ============
const removegems = {
    name: 'removegems',
    category: 'Owner',
    description: '👑 Remove gems from a user (Owner only)',
    aliases: ['takegems'],
    ownerOnly: true,

    async run(message, args, client, config) {
        // Check if owner
        if (message.author.id !== '972306366599397427') {
            return message.reply('❌ This command is in beta!');
        }

        // Usage: %removegems @user <amount>
        if (args.length < 2) {
            return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%removegems @user <amount>`');
        }

        // Get target user
        const targetUser = message.mentions.users.first();
        if (!targetUser) {
            return message.reply('❌ Please mention a user!');
        }

        // Get amount
        const amount = parseInt(args[1]);
        if (isNaN(amount) || amount <= 0) {
            return message.reply('❌ Please provide a valid positive number!');
        }

        // Check if user has enough gems
        const currentBalance = dbManager.getUserGems(message.guild.id, targetUser.id);
        if (currentBalance < amount) {
            return message.reply(`❌ ${targetUser.username} only has **${currentBalance}** 💎!`);
        }

        // Remove gems
        dbManager.removeGems(message.guild.id, targetUser.id, amount);
        const newBalance = dbManager.getUserGems(message.guild.id, targetUser.id);

        const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
            .setTitle('💎 Gems Removed!')
            .setDescription(`Removed **${amount}** 💎 from ${targetUser}`)
            .setColor(0xE74C3C)
            .addFields(
                { name: 'Amount Removed', value: `-${amount} 💎`, inline: true },
                { name: 'New Balance', value: `${newBalance} 💎`, inline: true }
            )
            .setThumbnail(targetUser.displayAvatarURL())
            .setFooter({ text: '👑 Owner Command' })
            .setTimestamp();

        await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });

        console.log(`👑 ${message.author.tag} removed ${amount} gems from ${targetUser.tag}`);
    }
};

// ============ %SETGEMS COMMAND (OWNER ONLY) ============
const setgems = {
    name: 'setgems',
    category: 'Owner',
    description: '👑 Set a user\'s gem balance (Owner only)',
    ownerOnly: true,

    async run(message, args, client, config) {
        // Check if owner
        if (message.author.id !== '972306366599397427') {
            return message.reply('❌ This command is in beta!');
        }

        // Usage: %setgems @user <amount>
        if (args.length < 2) {
            return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%setgems @user <amount>`');
        }

        // Get target user
        const targetUser = message.mentions.users.first();
        if (!targetUser) {
            return message.reply('❌ Please mention a user!');
        }

        // Get amount
        const amount = parseInt(args[1]);
        if (isNaN(amount) || amount < 0) {
            return message.reply('❌ Please provide a valid number (0 or higher)!');
        }

        // Set gems
        dbManager.setGems(message.guild.id, targetUser.id, amount);

        const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
            .setTitle('💎 Gems Set!')
            .setDescription(`Set ${targetUser}'s gem balance to **${amount}** 💎`)
            .setColor(0xF1C40F)
            .addFields(
                { name: 'New Balance', value: `${amount} 💎`, inline: false }
            )
            .setThumbnail(targetUser.displayAvatarURL())
            .setFooter({ text: '👑 Owner Command' })
            .setTimestamp();

        await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });

        console.log(`👑 ${message.author.tag} set ${targetUser.tag}'s gems to ${amount}`);
    }
};

// ============ %GEMSTOP COMMAND (OWNER ONLY) ============
const gemstop = {
    name: 'gemstop',
    category: 'Owner',
    description: '👑 View all users with gems (Owner only)',
    aliases: ['gemslist', 'gemleaderboard'],
    ownerOnly: true,

    async run(message, args, client, config) {

        try {
            // Get all users with gems in this server
            const db = require('better-sqlite3')('./bot.db');
            const users = db.prepare(`
                SELECT user_id, gems 
                FROM economy 
                WHERE server_id = ? AND gems > 0
                ORDER BY gems DESC
                LIMIT 25
            `).all(message.guild.id);

            if (users.length === 0) {
                return message.reply('💎 No users have gems yet!');
            }

            // Build leaderboard
            const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                .setTitle('💎 Gem Leaderboard (Top 25)')
                .setDescription(`Total users with gems: **${users.length}**`)
                .setColor(0x9B59B6)
                .setFooter({ text: '👑 Owner Command' })
                .setTimestamp();

            let leaderboardText = '';
            let totalGems = 0;

            for (let i = 0; i < users.length; i++) {
                const user = users[i];
                const member = await message.guild.members.fetch(user.user_id).catch(() => null);
                const name = member ? member.displayName : `User ${user.user_id}`;

                const medal = i === 0 ? '🥇' : i === 1 ? '🥈' : i === 2 ? '🥉' : `${i + 1}.`;
                leaderboardText += `${medal} **${name}** - ${user.gems}💎\n`;
                totalGems += user.gems;
            }

            embed.addFields(
                { name: 'Leaderboard', value: leaderboardText || 'No data', inline: false },
                { name: 'Total Gems in Circulation', value: `${totalGems}💎`, inline: false }
            );

            await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });

        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Error in gemstop:', error);
            await message.reply('❌ An error occurred while fetching gem data.');
        }
    }
};

async function showShop(ctx, client) {
    const userGems = dbManager.getUserGems(ctx.guild.id, ctx.user?.id || ctx.author.id);

    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
        .setTitle('💎 Gem Shop')
        .setDescription(`Your Gems: **${userGems}** 💎\n\n**Available Items:**`)
        .setColor(0x9B59B6)
        .setFooter({ text: 'Use /gemshop <item> or %gemshop <item> to purchase!' });

    // Group items by type
    const cosmetics = [];
    const boosts = [];

    for (const [id, item] of Object.entries(GEM_SHOP)) {
        const canAfford = userGems >= item.cost;
        const affordText = canAfford ? '✅' : '❌';
        const itemText = `${item.emoji} **${item.name}** - ${item.cost}💎 ${affordText}\n*${item.description}*`;

        if (item.type === 'cosmetic') {
            cosmetics.push(itemText);
        } else {
            boosts.push(itemText);
        }
    }

    if (cosmetics.length > 0) {
        embed.addFields({ name: '🎨 Cosmetics', value: cosmetics.join('\n\n'), inline: false });
    }

    if (boosts.length > 0) {
        embed.addFields({ name: '⚡ Boosts', value: boosts.join('\n\n'), inline: false });
    }

    if (ctx.reply) {
        await ctx.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
    } else {
        await ctx.channel.send({ embeds: [embed] });
    }
}

async function purchaseItem(ctx, client, itemId, colorHex = null) {
    const userId = ctx.user?.id || ctx.author.id;
    const serverId = ctx.guild.id;

    // Validate item
    const item = GEM_SHOP[itemId];
    if (!item) {
        const errorMsg = '❌ Invalid item! Use `/gemshop` to see available items.';
        if (ctx.reply) {
            return ctx.reply({ content: errorMsg, ephemeral: true });
        } else {
            return ctx.channel.send(errorMsg);
        }
    }

    // Check balance
    const userGems = dbManager.getUserGems(serverId, userId);
    if (userGems < item.cost) {
        const errorMsg = `❌ Not enough gems! You have **${userGems}** 💎 but need **${item.cost}** 💎`;
        if (ctx.reply) {
            return ctx.reply({ content: errorMsg, ephemeral: true });
        } else {
            return ctx.channel.send(errorMsg);
        }
    }

    // Handle profile color purchase
    if (itemId === 'profile_color') {
        if (!colorHex) {
            const errorMsg = '❌ Please provide a color! Example: `/gemshop item:profile_color color:#FF0000`';
            if (ctx.reply) {
                return ctx.reply({ content: errorMsg, ephemeral: true });
            } else {
                return ctx.channel.send(errorMsg);
            }
        }

        // Validate hex color
        const hexPattern = /^#[0-9A-F]{6}$/i;
        if (!hexPattern.test(colorHex)) {
            const errorMsg = '❌ Invalid color! Use hex format like #FF0000';
            if (ctx.reply) {
                return ctx.reply({ content: errorMsg, ephemeral: true });
            } else {
                return ctx.channel.send(errorMsg);
            }
        }

        // Purchase
        dbManager.removeGems(serverId, userId, item.cost);
        dbManager.setProfileColor(serverId, userId, colorHex);
        dbManager.purchaseItem(serverId, userId, itemId, null, JSON.stringify({ color: colorHex }));

        const successMsg = `✅ Purchased **${item.name}**!\n🎨 Your XP card color is now ${colorHex}`;
        if (ctx.reply) {
            await ctx.reply(successMsg);
        } else {
            await ctx.channel.send(successMsg);
        }
        return;
    }

    // Handle boost purchases
    if (item.type === 'boost') {
        // Check if already have active boost of this type
        if (dbManager.hasActiveBoost(serverId, userId, itemId)) {
            const errorMsg = `❌ You already have an active **${item.name}**!`;
            if (ctx.reply) {
                return ctx.reply({ content: errorMsg, ephemeral: true });
            } else {
                return ctx.channel.send(errorMsg);
            }
        }

        // Purchase
        const expiresAt = Date.now() + item.duration;
        dbManager.removeGems(serverId, userId, item.cost);
        dbManager.purchaseItem(serverId, userId, itemId, expiresAt);

        const durationText = formatDuration(item.duration);
        const successMsg = `✅ Purchased **${item.name}**!\n⏱️ Active for **${durationText}**`;
        if (ctx.reply) {
            await ctx.reply(successMsg);
        } else {
            await ctx.channel.send(successMsg);
        }
        return;
    }
}

function formatDuration(ms) {
    const hours = Math.floor(ms / 3600000);
    const days = Math.floor(hours / 24);

    if (days > 0) return `${days} day${days > 1 ? 's' : ''}`;
    if (hours > 0) return `${hours} hour${hours > 1 ? 's' : ''}`;
    return `${Math.floor(ms / 60000)} minutes`;
}

function setup(client) {
    client.commands.set('gems', gems);
    client.commands.set('gem', gems);
    client.commands.set('balgem', gems);

    client.commands.set('gemshop', gemshop);
    client.commands.set('gemsshop', gemshop);
    client.commands.set('gemstop', gemstop);

    // Owner-only gem management commands
    client.commands.set('addgems', addgems);
    client.commands.set('givegems', addgems);
    client.commands.set('removegems', removegems);
    client.commands.set('takegems', removegems);
    client.commands.set('setgems', setgems);

    console.log('✅ Gem commands registered (including owner commands)');
}

module.exports = {
    gems,
    gemshop,
    addgems,
    removegems,
    setgems,
    gemstop,
    setup,
    GEM_SHOP // Export for use in other systems

};

const { EmbedBuilder, PermissionFlagsBits, ChannelType } = require('discord.js');
const DatabaseManager = require('../utils/DatabaseManager');

const dbManager = new DatabaseManager();

module.exports = {
    name: 'levelup',
    description: 'Configure level-up announcement settings (Admin only)',
    aliases: ['lvlup', 'levelconfig'],

    async execute(message, args, client) {
        // Check permissions
        if (!message.member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.ManageGuild) &&
            message.author.id !== '972306366599397427') {
            return message.reply('❌ You need Manage Server permission!');
        }

        try {
            const config = dbManager.getServerConfig(message.guild.id);

            // Initialize defaults if missing
            if (config.levelup_enabled === undefined) config.levelup_enabled = true;
            if (config.levelup_min_level === undefined) config.levelup_min_level = 5;

            // If no args, show current settings
            if (args.length === 0) {
                const channelMention = config.levelup_channel_id ?
                    `<#${config.levelup_channel_id}>` :
                    '**Same channel as message**';

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('🎉 Level-Up Announcement Settings')
                    .setDescription(`Configuration for **${message.guild.name}**`)
                    .setColor(config.levelup_enabled ? 0x00FF00 : 0xFF0000)
                    .addFields(
                        {
                            name: 'Status',
                            value: config.levelup_enabled ? '✅ Enabled' : '❌ Disabled',
                            inline: true
                        },
                        {
                            name: 'Minimum Level',
                            value: `Level ${config.levelup_min_level}+`,
                            inline: true
                        },
                        {
                            name: 'Announcement Channel',
                            value: channelMention,
                            inline: false
                        },
                        {
                            name: 'Available Commands',
                            value:
                                '`%levelup toggle` - Enable/disable announcements\n' +
                                '`%levelup channel <#channel>` - Set announcement channel\n' +
                                '`%levelup channel reset` - Reset to default (same channel)\n' +
                                '`%levelup minlevel <number>` - Set minimum level to announce\n' +
                                '\n**Examples:**\n' +
                                '`%levelup channel #level-ups` - Send to #level-ups\n' +
                                '`%levelup minlevel 10` - Only announce level 10+',
                            inline: false
                        }
                    )
                    .setFooter({ text: 'Use the commands above to change settings' })
                    .setTimestamp();

                return message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
            }

            const action = args[0].toLowerCase();

            // Toggle announcements
            if (action === 'toggle') {
                config.levelup_enabled = !config.levelup_enabled;
                dbManager.saveServerConfig(message.guild.id, config);

                const status = config.levelup_enabled ? '✅ **enabled**' : '❌ **disabled**';
                return message.reply(`Level-up announcements ${status}!`);
            }

            // Set announcement channel
            if (action === 'channel' || action === 'ch') {
                if (args.length < 2) {
                    return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%levelup channel <#channel>` or `%levelup channel reset`');
                }

                // Reset to default
                if (args[1].toLowerCase() === 'reset' || args[1].toLowerCase() === 'remove') {
                    config.levelup_channel_id = null;
                    dbManager.saveServerConfig(message.guild.id, config);
                    return message.reply('✅ Level-up announcements will now appear in the **same channel** as messages!');
                }

                // Set specific channel
                const channelMention = message.mentions.channels.first();
                if (!channelMention) {
                    return message.reply('❌ Please mention a channel! Example: `%levelup channel #level-ups`');
                }

                if (!channelMention.isTextBased()) {
                    return message.reply('❌ Please mention a text channel!');
                }

                config.levelup_channel_id = channelMention.id;
                dbManager.saveServerConfig(message.guild.id, config);

                return message.reply(`✅ Level-up announcements will now be sent to ${channelMention}!`);
            }

            // Set minimum level
            if (action === 'minlevel' || action === 'min') {
                if (args.length < 2) {
                    return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%levelup minlevel <number>`\nExample: `%levelup minlevel 5`');
                }

                const minLevel = parseInt(args[1]);

                if (isNaN(minLevel) || minLevel < 0 || minLevel > 100) {
                    return message.reply('❌ Minimum level must be between 0 and 100!');
                }

                config.levelup_min_level = minLevel;
                dbManager.saveServerConfig(message.guild.id, config);

                if (minLevel === 0) {
                    return message.reply('✅ Level-up announcements will now show for **all levels**!');
                } else {
                    return message.reply(`✅ Level-up announcements will now only show for **level ${minLevel} and above**!`);
                }
            }

            // Unknown action
            return message.reply('❌ Unknown action! Run `%levelup` to see all commands.');

        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Error in levelup command:', error);
            await message.reply(`❌ Error: ${error.message}`);
        }
    },

    async run(message, args, client) {
        return this.execute(message, args, client);
    }
};

const { SlashCommandBuilder, PermissionFlagsBits } = require('discord.js');
const mathSystem = require('../systems/mathSystem');
const { Easy, Hard, Extreme } = require('../systems/mathProblems');
const { createTimestamp } = require('../utils/timestampHelper');
const config = require('../config');

const easyProblems = new Easy();
const hardProblems = new Hard();
const extremeProblems = new Extreme();

const hardMethods = Object.getOwnPropertyNames(Object.getPrototypeOf(hardProblems))
    .filter(name =>
        typeof hardProblems[name] === 'function' &&
        name !== 'constructor' &&
        !name.startsWith('_')
    );

const easyMethods = Object.getOwnPropertyNames(Object.getPrototypeOf(easyProblems))
    .filter(name =>
        typeof easyProblems[name] === 'function' &&
        name !== 'constructor' &&
        !name.startsWith('_')
    );

const extremeMethods = Object.getOwnPropertyNames(Object.getPrototypeOf(extremeProblems))
    .filter(name =>
        typeof extremeProblems[name] === 'function' &&
        name !== 'constructor' &&
        !name.startsWith('_')
    );

const easyProblems_generators = [
    ...easyMethods.map(methodName => () => easyProblems[methodName]())
];

const hardProblems_generators = [
    ...easyProblems_generators,
    ...hardMethods.map(methodName => () => hardProblems[methodName]())
];

const extremeProblems_generators = [
    ...hardProblems_generators,
    ...extremeMethods.map(methodName => () => extremeProblems[methodName]())
];

// Only the specific difficulty problems (no mixing)
const easyOnly_generators = [
    ...easyMethods.map(methodName => () => easyProblems[methodName]())
];

const hardOnly_generators = [
    ...hardMethods.map(methodName => () => hardProblems[methodName]())
];

const extremeOnly_generators = [
    ...extremeMethods.map(methodName => () => extremeProblems[methodName]())
];

/**
 * Select random problems from difficulty level
 * @param {Array} problemArray - Array of problem generator functions
 * @param {number} count - Number of problems to select
 * @returns {Array} Selected problem generators
 */
function selectRandomProblems(problemArray, count = 5) {
    const shuffled = [...problemArray];
    for (let i = shuffled.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
        const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
        [shuffled[i], shuffled[j]] = [shuffled[j], shuffled[i]];
    }
    return shuffled.slice(0, Math.min(count, shuffled.length));
}

function setup(client) {
    // Register prefix commands
    client.commands.set('math', module.exports);
    client.commands.set('math-start', module.exports);
    client.commands.set('mquiz', module.exports);

    console.log('✅ Math commands registered');
    console.log(`- Easy mode: ${easyProblems_generators.length} problems (${easyOnly_generators.length} unique)`);
    console.log(`- Hard mode: ${hardProblems_generators.length} problems (${hardOnly_generators.length} unique)`);
    console.log(`- Extreme mode: ${extremeProblems_generators.length} problems (${extremeOnly_generators.length} unique)`);
}

module.exports = {
    setup,
    name: 'math',
    description: 'Start a math quiz',
    aliases: ['math-start', 'mquiz'],
    category: 'fun',

    async run(message, args) {
        try {
            // Parse arguments
            let difficulty = 'easy';
            let useOnly = false;
            let autoCorrection = false;

            // Check for flags (owner only)
            const isOwner = message.author.id === config.OWNER_ID;

            for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
                const arg = args[i].toLowerCase();

                if (['easy', 'hard', 'extreme'].includes(arg)) {
                    difficulty = arg;
                } else if (arg === '--only') {
                    useOnly = true;
                } else if (arg === '--auto-correction' && isOwner) {
                    autoCorrection = true;
                }
            }

            // Validate difficulty
            if (!['easy', 'hard', 'extreme'].includes(difficulty)) {
                return message.reply('`Usage: %math <easy|hard|extreme> [--only] [--auto-correction]`');
            }

            // If non-owner tries to use flags, ignore them silently
            if (!isOwner) {
                useOnly = false;
                autoCorrection = false;
            }

            // Select problem array based on difficulty and --only flag
            let problemArray;
            if (useOnly) {
                // Only problems from this difficulty
                if (difficulty === 'extreme') {
                    problemArray = extremeOnly_generators;
                } else if (difficulty === 'hard') {
                    problemArray = hardOnly_generators;
                } else {
                    problemArray = easyOnly_generators;
                }
            } else {
                // Normal mode (includes easier problems)
                if (difficulty === 'extreme') {
                    problemArray = extremeProblems_generators;
                } else if (difficulty === 'hard') {
                    problemArray = hardProblems_generators;
                } else {
                    problemArray = easyProblems_generators;
                }
            }

            const selectedProblems = selectRandomProblems(problemArray, problemArray.length);

            // AUTO-CORRECTION MODE (Owner only, for debugging)
            if (autoCorrection && isOwner) {
                await message.reply(`🔧 **DEBUG MODE: Auto-Correction Enabled**\n📝 Testing ${difficulty.toUpperCase()} problems${useOnly ? ' (ONLY)' : ''}...`);

                let successCount = 0;
                let failCount = 0;
                const errors = [];

                for (let i = 0; i < selectedProblems.length; i++) {
                    try {
                        const problem = selectedProblems[i]();

                        if (!problem || !problem.question || !problem.checker) {
                            throw new Error('Invalid problem structure');
                        }

                        // Test the problem by extracting answer from question or using checker
                        await message.channel.send(`**Question ${i + 1}:**\n${problem.question}`);

                        // Try to test the checker
                        const testResult = problem.checker("test");

                        await message.channel.send(`✅ Problem ${i + 1} generated successfully!\n*Checker function: ${typeof problem.checker === 'function' ? 'Valid' : 'Invalid'}*`);
                        successCount++;

                        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2000)); // 2 second delay
                    } catch (error) {
                        failCount++;
                        errors.push(`Problem ${i + 1}: ${error.message}`);
                        await message.channel.send(`❌ Problem ${i + 1} FAILED: ${error.message}`);
                    }
                }

                // Summary
                await message.channel.send(
                    `\n📊 **Debug Summary:**\n` +
                    `✅ Success: ${successCount}/${selectedProblems.length}\n` +
                    `❌ Failed: ${failCount}/${selectedProblems.length}\n` +
                    (errors.length > 0 ? `\n**Errors:**\n${errors.join('\n')}` : '')
                );

                return;
            }

            // NORMAL QUIZ MODE
            mathSystem.activePlayers.clear();

            const quizContext = {
                channel: message.channel,
                guild: message.guild,
                author: message.author,
            };

            // Show debug info for owner
            let debugInfo = '';
            if (isOwner && (useOnly || autoCorrection)) {
                debugInfo = `\n**MODE:** ${useOnly ? '[ONLY MODE] ' : ''}${autoCorrection ? '[AUTO-CORRECTION] ' : ''}`;
            }

            const joinMsg = await message.reply({
                content: `📊 **Math Quiz Starting!**\nReact with ✅ to join!\nQuiz starts ${createTimestamp(Date.now() + 30000, 'R')}...${debugInfo}`,
            });

            await joinMsg.react('✅');

            const filter = (reaction, user) => {
                return reaction.emoji.name === '✅' && !user.bot;
            };

            await joinMsg
                .awaitReactions({ filter, time: 30000 })
                .then((collected) => {
                    collected.first()?.users.cache.forEach((user) => {
                        if (!user.bot) {
                            mathSystem.activePlayers.set(user.id, 0);
                        }
                    });
                })
                .catch(() => { });

            if (mathSystem.activePlayers.size === 0) {
                return message.channel.send('❌ No one joined the quiz. Game cancelled.');
            }

            const playerList = Array.from(mathSystem.activePlayers.keys())
                .map(userId => `<@${userId}>`)
                .join(', ');

            await message.channel.send(
                `🎮 **Quiz Started!** Players: ${playerList}\n\n**${difficulty.toUpperCase()} Difficulty.**${useOnly ? ' **(ONLY MODE)**' : ''}`
            );

            console.log(`🎮 Starting quiz: difficulty=${difficulty}, only=${useOnly}, auto-correction=${autoCorrection}`);
            await mathSystem.runQuiz(quizContext, selectedProblems, difficulty);

        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Error in math command:', error);
            message.reply(`❌ Error: ${error.message}`).catch(() => { });
        }
    },
};

///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//                    MATH STOP/SURRENDER COMMAND                    //
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

const { EmbedBuilder } = require('discord.js');
const mathSystem = require('../systems/mathSystem');
const config = require('../config');

// ============ STATE MANAGEMENT ============

// Track active votes per channel
const activeVotes = new Map(); // channelId → voteData

// Track cooldowns per user (global across all games)
const voteCooldowns = new Map(); // userId → timestamp

// ============ VOTE DATA STRUCTURE ============
/*
{
    guildId: string,
    channelId: string,
    messageId: string,
    message: Message,
    startTime: number,
    endTime: number,
    requiredVotes: number,
    yesVotes: Set<userId>,
    noVotes: Set<userId>,
    eligibleVoters: Set<userId>,
    status: 'active' | 'passed' | 'failed',
    initiatorId: string
}
*/

// ============ HELPER FUNCTIONS ============

/**
 * Calculate required votes based on player count
 * @param {number} totalPlayers - Total number of players
 * @returns {number} Required votes to pass
 */
function getRequiredVotes(totalPlayers) {
    if (totalPlayers === 1) return 1;  // Solo = instant
    return totalPlayers - 1;           // Others = (n-1) votes
}

/**
 * Check if user is on cooldown
 * @param {string} userId - User ID to check
 * @returns {number} Seconds remaining (0 if not on cooldown)
 */
function getCooldownRemaining(userId) {
    const cooldownEnd = voteCooldowns.get(userId);
    if (!cooldownEnd || Date.now() >= cooldownEnd) {
        return 0;
    }
    return Math.ceil((cooldownEnd - Date.now()) / 1000);
}

/**
 * Apply cooldown to all eligible voters
 * @param {Set<string>} voters - Set of user IDs
 * @param {number} duration - Cooldown duration in milliseconds (default 3 minutes)
 */
function applyCooldown(voters, duration = 180000) {
    const cooldownEnd = Date.now() + duration;
    for (const userId of voters) {
        voteCooldowns.set(userId, cooldownEnd);
    }
}

/**
 * Stop the active game
 * @param {Object} interaction - Quiz context
 * @param {string} reason - Reason for stopping
 */
async function stopGame(interaction, reason = 'Vote passed') {
    // Clear active players
    mathSystem.activePlayers.clear();

    // Clear active quiz state
    mathSystem.stopCurrentQuiz();

    // Send stop message
    await interaction.channel.send({
        embeds: [
            new EmbedBuilder()
                .setTitle('🛑 Game Stopped')
                .setDescription(`The math quiz has been stopped.\n**Reason:** ${reason}`)
                .setColor('Red')
                .setTimestamp()
        ]
    });
}

/**
 * Update vote embed with current status
 * @param {Object} voteData - Vote data object
 */
async function updateVoteEmbed(voteData) {
    const { message, yesVotes, noVotes, requiredVotes, eligibleVoters, endTime } = voteData;

    const yesCount = yesVotes.size;
    const noCount = noVotes.size;
    const abstainCount = eligibleVoters.size - yesCount - noCount;
    const totalVoters = eligibleVoters.size;

    const passed = yesCount >= requiredVotes;

    // Create progress bar
    const barLength = 10;
    const yesBar = Math.floor((yesCount / totalVoters) * barLength);
    const noBar = Math.floor((noCount / totalVoters) * barLength);
    const progressBar = '🟩'.repeat(yesBar) + '🟥'.repeat(noBar) + '⬜'.repeat(barLength - yesBar - noBar);

    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
        .setTitle('🛑 Stop Game Vote')
        .setDescription(
            `A player wants to stop the game!\n\n` +
            `**Vote Progress:**\n${progressBar}\n\n` +
            `✅ **Yes:** ${yesCount}/${requiredVotes} (${Math.round(yesCount / totalVoters * 100)}%)\n` +
            `❌ **No:** ${noCount} (${Math.round(noCount / totalVoters * 100)}%)\n` +
            `⚪ **Abstain:** ${abstainCount}\n\n` +
            `React with ✅ to **stop** the game\n` +
            `React with ❌ to **continue** playing\n\n` +
            `⏰ Vote ends <t:${Math.floor(endTime / 1000)}:R>`
        )
        .setColor(passed ? 'Green' : yesCount > totalVoters / 2 ? 'Yellow' : 'Orange')
        .setFooter({ text: '⚠️ All players will be on 3-minute cooldown after this vote!' })
        .setTimestamp();

    if (passed) {
        embed.setTitle('✅ Vote Passed!');
        embed.setDescription(
            `The vote has passed! The game will stop now.\n\n` +
            `**Final Votes:**\n` +
            `✅ Yes: ${yesCount}/${requiredVotes}\n` +
            `❌ No: ${noCount}`
        );
    }

    try {
        await message.edit({ embeds: [embed] });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Failed to update vote embed:', error);
    }
}

/**
 * Start a vote to stop the game
 * @param {Message} message - Message that triggered the vote
 * @param {Object} interaction - Quiz context
 * @returns {Promise<void>}
 */
async function startVote(message, interaction) {
    const channelId = message.channel.id;

    // Filter out any bots that might have snuck into activePlayers
    const humanPlayers = new Set();
    for (const playerId of mathSystem.activePlayers.keys()) {
        // Fetch user to check if bot
        try {
            const user = await message.client.users.fetch(playerId);
            if (!user.bot) {
                humanPlayers.add(playerId);
            }
        } catch (error) {
            // If can't fetch, assume human and include
            humanPlayers.add(playerId);
        }
    }

    const totalPlayers = humanPlayers.size;
    const requiredVotes = getRequiredVotes(totalPlayers);
    const eligibleVoters = humanPlayers;

    // Solo player - instant stop
    if (totalPlayers === 1) {
        await message.reply('✅ You are the only player. Stopping the game...');
        await stopGame(interaction, 'Solo player quit');
        applyCooldown(eligibleVoters); // Apply cooldown to solo player
        return;
    }

    // Create vote embed
    const endTime = Date.now() + 35000; // 35 seconds
    const timestamp = Math.floor(endTime / 1000);

    const initialEmbed = new EmbedBuilder()
        .setTitle('🛑 Stop Game Vote')
        .setDescription(
            `${message.author} wants to stop the game!\n\n` +
            `**Required votes:** ${requiredVotes}/${totalPlayers}\n` +
            `**Current votes:** 1/${requiredVotes}\n\n` +
            `✅ = Yes, stop the game\n` +
            `❌ = No, continue playing\n\n` +
            `Vote ends <t:${timestamp}:R>`
        )
        .setColor('Yellow')
        .setFooter({ text: '⚠️ All players will be on 3-minute cooldown after this vote!' })
        .setTimestamp();

    const voteMessage = await message.channel.send({ embeds: [initialEmbed] });
    await voteMessage.react('✅');
    await voteMessage.react('❌');

    // Initialize vote data
    const voteData = {
        guildId: message.guild.id,
        channelId: channelId,
        messageId: voteMessage.id,
        message: voteMessage,
        startTime: Date.now(),
        endTime: endTime,
        requiredVotes: requiredVotes,
        yesVotes: new Set([message.author.id]), // Initiator auto-votes YES
        noVotes: new Set(),
        eligibleVoters: eligibleVoters,
        status: 'active',
        initiatorId: message.author.id
    };

    activeVotes.set(channelId, voteData);

    // Update embed with initiator's auto-vote
    await updateVoteEmbed(voteData);

    // Listen for reactions
    const filter = (reaction, user) => {
        return ['✅', '❌'].includes(reaction.emoji.name) &&
            eligibleVoters.has(user.id) &&
            !user.bot;
    };

    const collector = voteMessage.createReactionCollector({
        filter,
        time: 35000
    });

    collector.on('collect', async (reaction, user) => {
        // Double-check: ignore bots (shouldn't happen due to filter, but safety check)
        if (user.bot) return;

        // Remove from opposite vote
        if (reaction.emoji.name === '✅') {
            voteData.yesVotes.add(user.id);
            voteData.noVotes.delete(user.id);
        } else {
            voteData.noVotes.add(user.id);
            voteData.yesVotes.delete(user.id);
        }

        // Update embed
        await updateVoteEmbed(voteData);

        // Check if threshold reached
        if (voteData.yesVotes.size >= requiredVotes) {
            collector.stop('passed');
        }
    });

    collector.on('end', async (collected, reason) => {
        // Apply cooldown to ALL players regardless of result
        applyCooldown(eligibleVoters);

        if (reason === 'passed') {
            // Vote passed - stop the game
            voteData.status = 'passed';
            await updateVoteEmbed(voteData);
            await stopGame(interaction, 'Vote passed');
        } else {
            // Vote failed - continue game
            voteData.status = 'failed';

            const finalEmbed = new EmbedBuilder()
                .setTitle('❌ Vote Failed')
                .setDescription(
                    `The vote did not pass. The game continues!\n\n` +
                    `**Final Votes:**\n` +
                    `✅ Yes: ${voteData.yesVotes.size}/${requiredVotes}\n` +
                    `❌ No: ${voteData.noVotes.size}\n\n` +
                    `⏱️ All players are now on 3-minute cooldown.`
                )
                .setColor('Red')
                .setTimestamp();

            await voteMessage.edit({ embeds: [finalEmbed] });
        }

        // Clean up
        activeVotes.delete(channelId);
    });
}

// ============ COMMAND SETUP ============

function setup(client) {
    client.commands.set('math-stop', module.exports);
    client.commands.set('mathstop', module.exports);
    console.log('✅ Math stop command registered');
}

// ============ COMMAND EXPORT ============

module.exports = {
    setup,
    name: 'math-stop',
    description: 'Vote to stop the current math quiz',
    aliases: ['mathstop'],
    category: 'fun',

    async run(message, args) {
        try {
            // Prevent bots from triggering commands
            if (message.author.bot) return;

            const channelId = message.channel.id;
            const isOwner = message.author.id === config.OWNER_ID;

            // Check for --force flag (owner only)
            const forceStop = args.includes('--force') && isOwner;

            // Check if there's an active game
            if (mathSystem.activePlayers.size === 0) {
                return message.reply('❌ There is no active math quiz to stop.');
            }

            // Check if user is in the game
            if (!mathSystem.activePlayers.has(message.author.id) && !isOwner) {
                return message.reply('❌ You are not in this game! Only players can vote to stop.');
            }

            // Owner force stop
            if (forceStop) {
                await message.reply('🔧 **Owner override:** Stopping game immediately...');
                const quizContext = {
                    channel: message.channel,
                    guild: message.guild
                };
                await stopGame(quizContext, 'Owner force stop');
                return;
            }

            // Check if vote is already active
            if (activeVotes.has(channelId)) {
                return message.reply('⚠️ A vote is already in progress! React to the vote message above.');
            }

            // Check cooldown
            const cooldownRemaining = getCooldownRemaining(message.author.id);
            if (cooldownRemaining > 0) {
                const minutes = Math.floor(cooldownRemaining / 60);
                const seconds = cooldownRemaining % 60;
                const timeStr = minutes > 0 ?
                    `${minutes}m ${seconds}s` :
                    `${seconds}s`;

                return message.reply(
                    `⏱️ You're on cooldown! You can start another vote in **${timeStr}**.\n`
                );
            }

            // Start vote
            const quizContext = {
                channel: message.channel,
                guild: message.guild
            };

            await startVote(message, quizContext);

        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Error in math-stop command:', error);
            message.reply(`❌ Error: ${error.message}`).catch(() => { });
        }
    },

    // Export for testing/external use
    getRequiredVotes,
    getCooldownRemaining,
    applyCooldown,
    stopGame,
    activeVotes,
    voteCooldowns
};
