const MinecraftBotManager = require('../systems/minecraftBotManager');

const mcBotManager = new MinecraftBotManager();

function setup(client) {
    // Set status callback to send to Discord
    mcBotManager.setStatusCallback((message) => {
        // You can configure a specific channel for MC bot status
        console.log('[MC BOT]', message);
    });

    // ============ MC CREATE COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('mc-create', {
        name: 'mc-create',
        description: 'Create Minecraft AFK bot',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const config = client.config || require('../config');
            if (message.author.id !== config.OWNER_ID && !message.member.permissions.has('ManageGuild')) {
                return message.reply('❌ You need Manage Server permission!');
            }

            if (args.length < 4) {
                return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%mc-create <name> <password> <ip> <version> [port]`');
            }

            const [name, password, serverIp, version, port] = args;

            try {
                const result = await mcBotManager.createBot(name, password, serverIp, version, port ? parseInt(port) : undefined);
                await message.reply(result);
            } catch (err) {
                await message.reply(`❌ Error: ${err.message}`);
            }
        }
    });

    // ============ MC DELETE COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('mc-delete', {
        name: 'mc-delete',
        description: 'Delete Minecraft AFK bot',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const config = client.config || require('../config');
            if (message.author.id !== config.OWNER_ID && !message.member.permissions.has('ManageGuild')) {
                return message.reply('❌ You need Manage Server permission!');
            }

            if (args.length < 1) {
                return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%mc-delete <name>`');
            }

            try {
                const result = mcBotManager.deleteBot(args[0]);
                await message.reply(result);
            } catch (err) {
                await message.reply(`❌ Error: ${err.message}`);
            }
        }
    });

    // ============ MC START COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('mc-start', {
        name: 'mc-start',
        description: 'Start Minecraft AFK bot',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const config = client.config || require('../config');
            if (message.author.id !== config.OWNER_ID && !message.member.permissions.has('ManageGuild')) {
                return message.reply('❌ You need Manage Server permission!');
            }

            if (args.length < 1) {
                return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%mc-start <name>`');
            }

            try {
                const result = mcBotManager.startBot(args[0]);
                await message.reply(result);
            } catch (err) {
                await message.reply(`❌ Error: ${err.message}`);
            }
        }
    });

    // ============ MC STOP COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('mc-stop', {
        name: 'mc-stop',
        description: 'Stop Minecraft AFK bot',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const config = client.config || require('../config');
            if (message.author.id !== config.OWNER_ID && !message.member.permissions.has('ManageGuild')) {
                return message.reply('❌ You need Manage Server permission!');
            }

            if (args.length < 1) {
                return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%mc-stop <name>`');
            }

            try {
                const result = mcBotManager.stopBot(args[0]);
                await message.reply(result);
            } catch (err) {
                await message.reply(`❌ Error: ${err.message}`);
            }
        }
    });

    // ============ MC RESTART COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('mc-restart', {
        name: 'mc-restart',
        description: 'Restart Minecraft AFK bot',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const config = client.config || require('../config');
            if (message.author.id !== config.OWNER_ID && !message.member.permissions.has('ManageGuild')) {

}

///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//                      MODERATION COMMANDS                           //
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

const { PermissionFlagsBits, EmbedBuilder, ButtonStyle, ButtonBuilder, ComponentType, ActionRowBuilder, AttachmentBuilder, PermissionsBitField } = require('discord.js');
const { parseTime } = require('../utils/helpers');
const { createCanvas } = require('@napi-rs/canvas');
const DatabaseManager = require("../utils/DatabaseManager");
const dataManager = new DatabaseManager();

// Helper function to check if user is owner or has ManageGuild permission
function canManageServer(member, config) {
    return member.id === config.OWNER_ID || member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.ManageGuild);
}

function setup(client) {

    client.commands.set('membercount', {
        name: 'membercount',
        description: 'Shows server member statistics',
        aliases: ['members', 'serverinfo'],

        async execute(message, args, client) {
            try {
                const guild = message.guild;

                // FIX 1: Fetch all members to ensure cache is full (vô cùng quan trọng)
                const allMembers = await guild.members.fetch();

                const totalMembers = guild.memberCount; // Luôn chính xác từ API
                const humanMembers = allMembers.filter(member => !member.user.bot).size;
                const botMembers = allMembers.filter(member => member.user.bot).size;

                // FIX 2: Online status (Chỉ hoạt động nếu có GuildPresences intent)
                const onlineMembers = allMembers.filter(member =>
                    member.presence && ['online', 'idle', 'dnd'].includes(member.presence.status)
                ).size;

                const activityRate = ((onlineMembers / totalMembers) * 100).toFixed(1);

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle(`👥 ${guild.name} Statistics`)
                    .setColor(0x5865F2)
                    .setThumbnail(guild.iconURL({ dynamic: true }))
                    .addFields(
                        { name: '📊 Total', value: `\`${totalMembers}\``, inline: true },
                        { name: 'Humans', value: `\`${humanMembers}\``, inline: true },
                        { name: 'Bots', value: `\`${botMembers}\``, inline: true },
                        { name: 'Online', value: `\`${onlineMembers || '0'}\``, inline: true },
                        { name: 'Bot %', value: `\`${((botMembers / totalMembers) * 100).toFixed(1)}%\``, inline: true },
                        { name: '⚡ Activity Rate', value: `\`${activityRate}%\``, inline: true },

                    )
                    .setFooter({ text: `Created: ${guild.createdAt.toLocaleDateString()}` })
                    .setTimestamp();

                await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });

            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error:', error);
                await message.reply('❌ Failed to fetch statistics!');
            }
        }
    });

    // Aliases
    client.commands.set('members', client.commands.get('membercount'));
    client.commands.set('serverinfo', client.commands.get('membercount'));

    client.commands.set('clearbot', {
        name: 'clearbot',
        description: 'Delete bot messages',
        aliases: ['purgebot', 'deletebot', 'delbot'],
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            // 1. Permission Check
            if (!canManageServer(message.member, client.config)) {
                return message.reply("❌ You do not have permission!");
            }

            // 2. Usage Check: Ensure a number between 1-99 is provided
            const amount = parseInt(args[0]); // Using args[0] for command handlers

            if (!args[0] || isNaN(amount) || amount < 1 || amount > 99) {
                return message.reply("⚠️ **Usage:** `%clearbot <1-99>`\n*Example: %clearbot 20*");
            }

            try {
                // 3. DELETE AUTHOR MESSAGE: Immediately delete the %clearbot command
                // We use .catch() to prevent errors if the message was already deleted
                await message.delete().catch(() => null);

                // 4. Fetch the last 100 messages to scan for bots
                const fetched = await message.channel.messages.fetch({ limit: 100 });

                // 5. THE BOT CHECK: Filter messages where author.bot is true
                const botMessages = fetched.filter(m => m.author.bot).first(amount);

                if (botMessages.length === 0) {
                    const noMsg = await message.channel.send("🔍 No bot messages found.");
                    return setTimeout(() => noMsg.delete().catch(() => null), 3000);
                }

                // 6. Bulk Delete (true skips messages older than 14 days)
                const deleted = await message.channel.bulkDelete(botMessages, true);

                const status = await message.channel.send(`✅ Cleaned up **${deleted.size}** bot messages.`);

                // Self-destruct status message after 3 seconds
                setTimeout(() => status.delete().catch(() => null), 3000);

            } catch (error) {
                console.error("Clearbot Error:", error);
                // Don't use message.reply here as the original message is deleted
                message.channel.send("❌ Error: Messages may be older than 14 days");
            }
        }
    });

    // ============ CHANGE MEMBER NAME COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('setnick', {
        name: 'setnick',
        description: 'Change a member\'s nickname in the server',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            // 1. Owner Security Check
            if (!canManageServer(message.member, client.config)) {
                return message.reply("❌ You do not have permission to manage nicknames in this server!");
            }
            // 2. Identify the Target Member and the New Nickname
            const targetMember = message.mentions.members.first();
            const newNickname = args.slice(1).join(' ').trim() // Skip the mention, take the rest

            if (!targetMember) {
                return message.reply('❌ Please mention a member! Usage: `!setnick @user New Name`');
            }

            if (!newNickname) {
                return message.reply('❌ Please provide a new nickname!');
            }

            // 3. Permission & Hierarchy Check
            if (!message.guild.members.me.permissions.has('ManageNicknames')) {
                return message.reply('❌ I do not have the **Manage Nicknames** permission!');
            }

            if (targetMember.roles.highest.position >= message.guild.members.me.roles.highest.position) {
                return message.reply('❌ I cannot change the nickname of this user because their role is higher than or equal to mine!');
            }

            try {
                // 4. Perform the change
                const oldName = targetMember.displayName;
                await targetMember.setNickname(newNickname);

                return message.reply(`✅ Changed **${oldName}**'s nickname to **${newNickname}**`);
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(error);
                return message.reply('❌ An error occurred while trying to change the nickname.');
            }
        }
    });

    // ============ CLEAR COMMAND ============
    client.commands.set('clear', {
        name: 'clear',
        aliases: ['purge', 'delete'],
        description: 'Clear messages',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            if (!canManageServer(message.member, client.config)) {
                return message.reply('❌ You need Manage Server permission!');
            }

            if (args.length === 0) {
                return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%clear <amount>`');
            }

            const amount = parseInt(args[0]);
            if (isNaN(amount) || amount < 1 || amount > 99) {
                return message.reply('❌ Please provide a number between 1-99!');
            }

            try {
                const deleted = await message.channel.bulkDelete(amount + 1, true);
                const reply = await message.channel.send(`✅ Deleted **${deleted.size - 1}** messages!`);

                setTimeout(() => reply.delete().catch(() => { }), 5000);
            } catch (error) {
                await message.reply(`❌ Failed to delete messages: ${error.message}`);
            }
        }
    });

    client.commands.set('purge', client.commands.get('clear'));
    client.commands.set('delete', client.commands.get('clear'));

    // =============== LOCK & UNLOCK CHANNEL COMMAND ================
    client.commands.set('lock', {
        name: 'lock',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            if (!canManageServer(message.member, client.config)) return message.reply('❌ No permission!');
            const channel = message.mentions.channels.first() || message.channel;

            try {
                // CHỈ sửa đúng role @everyone, các role khác giữ nguyên 100%
                await channel.permissionOverwrites.edit(message.guild.roles.everyone, {
                    SendMessages: false
                });
                return message.reply(`🔒 **${channel.name}** has been locked for @everyone.`);
            } catch (error) {
                return message.reply('❌ Missing Permissions! Check my Role position.');
            }
        }
    });


    client.commands.set('unlock', {
        name: 'unlock',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            if (!canManageServer(message.member, client.config)) return message.reply('❌ No permission!');
            const channel = message.mentions.channels.first() || message.channel;

            try {
                // Trả quyền @everyone về mặc định, KHÔNG xóa các role khác
                await channel.permissionOverwrites.edit(message.guild.roles.everyone, {
                    SendMessages: null
                });
                return message.reply(`🔓 **${channel.name}** has been unlocked.`);
            } catch (error) {
                return message.reply('❌ Missing Permissions!');
            }
        }
    });


    client.commands.set('lockchannel', client.commands.get('lock'));
    client.commands.set('unlockchannel', client.commands.get('unlock'));
    client.commands.set('open', client.commands.get('unlock'));

    // ============== SNIPE MESSAGE ====================
    client.commands.set('snipe', {
        name: 'snipe',
        description: 'View the last 99 deleted messages in this channel',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            // 1. Permission Check
            if (!canManageServer(message.member, client.config)) {
                return message.reply('❌ You need **Manage Server** permission to use this command!');
            }

            // 2. Check if snipes map exists and has data for this channel
            if (!client.snipes) client.snipes = new Map();
            const snipes = client.snipes.get(message.channel.id);

            if (!snipes || snipes.length === 0) {
                return message.reply("❌ There are no recently deleted messages in this channel!");
            }

            let currentPage = 0;

            const generateEmbed = (page) => {
                const data = snipes[page];
                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setAuthor({ name: data.author.tag, iconURL: data.author.displayAvatarURL() })
                    .setDescription(data.content || "*[No text content / Attachment only]*")
                    .addFields({ name: 'Message', value: `\`${page + 1}\` of \`${snipes.length}\``, inline: true })
                    .setColor('#2f3136')
                    .setTimestamp(data.timestamp)
                    .setFooter({ text: `Requested by ${message.author.tag}`, iconURL: message.author.displayAvatarURL() });

                if (data.image) embed.setImage(data.image);
                return embed;
            };

            const getButtons = (page) => {
                return new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(
                    new ButtonBuilder()
                        .setCustomId('prev')
                        .setLabel('◀️ Previous')
                        .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary)
                        .setDisabled(page === 0),
                    new ButtonBuilder()
                        .setCustomId('next')
                        .setLabel('Next ▶️')
                        .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary)
                        .setDisabled(page === snipes.length - 1)
                );
            };

            const mainMessage = await message.reply({
                embeds: [generateEmbed(currentPage)],
                components: [getButtons(currentPage)]
            });

            const collector = mainMessage.createMessageComponentCollector({
                componentType: ComponentType.Button,
                time: 60000
            });

            collector.on('collect', async (interaction) => {
                if (interaction.user.id !== message.author.id) {
                    return interaction.reply({
                        content: "❌ Only the person who used the command can switch pages.",
                        ephemeral: true
                    });
                }

                if (interaction.customId === 'prev') currentPage--;
                if (interaction.customId === 'next') currentPage++;

                await interaction.update({
                    embeds: [generateEmbed(currentPage)],
                    components: [getButtons(currentPage)]
                }).catch(() => { });
            });

            collector.on('end', () => {
                const disabledRow = new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(
                    new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId('p').setLabel('◀️').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary).setDisabled(true),
                    new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId('n').setLabel('▶️').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary).setDisabled(true)
                );
                mainMessage.edit({ components: [disabledRow] }).catch(() => { });
            });
        }
    });


    client.commands.set('serverstats', {
        name: 'serverstats',
        aliases: ['growth', 'analytics'],
        description: 'Show server growth statistics',

        async execute(message) {
            try {
                if (!message.guild) {
                    return message.reply("❌ Server only command!");
                }

                // ===== SAFE HELPERS =====
                const safeText = (v) => (v === undefined || v === null) ? "" : String(v);
                const safeNum = (v) => {
                    const n = Number(v);
                    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
                };

                const drawText = (ctx, text, x, y) => {
                    ctx.fillText(safeText(text), safeNum(x), safeNum(y));
                };

                const roundRectPath = (ctx, x, y, w, h, r) => {
                    const radius = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2);
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.moveTo(x + radius, y);
                    ctx.lineTo(x + w - radius, y);
                    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + radius);
                    ctx.lineTo(x + w, y + h - radius);
                    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y + h, x + w - radius, y + h);
                    ctx.lineTo(x + radius, y + h);
                    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y + h, x, y + h - radius);
                    ctx.lineTo(x, y + radius);
                    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y, x + radius, y);
                    ctx.closePath();
                };

                // ===== DATA =====
                const raw = dataManager.getRecentAnalytics(message.guild.id, 7) || [];

                const rows = raw
                    .map(r => ({
                        joined: safeNum(r?.joined),
                        left: safeNum(r?.left),
                        total_at_day: safeNum(r?.total_at_day),
                        date: typeof r?.date === "string" ? r.date : null
                    }))
                    .filter(r => r);

                if (rows.length < 2) {
                    return message.reply("📊 Need at least 2 days of data!");
                }

                // ===== CANVAS =====
                const canvas = createCanvas(760, 480);
                const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

                const paddingLeft = 70;
                const paddingRight = 30;
                const paddingTop = 70;
                const paddingBottom = 70;

                const chartWidth = canvas.width - paddingLeft - paddingRight;
                const chartHeight = canvas.height - paddingTop - paddingBottom;

                // ===== BACKGROUND =====
                const bg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, canvas.height);
                bg.addColorStop(0, '#101820');
                bg.addColorStop(1, '#1b2430');
                ctx.fillStyle = bg;
                ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

                // Subtle panel
                ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.03)';
                roundRectPath(ctx, 18, 18, canvas.width - 36, canvas.height - 36, 18);
                ctx.fill();

                // ===== TITLE =====
                ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
                ctx.font = 'bold 24px sans-serif';
                drawText(ctx, `${message.guild.name} Growth Analytics`, paddingLeft, 38);

                ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.70)';
                ctx.font = '13px sans-serif';
                drawText(ctx, `Last ${rows.length} days • Join vs Leave`, paddingLeft, 58);

                // ===== SCALE =====
                const maxValue = Math.max(
                    ...rows.map(r => Math.max(r.joined, r.left)),
                    5
                );

                const yTicks = 5;
                const stepY = chartHeight / yTicks;

                // ===== GRID + Y AXIS LABELS =====
                ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)';
                ctx.lineWidth = 1;
                ctx.font = '12px sans-serif';
                ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.65)';

                for (let i = 0; i <= yTicks; i++) {
                    const y = paddingTop + (chartHeight - (i * stepY));
                    const val = Math.round((maxValue / yTicks) * i);

                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.moveTo(paddingLeft, y);
                    ctx.lineTo(canvas.width - paddingRight, y);
                    ctx.stroke();

                    drawText(ctx, val, 22, y + 4);
                }

                // Y-axis line
                ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)';
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.moveTo(paddingLeft, paddingTop);
                ctx.lineTo(paddingLeft, paddingTop + chartHeight);
                ctx.stroke();

                // ===== BAR LAYOUT =====
                const groupCount = rows.length;
                const groupGap = 16;
                const plotWidth = chartWidth;
                const groupWidth = (plotWidth - groupGap * (groupCount - 1)) / groupCount;
                const barGap = 8;
                const barWidth = Math.max(8, (groupWidth - barGap) / 2);

                const chartBottom = paddingTop + chartHeight;
                const scaleY = (value) => {
                    const safe = safeNum(value);
                    return chartBottom - ((safe / maxValue) * (chartHeight - 20));
                };

                // ===== LEGEND =====
                const legendY = 26;

                const legendItem = (x, color, label) => {
                    ctx.fillStyle = color;
                    ctx.beginPath();
                    ctx.arc(x, legendY, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                    ctx.fill();

                    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.90)';
                    ctx.font = '13px sans-serif';
                    drawText(ctx, label, x + 10, legendY + 4);
                };

                legendItem(paddingLeft + 0, '#43b581', 'Join');
                legendItem(paddingLeft + 110, '#f04747', 'Leave');

                // ===== BAR DRAWER =====
                const drawBar = (x, y, w, h, color) => {
                    const radius = 8;
                    const topY = Math.min(y, y + h);
                    const height = Math.abs(h);

                    const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, topY, 0, topY + height);
                    gradient.addColorStop(0, color);
                    gradient.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.20)');

                    ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
                    ctx.shadowColor = color;
                    ctx.shadowBlur = 10;

                    roundRectPath(ctx, x, topY, w, height, radius);
                    ctx.fill();

                    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                };

                // ===== DRAW BARS =====
                rows.forEach((row, i) => {
                    const groupX = paddingLeft + i * (groupWidth + groupGap);
                    const bar1X = groupX;
                    const bar2X = groupX + barWidth + barGap;

                    const joinY = scaleY(row.joined);
                    const leaveY = scaleY(row.left);

                    const joinH = chartBottom - joinY;
                    const leaveH = chartBottom - leaveY;

                    drawBar(bar1X, joinY, barWidth, joinH, '#43b581');
                    drawBar(bar2X, leaveY, barWidth, leaveH, '#f04747');

                    // Value labels on top
                    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
                    ctx.font = 'bold 12px sans-serif';
                    drawText(ctx, row.joined, bar1X + (barWidth / 2) - 6, joinY - 8);
                    drawText(ctx, row.left, bar2X + (barWidth / 2) - 6, leaveY - 8);

                    // Date label
                    let dateStr = `D${i + 1}`;
                    if (row.date) {
                        const parts = row.date.split('-');
                        if (parts.length >= 3) {
                            dateStr = `${parts[2]}/${parts[1]}`;
                        }
                    }

                    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.68)';
                    ctx.font = '12px sans-serif';
                    drawText(ctx, dateStr, groupX + (groupWidth / 2) - 16, chartBottom + 24);
                });

                // ===== FOOTER SUMMARY =====
                const last = rows[rows.length - 1] || {};
                const totalJoined = rows.reduce((sum, r) => sum + safeNum(r.joined), 0);
                const totalLeft = rows.reduce((sum, r) => sum + safeNum(r.left), 0);

                ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)';
                roundRectPath(ctx, paddingLeft, canvas.height - 48, canvas.width - paddingLeft - paddingRight, 34, 12);
                ctx.fill();

                ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.92)';
                ctx.font = '13px sans-serif';
                drawText(ctx, `Total joins: ${totalJoined}`, paddingLeft + 14, canvas.height - 26);
                drawText(ctx, `Total leaves: ${totalLeft}`, paddingLeft + 170, canvas.height - 26);
                drawText(ctx, `Today: +${safeNum(last.joined)} / -${safeNum(last.left)}`, paddingLeft + 330, canvas.height - 26);

                // ===== EXPORT =====
                const attachment = new AttachmentBuilder(
                    canvas.toBuffer('image/png'),
                    { name: 'growth-bar-chart.png' }
                );

                const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle(`📊 ${safeText(message.guild.name)} Growth Stats`)
                    .setDescription('Bar chart: **green = joins**, **red = leaves**')
                    .addFields(
                        { name: 'Today Joins', value: `\`${safeNum(last.joined)}\``, inline: true },
                        { name: 'Today Leaves', value: `\`${safeNum(last.left)}\``, inline: true },
                        { name: 'Current Total', value: `\`${safeNum(message.guild.memberCount)}\``, inline: true }
                    )
                    .setColor('#5865F2')
                    .setImage('attachment://growth-bar-chart.png')
                    .setFooter({ text: 'Data updates from join/leave history' })
                    .setTimestamp();

                return message.reply({
                    embeds: [embed],
                    files: [attachment]
                });

            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Growth error:", err);
                return message.reply("❌ Error generating chart!");
            }
        }
    });

    // Aliases
    client.commands.set('growth', client.commands.get('serverstats'));
    client.commands.set('analytics', client.commands.get('serverstats'));

    client.commands.set('createrole', {
        name: 'createrole',
        aliases: ['cr', 'newrole'],
        description: 'Create a role with Name, Color (Hex), and Permission (Name or Integer)',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            // 1. Permission Check
            if (!message.member.permissions.has(PermissionsBitField.Flags.ManageRoles)) {
                return message.reply('❌ You need **Manage Roles** permission!');
            }

            // 2. Validate Args: %createrole <name> <color> <permission>
            if (args.length < 1) {
                return message.reply('⚠️ Usage: `%createrole <name> [color_hex] [permission]`\nExample: `%createrole VIP #f1c40f 8` (8 = Admin)');
            }

            const roleName = args[0];
            const roleColor = args[1] || 'Default';
            const rawPerm = args[2] || '0'; // Mặc định là 0 (Không quyền đặc biệt)

            try {
                let finalPerms;

                // Kiểm tra nếu người dùng nhập số (Integer) hay chữ (String)
                if (!isNaN(rawPerm)) {
                    finalPerms = BigInt(rawPerm); // Chuyển chuỗi số thành BigInt
                } else {
                    finalPerms = PermissionsBitField.Flags[rawPerm];
                    if (!finalPerms && rawPerm !== '0') throw new Error('INVALID_PERM');
                }

                // 3. Tạo Role
                const newRole = await message.guild.roles.create({
                    name: roleName,
                    color: roleColor,
                    permissions: [finalPerms],
                    reason: `Created by ${message.author.tag}`
                });

                const successEmbed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('✅ Role Created')
                    .setColor(newRole.color || '#ffffff')
                    .addFields(
                        { name: 'Name', value: `\`${newRole.name}\``, inline: true },
                        { name: 'Color', value: `\`${roleColor}\``, inline: true },
                        { name: 'Perm Bitfield', value: `\`${finalPerms.toString()}\``, inline: true }
                    )
                    .setTimestamp();

                return message.reply({ embeds: [successEmbed] });

            } catch (error) {
                // 4. HIỂN THỊ GỢI Ý KHI SAI PERMISSION
                const helpEmbed = new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setTitle('❌ Invalid Permission!')
                    .setDescription('Please use a valid **Permission Name** or **Integer (Bitfield)**.')
                    .setColor('#ff4742')
                    .addFields(
                        { name: 'Common Integers', value: '`8`: Administrator\n`32`: Manage Server\n`2`: Kick Members\n`4`: Ban Members\n`2048`: Send Messages', inline: true },
                        { name: 'Common Names', value: '`Administrator` (Full access)\n`ManageMessages` (Clear chat)\n`MentionEveryone` (Ping all)\n`ManageChannels` (Edit rooms)', inline: true }
                    )
                    .setFooter({ text: 'Tip: You can use 0 for no special permissions.' });

                return message.reply({ embeds: [helpEmbed] });
            }
        }
    });



    console.log('✅ Moderation commands loaded');
}

module.exports = { setup };

//======================================================================
//                    PERMISSION COMMANDS
//======================================================================

const PermissionManager = require('../systems/permissionManager');
const { EmbedBuilder } = require('discord.js');

const permManager = new PermissionManager();

function setup(client) {
    // ============ ADD WHITELIST ROLE ============
    client.commands.set('whitelist-add', {
        name: 'whitelist-add',
        description: 'Add role to admin whitelist',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const config = client.config || require('../config');
            if (message.author.id !== config.OWNER_ID && !message.member.permissions.has('ManageGuild')) {
                return message.reply('❌ You need Manage Server permission!');
            }

            const role = message.mentions.roles.first();
            if (!role) {
                return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%whitelist-add @role`');
            }

            permManager.addWhitelistedRole(message.guild.id, role.id);
            await message.reply(`✅ Added ${role} to admin whitelist!`);
        }
    });

    // ============ REMOVE WHITELIST ROLE ============
    client.commands.set('whitelist-remove', {
        name: 'whitelist-remove',
        description: 'Remove role from admin whitelist',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const config = client.config || require('../config');
            if (message.author.id !== config.OWNER_ID && !message.member.permissions.has('ManageGuild')) {
                return message.reply('❌ You need Manage Server permission!');
            }

            const role = message.mentions.roles.first();
            if (!role) {
                return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%whitelist-remove @role`');
            }

            permManager.removeWhitelistedRole(message.guild.id, role.id);
            await message.reply(`✅ Removed ${role} from admin whitelist!`);
        }
    });

    // ============ LIST WHITELIST ROLES ============
    client.commands.set('whitelist-list', {
        name: 'whitelist-list',
        aliases: ['whitelist'],
        description: 'List all whitelisted roles',
        async execute(message, args, client) {
            const roles = await permManager.listWhitelistedRoles(message.guild);

            if (roles.length === 0) {
                return message.reply('📭 No whitelisted roles configured!\n\nBy default, these users can use admin commands:\n- Bot Owner\n- Users with Manage Server permission');
            }

            const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                .setTitle('🔐 Admin Whitelist Roles')
                .setDescription('Users with these roles can use admin commands:')
                .setColor(0x0099FF)
                .addFields({ 
                    name: 'Default Permissions', 
                    value: '✅ Bot Owner\n✅ Manage Server Permission', 
                    inline: false 
                });

            let rolesText = '';
            roles.forEach(role => {
                rolesText += `<@&${role.id}> (${role.name})\n`;
            });

            if (rolesText) {
                embed.addFields({ name: 'Whitelisted Roles', value: rolesText, inline: false });
            }

            embed.setFooter({ text: 'Use %whitelist-add @role to add more roles' });

            await message.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
        }
    });

    client.commands.set('whitelist', client.commands.get('whitelist-list'));

    console.log('✅ Permission commands loaded');
}

module.exports = { setup, permManager };

//======================================================================
//                      POLL COMMANDS
//======================================================================

const { EmbedBuilder } = require('discord.js');
const { activePolls, parseTime, closePollInternal } = require('../systems/pollSystem');

module.exports = {
  name: 'poll',
  category: 'Utility',
  description: 'Create a poll',
  aliases: [],
  
  async execute(message, args, client) {
    if (args.length === 0) {
      return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%poll [time] <question>` or `%poll [time] multiple <question> | <option1> | <option2> | ...`');
    }
    
    let duration = null;
    let startIdx = 0;
    
    // Check if first arg is time
    const firstArgTime = parseTime(args[0]);
    if (firstArgTime) {
      duration = firstArgTime;
      startIdx = 1;
    }
    
    const content = args.slice(startIdx).join(' ');
    
    if (content.toLowerCase().startsWith('multiple')) {
      // Multiple choice poll
      const parts = content.split('|').map(p => p.trim());
      const question = parts[0].replace(/^multiple/i, '').trim();
      const options = parts.slice(1);
      
      if (options.length < 2) {
        return message.reply('❌ Need at least 2 options! Format: `%poll multiple <question> | 🎮 Option1 | 🎵 Option2`');
      }
      
      const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
        .setTitle('Multiple-Choice Poll')
        .setDescription(question)
        .setColor(0x0099FF)
        .setFooter({ text: `Poll by ${message.author.username}` });
      
      const msg = await message.channel.send({ embeds: [embed] });
      
      const votes = {};
      
      for (const opt of options) {
        try {
          const emoji = opt.split(' ')[0];
          await msg.react(emoji);
          votes[emoji] = [];
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Failed to add reaction:', err);
        }
      }
      
      activePolls.set(msg.id, {
        multiple: true,
        votes,
        closed: false
      });
      
      if (duration) {
        setTimeout(async () => {
          await closePollInternal(message.channel, msg.id);
        }, duration);
      }
    } else {
      // Yes/No poll
      const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
        .setTitle('Poll')
        .setDescription(content)
        .setColor(0x0099FF)
        .setFooter({ text: `Poll by ${message.author.username}` });
      
      const msg = await message.channel.send({ embeds: [embed] });
      await msg.react('👍');
      await msg.react('👎');
      
      activePolls.set(msg.id, {
        multiple: false,
        votes: { '👍': [], '👎': [] },
        closed: false
      });
      
      if (duration) {
        setTimeout(async () => {
          await closePollInternal(message.channel, msg.id);
        }, duration);
      }
    }
  }
};

// Close poll command
const closepoll = {
  name: 'closepoll',
  category: 'Utility',
  description: 'Close a poll',
  
  async execute(message, args, client) {
    if (args.length === 0) {
      return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%closepoll <message_id>`');
    }
    
    const messageId = args[0];
    
    if (!activePolls.has(messageId)) {
      return message.reply('❌ Poll not found!');
    }
    
    await closePollInternal(message.channel, messageId);
    await message.react('✅');
  }
};

// Results command
const results = {
  name: 'results',
  category: 'Utility',
  description: 'Show poll results',
  
  async execute(message, args, client) {
    if (args.length === 0) {
      return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%results <message_id>`');
    }
    
    const messageId = args[0];
    
    if (!activePolls.has(messageId)) {
      return message.reply('❌ Poll not found!');
    }
    
    const poll = activePolls.get(messageId);
    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
      .setTitle('Poll Results')
      .setColor(0x00FF00);
    
    let totalVotes = 0;
    for (const voters of Object.values(poll.votes)) {
      totalVotes += voters.length;
    }
    
    if (totalVotes === 0) totalVotes = 1;
    
    for (const [emoji, voters] of Object.entries(poll.votes)) {
      const percent = (voters.length / totalVotes) * 100;
      embed.addFields({
        name: emoji,
        value: `${voters.length} votes (${percent.toFixed(1)}%)`,
        inline: false
      });
    }
    
    await message.channel.send({ embeds: [embed] });
  }
};

function setup(client) {
  client.commands.set('poll', module.exports);
  client.commands.set('closepoll', closepoll);
  client.commands.set('results', results);
  
  console.log('✅ Poll commands registered');
}

module.exports.closepoll = closepoll;
module.exports.results = results;
module.exports.setup = setup;



--- FILE: C:\Users\ADMIN\Documents\Nam\Project\Programmer being\Discord\src\commands\reminder.js ---
//======================================================================
//                      REMINDER COMMANDS
//======================================================================

const { SlashCommandBuilder } = require('discord.js');
const { parseTime, loadReminders, saveReminders, sendReminder, activeReminders } = require('../systems/reminderSystem');

module.exports = {
  name: 'remindme',
  category: 'Utility',
  description: 'Set a reminder',
  aliases: ['remind', 'reminder'],
  
  data: new SlashCommandBuilder()
    .setName('remindme')
    .setDescription('Set a reminder')
    .addStringOption(opt => opt
      .setName('time')
      .setDescription('Time (5m, 2h, 1d)')
      .setRequired(true)
    )
    .addStringOption(opt => opt
      .setName('reminder')
      .setDescription('Reminder message')
      .setRequired(true)
    ),
  
  async execute(interaction, client, config) {
    const time = interaction.options.getString('time');
    const reminder = interaction.options.getString('reminder');
    
    const ms = parseTime(time);
    
    if (!ms) {
      return interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Invalid format! Use: `10m`, `2h`, or `1d`', ephemeral: true });
    }
    
    if (ms > 604800000) { // 7 days
      return interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Max 7 days!', ephemeral: true });
    }
    
    if (ms < 60000) { // 1 minute
      return interaction.reply({ content: '❌ Min 1 minute!', ephemeral: true });
    }
    
    // Save reminder
    const reminders = loadReminders(interaction.guild.id);
    
    const reminderId = `${interaction.user.id}_${Date.now()}`;
    const remindAt = new Date(Date.now() + ms);
    
    reminders[reminderId] = {
      user_id: interaction.user.id,
      guild_id: interaction.guild.id,
      channel_id: interaction.channel.id,
      reminder,
      remind_at: remindAt.toISOString(),
      created_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    
    saveReminders(interaction.guild.id, reminders);
    
    // Schedule reminder
    const timestamp = Math.floor(remindAt.getTime() / 1000);
    await interaction.reply({ content: `⏰ I'll remind you <t:${timestamp}:R>!`, ephemeral: true });
    
    // Create timeout
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      sendReminder(client, reminderId, interaction.guild.id);
    }, ms);
    
    activeReminders.set(reminderId, timeout);
  },
  
  async run(message, args, client, config) {
    if (args.length < 2) {
      return message.reply('❌ Usage: `%remindme <time> <reminder>`\nExample: `%remindme 1h Buy groceries`');
    }
    
    const time = args[0];
    const reminder = args.slice(1).join(' ');
    
    const ms = parseTime(time);
    
    if (!ms) {
      return message.reply('❌ Invalid format! Use: `10m`, `2h`, or `1d`');
    }
    
    if (ms > 604800000) {
      return message.reply('❌ Max 7 days!');
    }
    
    if (ms < 60000) {
      return message.reply('❌ Min 1 minute!');
    }
    
    // Save reminder
    const reminders = loadReminders(message.guild.id);
    
    const reminderId = `${message.author.id}_${Date.now()}`;
    const remindAt = new Date(Date.now() + ms);
    
    reminders[reminderId] = {
      user_id: message.author.id,
      guild_id: message.guild.id,
      channel_id: message.channel.id,
      reminder,
      remind_at: remindAt.toISOString(),
      created_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    
    saveReminders(message.guild.id, reminders);
    
    // Schedule reminder
    const timestamp = Math.floor(remindAt.getTime() / 1000);
    await message.reply(`⏰ I'll remind you <t:${timestamp}:R>!`);
    
    // Create timeout
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      sendReminder(client, reminderId, message.guild.id);
    }, ms);
    
    activeReminders.set(reminderId, timeout);
  }
};

const { EmbedBuilder, SlashCommandBuilder, AttachmentBuilder } = require('discord.js');
const DatabaseManager = require('../utils/DatabaseManager');

const dbManager = new DatabaseManager();

// ============ %XP COMMAND ============
const xp = {
    name: 'xp',
    category: 'Leveling',
    description: 'Check your XP and level',
    aliases: ['level', 'rank', 'ranks'],

    data: new SlashCommandBuilder()
        .setName('xp')
        .setDescription('Check XP and level')
        .addUserOption(opt => opt
            .setName('user')
            .setDescription('Check another user\'s XP')
            .setRequired(false)
        ),

    async execute(interaction, client, config) {
        const targetUser = interaction.options.getUser('user') || interaction.user;
        await showXPCard(interaction, client, targetUser);
    },

    async run(message, args, client, config) {
        let targetUser = message.author;

        if (message.mentions.users.size > 0) {
            targetUser = message.mentions.users.first();
        }

        await showXPCard(message, client, targetUser);
    }
};

async function showXPCard(ctx, client, user) {
    const serverId = ctx.guild.id;
    const userId = user.id;

    const xpData = dbManager.getUserXP(serverId, userId);
    const rank = dbManager.getServerRank(serverId, userId);
    const xpForNext = dbManager.getXPForLevel(xpData.level + 1);

    // Calculate progress percentage
    const progressPercent = Math.floor((xpData.xp / xpForNext) * 100);
    const barLength = 20;
    const filledBars = Math.floor((progressPercent / 100) * barLength);
    const progressBar = '█'.repeat(filledBars) + '░'.repeat(barLength - filledBars);

    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
        .setTitle(`📈 ${user.username}'s Level Card`)
        .setColor(xpData.profile_color)
        .setThumbnail(user.displayAvatarURL({ size: 256 }))
        .addFields(
            { name: 'Level', value: `**${xpData.level}** ⭐`, inline: true },
            { name: 'Server Rank', value: `**#${rank}**`, inline: true },
            { name: '\u200b', value: '\u200b', inline: true },
            { name: 'Current XP', value: `${xpData.xp.toLocaleString()} / ${xpForNext.toLocaleString()}`, inline: false },
            { name: 'Progress', value: `\`[${progressBar}]\` ${progressPercent}%`, inline: false },
            { name: 'Total XP Earned', value: xpData.total_xp.toLocaleString(), inline: true }
        )
        .setFooter({ text: 'Earn XP by sending messages!' })
        .setTimestamp();

    // Check for active boosts
    if (dbManager.hasActiveBoost(serverId, userId, 'xp_boost_1h') || dbManager.hasActiveBoost(serverId, userId, 'xp_boost_24h')) {
        embed.addFields({ name: '⚡ Active Boost', value: '2x XP Boost Active!', inline: false });
    }

    if (ctx.reply) {
        await ctx.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
    } else {
        await ctx.channel.send({ embeds: [embed] });
    }
}

// ============ %XPTOP COMMAND ============

async function showXPLeaderboard(ctx, client, isGlobal) {
    const serverId = ctx.guild.id;

    let topUsers;
    let title;
    let color;

    if (isGlobal) {
        topUsers = dbManager.getGlobalXPLeaderboard(10);
        title = '🌎 Global Level Leaderboard';
        color = 0xFFD700;
    } else {
        topUsers = dbManager.getXPLeaderboard(serverId, 10);
        title = `📈 ${ctx.guild.name} - Level Leaderboard`;
        color = 0x5865F2;
    }

    if (topUsers.length === 0) {
        const noDataMsg = '💭 No one has earned XP yet!';
        if (ctx.reply) {
            return ctx.reply(noDataMsg);
        } else {
            return ctx.channel.send(noDataMsg);
        }
    }

    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
        .setTitle(title)
        .setColor(color)
        .setThumbnail(ctx.guild.iconURL())
        .setTimestamp();

    let description = '';

    for (let i = 0; i < topUsers.length; i++) {
        const userData = topUsers[i];
        const user = await client.users.fetch(userData.user_id).catch(() => null);
        const name = user ? user.tag : 'Unknown User';

        const medal = i === 0 ? '🥇' : i === 1 ? '🥈' : i === 2 ? '🥉' : `#${i + 1}`;

        if (isGlobal) {
            description += `${medal} **${name}**\n` +
                `Level: **${userData.max_level}** | Total XP: **${userData.global_xp.toLocaleString()}**\n\n`;
        } else {
            description += `${medal} **${name}**\n` +
                `Level: **${userData.level}** | Total XP: **${userData.total_xp.toLocaleString()}**\n\n`;
        }
    }

    embed.setDescription(description);

    if (ctx.reply) {
        await ctx.reply({ embeds: [embed] });
    } else {
        await ctx.channel.send({ embeds: [embed] });
    }
}

}

//======================================================================
//                     AI SYSTEM (GOOGLE GEMINI)
//======================================================================

const { GoogleGenerativeAI } = require('@google/generative-ai');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

let genAI = null;
let prompts = {};

// Load prompts from JSON file
function loadPrompts() {
  try {
    const propertiesPath = path.join(__dirname, 'aiconfig', 'prompt.json');
    const content = fs.readFileSync(propertiesPath, 'utf8');
    
    // Parse JSON file
    prompts = JSON.parse(content);
    
    console.log('✅ Prompts loaded from JSON file');
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn('⚠️  Could not load prompts from JSON file:', error.message);
  }
}

function setup(client, config) {
  loadPrompts();
  
  if (config.GOOGLE_API_KEY) {
    genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(config.GOOGLE_API_KEY);
    console.log('✅ AI system loaded (Google Gemini)');
  } else {
    console.warn('⚠️ AI system skipped (no GOOGLE_API_KEY in config)');
  }
}

async function generateResponse(prompt, systemInstruction, maxTokens = 2000) {
  if (!genAI) {
    throw new Error('AI system not initialized. Add GOOGLE_API_KEY to your .env file');
  }
  
  try {
    const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
      model: 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite',
      systemInstruction
    });
    
    const result = await model.generateContent(prompt);
    const response = await result.response;
    let text = response.text();
    
    return text;
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.message && error.message.includes('503')) {
      throw new Error('**Server Full!** Google is busy. Try again in 10s.');
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

module.exports = {
  setup,
  generateResponse,
  getAI: () => genAI,
  getPrompts: () => prompts
};



--- FILE: C:\Users\ADMIN\Documents\Nam\Project\Programmer being\Discord\src\systems\boardRenderer.js ---
//======================================================================
//                    CHESS BOARD RENDERER (Canvas Image)
//======================================================================

const { createCanvas, loadImage } = require('@napi-rs/canvas');
const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');

const SIZE = 800;
const SQ = SIZE / 8;

// Cache for loaded SVG images
const pieceImages = {};

// Preload all chess piece images
async function preloadPieceImages() {
    const pieces = ['wK', 'wQ', 'wR', 'wB', 'wN', 'wP', 'bK', 'bQ', 'bR', 'bB', 'bN', 'bP'];
    const assetsPath = path.join(__dirname, '../assets/chess-pieces');

    for (const piece of pieces) {
        const svgPath = path.join(assetsPath, `${piece}.svg`);
        try {
            if (fs.existsSync(svgPath)) {
                // Read SVG and convert to data URL for loading
                const svgContent = fs.readFileSync(svgPath, 'utf-8');
                const dataUrl = `data:image/svg+xml;base64,${Buffer.from(svgContent).toString('base64')}`;
                pieceImages[piece] = await loadImage(dataUrl);
            }
        } catch (err) {
            console.error(`Failed to load ${piece}.svg:`, err.message);
        }
    }
    console.log('✅ Chess piece images loaded');
}

// Map FEN characters to image names
function getPieceImageKey(fenChar) {
    const isWhite = fenChar === fenChar.toUpperCase();
    const color = isWhite ? 'w' : 'b';
    const piece = fenChar.toUpperCase();
    return `${color}${piece}`;
}

// Flip the board (rotate 180 degrees)
async function renderBoard(fen, lastMove, flipped = false) {
    // Ensure images are loaded
    if (Object.keys(pieceImages).length === 0) {
        await preloadPieceImages();
    }

    const canvas = createCanvas(SIZE, SIZE);
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

    // Draw checkered board
    for (let y = 0; y < 8; y++) {
        for (let x = 0; x < 8; x++) {
            const displayY = flipped ? (7 - y) : y;
            const displayX = flipped ? (7 - x) : x;
            ctx.fillStyle = (x + y) % 2 === 0 ? '#f0d9b5' : '#b58863';
            ctx.fillRect(displayX * SQ, displayY * SQ, SQ, SQ);
        }
    }

    // Highlight last move
    if (lastMove) {
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 0, 0.4)';

        // From square
        const fromFile = lastMove.from.charCodeAt(0) - 97;
        const fromRank = 8 - parseInt(lastMove.from[1]);
        const displayFromX = flipped ? (7 - fromFile) : fromFile;
        const displayFromY = flipped ? (7 - fromRank) : fromRank;
        ctx.fillRect(displayFromX * SQ, displayFromY * SQ, SQ, SQ);

        // To square
        const toFile = lastMove.to.charCodeAt(0) - 97;
        const toRank = 8 - parseInt(lastMove.to[1]);
        const displayToX = flipped ? (7 - toFile) : toFile;
        const displayToY = flipped ? (7 - toRank) : toRank;
        ctx.fillRect(displayToX * SQ, displayToY * SQ, SQ, SQ);
    }

    // Draw pieces from FEN
    const rows = fen.split(' ')[0].split('/');
    for (let y = 0; y < 8; y++) {
        let x = 0;
        for (const c of rows[y]) {
            if (isNaN(c)) {
                // Calculate display position based on flip
                const displayY = flipped ? (7 - y) : y;
                const displayX = flipped ? (7 - x) : x;

                // Draw the piece using SVG image
                const pieceKey = getPieceImageKey(c);
                const image = pieceImages[pieceKey];

                if (image) {
                    // Draw image with padding for better appearance
                    const padding = SQ * 0.1;
                    ctx.drawImage(image,
                        displayX * SQ + padding,
                        displayY * SQ + padding,
                        SQ - padding * 2,
                        SQ - padding * 2
                    );
                } else {
                    // Fallback: draw text if image not loaded
                    ctx.fillStyle = c === c.toUpperCase() ? '#ffffff' : '#000000';
                    ctx.font = 'bold 60px Arial';
                    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
                    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
                    ctx.fillText(c, displayX * SQ + SQ / 2, displayY * SQ + SQ / 2);
                }
                x++;
            } else {
                x += Number(c);
            }
        }
    }

    // Add coordinates (adjusted for flip)
    ctx.fillStyle = '#000000';
    ctx.font = 'bold 24px Arial';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';

    // Files (a-h) at bottom
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        const letter = String.fromCharCode(97 + (flipped ? (7 - i) : i));
        ctx.fillText(letter, i * SQ + SQ / 2, SIZE - 15);
    }

    // Ranks (8-1 from top to bottom)
    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        const rank = flipped ? (i + 1) : (8 - i);
        ctx.fillText(rank.toString(), 10, i * SQ + 30);
    }

    return canvas.toBuffer('image/png');
}

module.exports = { renderBoard, preloadPieceImages };



--- FILE: C:\Users\ADMIN\Documents\Nam\Project\Programmer being\Discord\src\systems\chessColors.js ---
//======================================================================
//                    CHESS COLOR ASSIGNMENT SYSTEM
//======================================================================

const DataManager = require('../utils/dataManager');
const dataManager = new DataManager();

// Track last colors played by each user
function getPlayerColorHistory(guildId, userId) {
    const colorData = dataManager.loadFile(guildId, 'chess_colors.json');
    
    if (!colorData[userId]) {
        colorData[userId] = {
            lastColors: [], // Last 3 games colors
            totalWhite: 0,
            totalBlack: 0
        };
        dataManager.saveFile(guildId, 'chess_colors.json', colorData);
    }
    
    return colorData[userId];
}

// Update color history after game
function updateColorHistory(guildId, userId, color) {
    const colorData = dataManager.loadFile(guildId, 'chess_colors.json');
    
    if (!colorData[userId]) {
        colorData[userId] = {
            lastColors: [],
            totalWhite: 0,
            totalBlack: 0
        };
    }
    
    // Add to last colors (keep only last 3)
    colorData[userId].lastColors.push(color);
    if (colorData[userId].lastColors.length > 3) {
        colorData[userId].lastColors.shift();
    }
    
    // Update totals
    if (color === 'white') {
        colorData[userId].totalWhite++;
    } else {
        colorData[userId].totalBlack++;
    }
    
    dataManager.saveFile(guildId, 'chess_colors.json', colorData);
}

// Assign colors fairly
function assignColors(guildId, player1Id, player2Id) {
    const p1History = getPlayerColorHistory(guildId, player1Id);
    const p2History = getPlayerColorHistory(guildId, player2Id);
    
    // Check if either player has streak
    const p1LastTwo = p1History.lastColors.slice(-2);
    const p2LastTwo = p2History.lastColors.slice(-2);
    
    const p1HasWhiteStreak = p1LastTwo.length === 2 && p1LastTwo.every(c => c === 'white');
    const p1HasBlackStreak = p1LastTwo.length === 2 && p1LastTwo.every(c => c === 'black');
    const p2HasWhiteStreak = p2LastTwo.length === 2 && p2LastTwo.every(c => c === 'white');
    const p2HasBlackStreak = p2LastTwo.length === 2 && p2LastTwo.every(c => c === 'black');
    
    let whitePlayer, blackPlayer;
    
    // Priority 1: If someone has 2 black games in a row, give them white
    if (p1HasBlackStreak && !p2HasBlackStreak) {
        whitePlayer = player1Id;
        blackPlayer = player2Id;
    } else if (p2HasBlackStreak && !p1HasBlackStreak) {
        whitePlayer = player2Id;
        blackPlayer = player1Id;
    }
    // Priority 2: If someone has 2 white games in a row, give them black
    else if (p1HasWhiteStreak && !p2HasWhiteStreak) {
        whitePlayer = player2Id;
        blackPlayer = player1Id;
    } else if (p2HasWhiteStreak && !p1HasWhiteStreak) {
        whitePlayer = player1Id;
        blackPlayer = player2Id;
    }
    // Priority 3: Balance based on total games
    else if (p1History.totalWhite > p1History.totalBlack + 2) {
        // Player 1 has played white much more
        whitePlayer = player2Id;
        blackPlayer = player1Id;
    } else if (p1History.totalBlack > p1History.totalWhite + 2) {
        // Player 1 has played black much more
        whitePlayer = player1Id;
        blackPlayer = player2Id;
    } else if (p2History.totalWhite > p2History.totalBlack + 2) {
        // Player 2 has played white much more
        whitePlayer = player1Id;
        blackPlayer = player2Id;
    } else if (p2History.totalBlack > p2History.totalWhite + 2) {
        // Player 2 has played black much more
        whitePlayer = player2Id;
        blackPlayer = player1Id;
    }
    // Priority 4: Random
    else {
        const random = Math.random() < 0.5;
        whitePlayer = random ? player1Id : player2Id;
        blackPlayer = random ? player2Id : player1Id;
    }
    
    // Update history
    updateColorHistory(guildId, whitePlayer, 'white');
    updateColorHistory(guildId, blackPlayer, 'black');
    
    return {
        white: whitePlayer,
        black: blackPlayer,
        method: p1HasBlackStreak || p2HasBlackStreak ? 'streak_balance' :
                p1HasWhiteStreak || p2HasWhiteStreak ? 'streak_balance' :
                Math.abs(p1History.totalWhite - p1History.totalBlack) > 2 ? 'total_balance' :
                Math.abs(p2History.totalWhite - p2History.totalBlack) > 2 ? 'total_balance' :
                'random'
    };
}

module.exports = {
    assignColors,
    updateColorHistory
};

//======================================================================
//                    CHESS ELO RATING SYSTEM
//======================================================================

const DataManager = require('../utils/dataManager');
const dataManager = new DataManager();

// ELO Constants
const BASE_ELO = 400;
const PROVISIONAL_GAMES = 5; // Show rating after 5 games
const K_FACTOR_NEW = 40;      // High volatility for new players
const K_FACTOR_REGULAR = 20;  // Regular players
const K_FACTOR_EXPERT = 10;   // Players above 2000 ELO

// Get or create player ELO data
function getPlayerElo(guildId, userId) {
    const eloData = dataManager.loadFile(guildId, 'chess_elo.json');
    
    if (!eloData[userId]) {
        eloData[userId] = {
            elo: BASE_ELO,
            games: 0,
            wins: 0,
            losses: 0,
            draws: 0,
            provisional: true,
            history: []
        };
        dataManager.saveFile(guildId, 'chess_elo.json', eloData);
    }
    
    return eloData[userId];
}

// Get global ELO (across all servers)
function getGlobalElo(userId) {
    const globalData = dataManager.loadFile('global', 'chess_global_elo.json');
    
    if (!globalData[userId]) {
        globalData[userId] = {
            elo: BASE_ELO,
            games: 0,
            wins: 0,
            losses: 0,
            draws: 0,
            provisional: true,
            servers: {},
            history: []
        };
        dataManager.saveFile('global', 'chess_global_elo.json', globalData);
    }
    
    return globalData[userId];
}

// Calculate K-factor based on player stats
function getKFactor(playerData) {
    // New/provisional players get high K-factor
    if (playerData.provisional || playerData.games < PROVISIONAL_GAMES) {
        return K_FACTOR_NEW;
    }
    
    // Expert players (2000+) get low K-factor
    if (playerData.elo >= 2000) {
        return K_FACTOR_EXPERT;
    }
    
    // Regular players
    return K_FACTOR_REGULAR;
}

// Calculate expected score (probability of winning)
function getExpectedScore(playerElo, opponentElo) {
    return 1 / (1 + Math.pow(10, (opponentElo - playerElo) / 400));
}

// Update ELO after a game
function updateElo(guildId, winnerId, loserId, isDraw = false) {
    const eloData = dataManager.loadFile(guildId, 'chess_elo.json');
    const globalData = dataManager.loadFile('global', 'chess_global_elo.json');
    
    // Get player data
    const winner = getPlayerElo(guildId, winnerId);
    const loser = getPlayerElo(guildId, loserId);
    const globalWinner = getGlobalElo(winnerId);
    const globalLoser = getGlobalElo(loserId);
    
    // Calculate expected scores
    const expectedWinner = getExpectedScore(winner.elo, loser.elo);
    const expectedLoser = getExpectedScore(loser.elo, winner.elo);
    
    // Actual scores (1 = win, 0.5 = draw, 0 = loss)
    const actualWinner = isDraw ? 0.5 : 1;
    const actualLoser = isDraw ? 0.5 : 0;
    
    // Get K-factors
    const kWinner = getKFactor(winner);
    const kLoser = getKFactor(loser);
    
    // Calculate ELO changes
    const winnerChange = Math.round(kWinner * (actualWinner - expectedWinner));
    const loserChange = Math.round(kLoser * (actualLoser - expectedLoser));
    
    // Old ELOs for history
    const oldWinnerElo = winner.elo;
    const oldLoserElo = loser.elo;
    
    // Update server ELOs
    winner.elo += winnerChange;
    loser.elo += loserChange;
    winner.games++;
    loser.games++;
    
    if (isDraw) {
        winner.draws++;
        loser.draws++;
    } else {
        winner.wins++;
        loser.losses++;
    }
    
    // Check if still provisional
    if (winner.games >= PROVISIONAL_GAMES) {
        winner.provisional = false;
    }
    if (loser.games >= PROVISIONAL_GAMES) {
        loser.provisional = false;
    }
    
    // Add to history
    winner.history.push({
        opponent: loserId,
        result: isDraw ? 'draw' : 'win',
        eloChange: winnerChange,
        newElo: winner.elo,
        timestamp: Date.now()
    });
    
    loser.history.push({
        opponent: winnerId,
        result: isDraw ? 'draw' : 'loss',
        eloChange: loserChange,
        newElo: loser.elo,
        timestamp: Date.now()
    });
    
    // Keep only last 20 games in history
    if (winner.history.length > 20) winner.history = winner.history.slice(-20);
    if (loser.history.length > 20) loser.history = loser.history.slice(-20);
    
    // Update server data
    eloData[winnerId] = winner;
    eloData[loserId] = loser;
    dataManager.saveFile(guildId, 'chess_elo.json', eloData);
    
    // Update global ELOs with same logic
    const globalExpectedWinner = getExpectedScore(globalWinner.elo, globalLoser.elo);
    const globalExpectedLoser = getExpectedScore(globalLoser.elo, globalWinner.elo);
    
    const globalKWinner = getKFactor(globalWinner);
    const globalKLoser = getKFactor(globalLoser);
    
    const globalWinnerChange = Math.round(globalKWinner * (actualWinner - globalExpectedWinner));
    const globalLoserChange = Math.round(globalKLoser * (actualLoser - globalExpectedLoser));
    
    globalWinner.elo += globalWinnerChange;
    globalLoser.elo += globalLoserChange;
    globalWinner.games++;
    globalLoser.games++;
    
    if (isDraw) {
        globalWinner.draws++;
        globalLoser.draws++;
    } else {
        globalWinner.wins++;
        globalLoser.losses++;
    }
    
    if (globalWinner.games >= PROVISIONAL_GAMES) {
        globalWinner.provisional = false;
    }
    if (globalLoser.games >= PROVISIONAL_GAMES) {
        globalLoser.provisional = false;
    }
    
    // Track server participation
    if (!globalWinner.servers[guildId]) {
        globalWinner.servers[guildId] = { games: 0, elo: BASE_ELO };
    }
    if (!globalLoser.servers[guildId]) {
        globalLoser.servers[guildId] = { games: 0, elo: BASE_ELO };
    }
    
    globalWinner.servers[guildId].games++;
    globalLoser.servers[guildId].games++;
    globalWinner.servers[guildId].elo = winner.elo;
    globalLoser.servers[guildId].elo = loser.elo;
    
    globalWinner.history.push({
        opponent: loserId,
        server: guildId,
        result: isDraw ? 'draw' : 'win',
        eloChange: globalWinnerChange,
        newElo: globalWinner.elo,
        timestamp: Date.now()
    });
    
    globalLoser.history.push({
        opponent: winnerId,
        server: guildId,
        result: isDraw ? 'draw' : 'loss',
        eloChange: globalLoserChange,
        newElo: globalLoser.elo,
        timestamp: Date.now()
    });
    
    if (globalWinner.history.length > 50) globalWinner.history = globalWinner.history.slice(-50);
    if (globalLoser.history.length > 50) globalLoser.history = globalLoser.history.slice(-50);
    
    globalData[winnerId] = globalWinner;
    globalData[loserId] = globalLoser;
    dataManager.saveFile('global', 'chess_global_elo.json', globalData);
    
    return {
        winner: {
            oldElo: oldWinnerElo,
            newElo: winner.elo,
            change: winnerChange,
            globalChange: globalWinnerChange,
            globalElo: globalWinner.elo,
            provisional: winner.provisional,
            games: winner.games
        },
        loser: {
            oldElo: oldLoserElo,
            newElo: loser.elo,
            change: loserChange,
            globalChange: globalLoserChange,
            globalElo: globalLoser.elo,
            provisional: loser.provisional,
            games: loser.games
        }
    };
}

// Get server leaderboard
function getServerLeaderboard(guildId, limit = 10) {
    const eloData = dataManager.loadFile(guildId, 'chess_elo.json');
    
    const players = Object.entries(eloData)
        .filter(([userId, data]) => data.games >= PROVISIONAL_GAMES)
        .map(([userId, data]) => ({
            userId,
            elo: data.elo,
            games: data.games,
            wins: data.wins,
            losses: data.losses,
            draws: data.draws,
            winRate: data.games > 0 ? ((data.wins / data.games) * 100).toFixed(1) : 0
        }))
        .sort((a, b) => b.elo - a.elo)
        .slice(0, limit);
    
    return players;
}

// Get global leaderboard
function getGlobalLeaderboard(limit = 10) {
    const globalData = dataManager.loadFile('global', 'chess_global_elo.json');
    
    const players = Object.entries(globalData)
        .filter(([userId, data]) => data.games >= PROVISIONAL_GAMES)
        .map(([userId, data]) => ({
            userId,
            elo: data.elo,
            games: data.games,
            wins: data.wins,
            losses: data.losses,
            draws: data.draws,
            servers: Object.keys(data.servers).length,
            winRate: data.games > 0 ? ((data.wins / data.games) * 100).toFixed(1) : 0
        }))
        .sort((a, b) => b.elo - a.elo)
        .slice(0, limit);
    
    return players;
}

// Get player stats
function getPlayerStats(guildId, userId) {
    const serverData = getPlayerElo(guildId, userId);
    const globalData = getGlobalElo(userId);
    
    return {
        server: {
            elo: serverData.elo,
            games: serverData.games,
            wins: serverData.wins,
            losses: serverData.losses,
            draws: serverData.draws,
            provisional: serverData.provisional,
            winRate: serverData.games > 0 ? ((serverData.wins / serverData.games) * 100).toFixed(1) : 0,
            recentHistory: serverData.history.slice(-5)
        },
        global: {
            elo: globalData.elo,
            games: globalData.games,
            wins: globalData.wins,
            losses: globalData.losses,
            draws: globalData.draws,
            provisional: globalData.provisional,
            servers: Object.keys(globalData.servers).length,
            winRate: globalData.games > 0 ? ((globalData.wins / globalData.games) * 100).toFixed(1) : 0,
            recentHistory: globalData.history.slice(-5)
        }
    };
}

module.exports = {
    updateElo,
    getPlayerElo,
    getGlobalElo,
    getServerLeaderboard,
    getGlobalLeaderboard,
    getPlayerStats,
    BASE_ELO,
    PROVISIONAL_GAMES
};
