//======================================================================
//                    PERMISSION MANAGER (Role Whitelist)
//======================================================================

const { PermissionFlagsBits } = require('discord.js');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

class PermissionManager {
    constructor() {
        this.dataPath = path.join(__dirname, '../data/permissions');
        this.ensureDataFolder();
    }

    ensureDataFolder() {
        if (!fs.existsSync(this.dataPath)) {
            fs.mkdirSync(this.dataPath, { recursive: true });
        }
    }

    getGuildPermissionsPath(guildId) {
        return path.join(this.dataPath, `${guildId}.json`);
    }

    loadGuildPermissions(guildId) {
        const filePath = this.getGuildPermissionsPath(guildId);
        if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
            const defaultPerms = {
                whitelistedRoles: [],
                adminCommands: [
                    'addmoney', 'removemoney', 'setmoney',
                    'giveaway', 'minecraftlock', 'minecraftunlock',
                    'mc-create', 'mc-delete', 'mc-start', 'mc-stop', 'mc-restart'
                ]
            };
            fs.writeFileSync(filePath, JSON.stringify(defaultPerms, null, 2));
            return defaultPerms;
        }
        return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8'));
    }

    saveGuildPermissions(guildId, permissions) {
        const filePath = this.getGuildPermissionsPath(guildId);
        fs.writeFileSync(filePath, JSON.stringify(permissions, null, 2));
    }

    addWhitelistedRole(guildId, roleId) {
        const perms = this.loadGuildPermissions(guildId);
        if (!perms.whitelistedRoles.includes(roleId)) {
            perms.whitelistedRoles.push(roleId);
            this.saveGuildPermissions(guildId, perms);
        }
        return perms;
    }

    removeWhitelistedRole(guildId, roleId) {
        const perms = this.loadGuildPermissions(guildId);
        perms.whitelistedRoles = perms.whitelistedRoles.filter(r => r !== roleId);
        this.saveGuildPermissions(guildId, perms);
        return perms;
    }

    getWhitelistedRoles(guildId) {
        const perms = this.loadGuildPermissions(guildId);
        return perms.whitelistedRoles || [];
    }

    // Check if user has permission (Owner, ManageGuild, or Whitelisted Role)
    hasPermission(member, ownerId, guildId, commandName) {
        // Owner always has permission
        if (member.id === ownerId) {
            return true;
        }

        // ManageGuild permission
        if (member.permissions.has(PermissionFlagsBits.ManageGuild)) {
            return true;
        }

        // Check whitelisted roles
        const whitelistedRoles = this.getWhitelistedRoles(guildId);
        const memberRoleIds = member.roles.cache.map(r => r.id);
        
        return whitelistedRoles.some(roleId => memberRoleIds.includes(roleId));
    }

    // List all whitelisted roles with names
    async listWhitelistedRoles(guild) {
        const roleIds = this.getWhitelistedRoles(guild.id);
        const roles = [];

        for (const roleId of roleIds) {
            try {
                const role = await guild.roles.fetch(roleId);
                if (role) {
                    roles.push({
                        id: role.id,
                        name: role.name,
                        color: role.hexColor,
                        position: role.position
                    });
                }
            } catch (err) {
                // Role doesn't exist anymore, skip
            }
        }

        return roles;
    }
}

module.exports = PermissionManager;

//======================================================================
//                          POLL SYSTEM
//======================================================================

const { EmbedBuilder } = require('discord.js');

// Global poll storage
const activePolls = new Map();

function parseTime(text) {
  const match = text.match(/(\d+)([smh])/);
  if (!match) return null;
  
  const num = parseInt(match[1]);
  const unit = match[2];
  
  if (unit === 's') return num * 1000;
  if (unit === 'm') return num * 60000;
  if (unit === 'h') return num * 3600000;
  
  return null;
}

async function closePollInternal(channel, messageId) {
  if (!activePolls.has(messageId)) return;
  
  const poll = activePolls.get(messageId);
  if (poll.closed) return;
  
  poll.closed = true;
  
  const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
    .setTitle('Poll Closed')
    .setColor(0xFF0000);
  
  let totalVotes = 0;
  for (const voters of Object.values(poll.votes)) {
    totalVotes += voters.length;
  }
  
  if (totalVotes === 0) totalVotes = 1;
  
  for (const [emoji, voters] of Object.entries(poll.votes)) {
    const percent = (voters.length / totalVotes) * 100;
    embed.addFields({
      name: emoji,
      value: `${voters.length} votes (${percent.toFixed(1)}%)`,
      inline: false
    });
  }
  
  await channel.send({ embeds: [embed] });
}

async function onReactionAdd(reaction, user) {
  if (user.bot) return;
  
  const msgId = reaction.message.id;
  if (!activePolls.has(msgId)) return;
  
  const poll = activePolls.get(msgId);
  if (poll.closed) return;
  
  const emoji = reaction.emoji.toString();
  if (!poll.votes[emoji]) return;
  
  if (poll.multiple) {
    // Multiple choice - can vote for multiple options
    if (!poll.votes[emoji].includes(user.id)) {
      poll.votes[emoji].push(user.id);
    }
  } else {
    // Single choice - remove from other options
    for (const e of Object.keys(poll.votes)) {
      const index = poll.votes[e].indexOf(user.id);
      if (index > -1) {
        poll.votes[e].splice(index, 1);
      }
    }
    poll.votes[emoji].push(user.id);
  }
}

function setup(client) {
  console.log('✅ Poll system loaded');
}

module.exports = {
  setup,
  activePolls,
  parseTime,
  closePollInternal,
  onReactionAdd
};



--- FILE: C:\Users\ADMIN\Documents\Nam\Project\Programmer being\Discord\src\systems\reminderSystem.js ---
//======================================================================
//                      REMINDER SYSTEM
//======================================================================

const { EmbedBuilder } = require('discord.js');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

// Active reminder timeouts
const activeReminders = new Map();

function parseTime(timeStr) {
  const match = timeStr.toLowerCase().match(/(\d+)([mhd])/);
  if (!match) return null;
  
  const value = parseInt(match[1]);
  const unit = match[2];
  
  const units = { m: 60000, h: 3600000, d: 86400000 };
  return value * (units[unit] || 0);
}

function loadReminders(guildId) {
  const dataPath = path.join(__dirname, '../../data', guildId.toString(), 'reminders.json');
  
  try {
    if (fs.existsSync(dataPath)) {
      return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(dataPath, 'utf8'));
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`Error loading reminders for guild ${guildId}:`, err);
  }
  
  return {};
}

function saveReminders(guildId, reminders) {
  const dataDir = path.join(__dirname, '../../data', guildId.toString());
  const dataPath = path.join(dataDir, 'reminders.json');
  
  try {
    if (!fs.existsSync(dataDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(dataDir, { recursive: true });
    }
    fs.writeFileSync(dataPath, JSON.stringify(reminders, null, 2));
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`Error saving reminders for guild ${guildId}:`, err);
  }
}

async function sendReminder(client, reminderId, guildId) {
  const reminders = loadReminders(guildId);
  
  if (!reminders[reminderId]) return;
  
  const data = reminders[reminderId];
  
  const user = await client.users.fetch(data.user_id).catch(() => null);
  if (!user) return;
  
  const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
    .setTitle('🔔 Reminder!')
    .setDescription(data.reminder)
    .setColor(0x00D4FF);
  
  try {
    await user.send({ embeds: [embed] });
  } catch {
    // Try channel fallback
    const channel = client.channels.cache.get(data.channel_id);
    if (channel) {
      await channel.send({ content: `🔔 ${user} **Reminder:** ${data.reminder}` });
    }
  }
  
  // Remove from storage
  delete reminders[reminderId];
  saveReminders(guildId, reminders);
  
  // Remove from active reminders
  activeReminders.delete(reminderId);
}

async function loadPendingReminders(client) {
  console.log('⏰ Loading pending reminders...');
  
  const dataFolder = path.join(__dirname, '../../data');
  
  if (!fs.existsSync(dataFolder)) return;
  
  let loadedCount = 0;
  
  for (const guildFolder of fs.readdirSync(dataFolder)) {
    const guildPath = path.join(dataFolder, guildFolder);
    
    if (!fs.statSync(guildPath).isDirectory()) continue;
    
    try {
      const guildId = guildFolder;
      const reminders = loadReminders(guildId);
      
      for (const [reminderId, data] of Object.entries(reminders)) {
        const remindAt = new Date(data.remind_at);
        const now = new Date();
        
        if (remindAt > now) {
          const delay = remindAt.getTime() - now.getTime();
          
          const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
            sendReminder(client, reminderId, guildId);
          }, delay);
          
          activeReminders.set(reminderId, timeout);
          loadedCount++;
          console.log(`   ⏰ Loaded reminder ${reminderId} (${Math.floor(delay / 1000)}s remaining)`);
        } else {
          // Reminder already passed, remove it
          delete reminders[reminderId];
          saveReminders(guildId, reminders);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(`   ❌ Error loading reminders for guild ${guildFolder}:`, err);
    }
  }
  
  console.log(`✅ Loaded ${loadedCount} pending reminder(s)`);
}

function setup(client) {
  // Load pending reminders when bot is ready
  client.once('ready', () => {
    loadPendingReminders(client);
  });
  
  console.log('✅ Reminder system loaded');
}

module.exports = {
  setup,
  parseTime,
  loadReminders,
  saveReminders,
  sendReminder,
  activeReminders
};



--- FILE: C:\Users\ADMIN\Documents\Nam\Project\Programmer being\Discord\src\systems\xpSystem.js ---
//======================================================================
//                   XP SYSTEM - MESSAGE XP GAIN
//======================================================================

const DatabaseManager = require('../utils/DatabaseManager');
const { EmbedBuilder } = require('discord.js');

const dbManager = new DatabaseManager();

// XP Cooldown tracking (1 minute per user)
const xpCooldowns = new Map();

// Config
const XP_CONFIG = {
    COOLDOWN: 60000, // 1 minute
    MIN_XP: 15,
    MAX_XP: 25,
    BOOSTER_MULTIPLIER: 2,
    MIN_LEVEL_ANNOUNCE: 5 // Only announce level 5 and above
};

/**
 * Handle XP gain on message
 * @param {Message} message - Discord message
 * @param {Client} client - Discord client
 */
async function onMessageXPGain(message, client) {
    // Ignore bots
    if (message.author.bot || !message.guild) return;

    // Check if XP is enabled for this server
    const config = dbManager.getServerConfig(message.guild.id);
    if (!config.xp_enabled) return;

    // Check cooldown
    const cooldownKey = `${message.guild.id}-${message.author.id}`;
    const now = Date.now();

    if (xpCooldowns.has(cooldownKey)) {
        const expirationTime = xpCooldowns.get(cooldownKey) + XP_CONFIG.COOLDOWN;
        if (now < expirationTime) {
            return; // Still on cooldown
        }
    }

    // Set new cooldown
    xpCooldowns.set(cooldownKey, now);

    // Calculate XP
    let baseXP = Math.floor(Math.random() * (XP_CONFIG.MAX_XP - XP_CONFIG.MIN_XP + 1)) + XP_CONFIG.MIN_XP;

    // Server booster multiplier
    const member = message.member;
    if (member && member.premiumSince) {
        baseXP *= XP_CONFIG.BOOSTER_MULTIPLIER;
    }

    // XP boost from gem shop
    if (dbManager.hasActiveBoost(message.guild.id, message.author.id, 'xp_boost_1h') ||
        dbManager.hasActiveBoost(message.guild.id, message.author.id, 'xp_boost_24h')) {
        baseXP *= 2;
    }

    // Server XP multiplier
    baseXP = Math.floor(baseXP * (config.xp_multiplier || 1.0));

    // Add XP and check for level up
    const result = dbManager.addXP(message.guild.id, message.author.id, baseXP);

    // If leveled up, announce it
    if (result.leveledUp) {
        // Check if level-up announcements are enabled
        if (config.levelup_enabled === false) {
            return; // Announcements disabled
        }

        // Only announce if level >= MIN_LEVEL_ANNOUNCE (default 5)
        const minLevel = config.levelup_min_level || XP_CONFIG.MIN_LEVEL_ANNOUNCE;
        if (result.newLevel < minLevel) {
            return; // Don't announce low levels
        }

        // Determine channel to send announcement
        let targetChannel = message.channel; // Default: same channel

        if (config.levelup_channel_id) {
            // Use configured channel if set
            const levelUpChannel = message.guild.channels.cache.get(config.levelup_channel_id);
            if (levelUpChannel && levelUpChannel.isTextBased()) {
                targetChannel = levelUpChannel;
            }
        }

        try {
            const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
                .setTitle('🎉 Level Up!')
                .setDescription(`${message.author} reached **Level ${result.newLevel}**!`)
                .setColor(0x00FF00)
                .addFields(
                    { name: 'Next Level', value: `${result.xp}/${result.xpForNext} XP`, inline: true }
                )
                .setThumbnail(message.author.displayAvatarURL())
                .setTimestamp();

            await targetChannel.send({ embeds: [embed] });

            // Level milestones rewards
            if (result.newLevel % 10 === 0) {
                // Every 10 levels, give gems
                const gemReward = Math.floor(result.newLevel / 10);
                dbManager.addGems(message.guild.id, message.author.id, gemReward);
                await targetChannel.send(`💎 Bonus! ${message.author} earned **${gemReward} gems** for reaching level ${result.newLevel}!`);
            }
        } catch (err) {
            console.error('Error sending level up message:', err);
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Clean up expired cooldowns
 */
function cleanupCooldowns() {
    const now = Date.now();
    for (const [key, timestamp] of xpCooldowns) {
        if (now - timestamp > XP_CONFIG.COOLDOWN) {
            xpCooldowns.delete(key);
        }
    }
}

// Clean up cooldowns every 5 minutes
setInterval(cleanupCooldowns, 300000);

function setup(client) {
    console.log('✅ XP system loaded');
}

module.exports = {
    onMessageXPGain,
    setup,
    XP_CONFIG
};

--- FILE: C:\Users\ADMIN\Documents\Nam\Project\Programmer being\Discord\src\utils\chessManager.js ---
import { Chess } from "chess.js";

export const pendingGames = new Map();
export const activeGames = new Map();

/**
 * Create pending chess request
 */
export function createPendingGame(player1, player2, duration) {
  pendingGames.set(player2, {
    player1,
    player2,
    duration,
    createdAt: Date.now()
  });
}

/**
 * Confirm pending game
 */
export function confirmGame(player2, player1) {
  const pending = pendingGames.get(player2);
  if (!pending || pending.player1 !== player1) return null;

  const chess = new Chess();
  const gameId = `${player1}-${player2}`;

  activeGames.set(gameId, {
    chess,
    players: {
      white: player1,
      black: player2
    },
    duration: pending.duration,
    lastMoveAt: Date.now()
  });

  pendingGames.delete(player2);
  return activeGames.get(gameId);
}

/**
 * Find game by player
 */
export function getGameByPlayer(userId) {
  for (const game of activeGames.values()) {
    if (game.players.white === userId || game.players.black === userId) {
      return game;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * Try algebraic move
 */
export function tryMove(userId, content) {
  const game = getGameByPlayer(userId);
  if (!game) return null;

  const chess = game.chess;
  const isWhiteTurn = chess.turn() === "w";
  const correctPlayer =
    (isWhiteTurn && game.players.white === userId) ||
    (!isWhiteTurn && game.players.black === userId);

  if (!correctPlayer) return null;

  const move = chess.move(content, { sloppy: true });
  if (!move) return null;

  game.lastMoveAt = Date.now();
  return {
    move,
    fen: chess.fen(),
    check: chess.isCheck(),
    gameOver: chess.isGameOver()
  };
}



--- FILE: C:\Users\ADMIN\Documents\Nam\Project\Programmer being\Discord\src\utils\codeValidator.js ---
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//                       CODE VALIDATOR                              //
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

/**
 * Validates user code for safety
 * - Prevents infinite loops
 * - Blocks input/stdin functions
 * - Limits sleep/wait calls to 10 seconds
 */

class CodeValidator {
    constructor() {
        // Forbidden patterns (loops, input)
        this.forbiddenPatterns = {
            loops: [
                /\bfor\s*[\(\s]/gi,               // for loops
                /\bwhile\s*[\(\s]/gi,             // while loops
                /\bdo\s*[\{\s]/gi,               // do-while loops
                /\.(forEach|map|reduce|repeat)\s*\(/gi, // JS Array/String loops
                /\b(range|itertools)\b/gi,        // Python loop helpers
                /\[.*\s+for\s+.*\s+in\s+.*\]/gi   // Python List Comprehensions
            ],
            inputFunctions: [
                /\binput\s*\(/gi,         // Python input()
                /\braw_input\s*\(/gi,     // Python 2 raw_input()
                /\binput\s*\(/gi,         // Generic input()
                /\bprompt\s*\(/gi,        // JS/Browser prompt()
                /\brequire\s*\(\s*['"]readline['"]\s*\)/gi, // Node readline
                /\bscanf\s*\(/gi,         // C-style scanf
                /\bgetline\s*\(/gi,       // C++ getline
            ],
            dangerousModules: [
                /\bimport\s+os\b/gi,      // Python os module
                /\brequire\s*\(\s*['"]os['"]\s*\)/gi,
                /\bos\s*\.\s*system/gi,   // os.system()
                /\bsubprocess\b/gi,       // Python subprocess
                /\bprocess\b/gi,                     // Fatal: Can kill the bot
                /\b(http|https|net|fetch)\b/gi,     // Networking: Prevents botnets/leaks
                /\b(env|environ)\b/gi,              // Token Security: Protects your 6 keys
                /\b(vm|vm2|isolated-vm)\b/gi,       // Escaping: Prevents sandbox escapes
                /\b(worker_threads|cluster)\b/gi,   // Resource Exhaustion: Prevents lag
                /\b(socket|urllib|requests)\b/gi,   // Python Networking
                /\b(pathlib|shutil|open)\b/gi   ,    // File System (Python alternatives)
                /\bexec\b/gi,             // exec function
                /\beval\b/gi,             // eval function
                /\brequire\s*\(\s*['"]fs['"]\s*\)/gi, // Node fs module
                /\brequire\s*\(\s*['"]child_process['"]\s*\)/gi, // Node child_process
                /\b__import__\b/gi,         // Bypasses 'import' keywords
                /\b__subclasses__\b/gi,     // Famous 'jailbreak' method to find hidden classes
                /\b__builtins__\b/gi,       // Accesses all internal functions
                /\b(os|subprocess|pty)\b/gi, // System commands and Terminal access
                /\b(requests|urllib|socket|asyncio)\b/gi, // Network/Internet access
                /\b(pickle|marshal|dbm)\b/gi, // Can execute code during 'unpickling' data
                /\b(sys\.(modules|argv|exit|path))\b/gi, // Internal system state
                /\b(threading|multiprocessing|asyncio)\b/gi ,
                /\bgetattr\b/gi,
                /\bsetattr\b/gi,
                /\.constructor\b/gi,
                /process\.env/gi,  // Protect your 6 tokens!
            ]
        };
    }

    /**
     * Validates code for security issues
     * @param {string} code - The code to validate
     * @param {string} language - Programming language (python, javascript, etc)
     * @returns {Object} - { valid: boolean, errors: Array<string> }
     */
    validateCode(code, language) {
        const errors = [];

        // Check for loops
        for (const pattern of this.forbiddenPatterns.loops) {
            if (pattern.test(code)) {
                errors.push('❌ Loops are not allowed (for, while, do-while)');
                break;
            }
        }

        // Check for input functions
        for (const pattern of this.forbiddenPatterns.inputFunctions) {
            if (pattern.test(code)) {
                errors.push('❌ Input/stdin functions are not allowed (input, prompt, etc)');
                break;
            }
        }

        // Check for dangerous modules/functions
        for (const pattern of this.forbiddenPatterns.dangerousModules) {
            if (pattern.test(code)) {
                errors.push('❌ Dangerous modules/functions are not allowed (os, subprocess, fs, child_process, exec, eval)');
                break;
            }
        }

        // Check sleep/wait/setTimeout total
        const sleepError = this.validateSleepCalls(code, language);
        if (sleepError) {
            errors.push(sleepError);
        }

        return {
            valid: errors.length === 0,
            errors: errors
        };
    }

    /**
     * Validates that total sleep time doesn't exceed 10 seconds
     * @param {string} code - The code to check
     * @param {string} language - Programming language
     * @returns {string|null} - Error message or null if valid
     */
    validateSleepCalls(code, language) {
        let sleepPatterns = [];
        let maxSleep = 5; // 5 seconds

        if (language.toLowerCase() === 'python') {
            // Python: time.sleep(seconds)
            sleepPatterns = [
                { regex: /time\.sleep\s*\(\s*([\d.]+)\s*\)/g, unit: 'seconds' },
                { regex: /sleep\s*\(\s*([\d.]+)\s*\)/g, unit: 'seconds' }
            ];
        } else if (language.toLowerCase() === 'javascript') {
            // JavaScript: setTimeout(fn, milliseconds) and Thread.sleep (Rhino)
            sleepPatterns = [
                { regex: /setTimeout\s*\(\s*[^,]*,\s*([\d.]+)\s*\)/g, unit: 'milliseconds' },
                { regex: /Thread\.sleep\s*\(\s*([\d.]+)\s*\)/g, unit: 'milliseconds' }
            ];
        } else if (language.toLowerCase() === 'java') {
            // Java: Thread.sleep(milliseconds)
            sleepPatterns = [
                { regex: /Thread\.sleep\s*\(\s*([\d.]+)\s*\)/g, unit: 'milliseconds' }
            ];
        }

        let totalSleepMs = 0;

        for (const pattern of sleepPatterns) {
            let match;
            while ((match = pattern.regex.exec(code)) !== null) {
                const value = parseFloat(match[1]);
                const sleepMs = pattern.unit === 'milliseconds' ? value : value * 1000;
                totalSleepMs += sleepMs;
            }
        }

        if (totalSleepMs > maxSleep * 1000) {
            return `❌ Total sleep time exceeds limit! Max: ${maxSleep}s, Got: ${(totalSleepMs / 1000).toFixed(2)}s`;
        }

        return null;
    }

    /**
     * Extracts code from Discord code block format
     * @param {string} content - The message content
     * @returns {Object} - { code: string, language: string, found: boolean }
     */
    extractCodeBlock(content) {
        // Match ```language code ``` pattern (flexible with whitespace)
        const codeBlockRegex = /```\s*(\w*)\s*\n([\s\S]*?)\n\s*```/;
        const match = content.match(codeBlockRegex);

        if (!match) {
            return {
                code: null,
                language: null,
                found: false
            };
        }

        return {
            code: match[2].trim(),
            language: match[1] || 'text',
            found: true
        };
    }
}

module.exports = CodeValidator;

const Database = require('better-sqlite3');
const path = require('path');

const dbPath = path.join(__dirname, '../bot.db');
const db = new Database(dbPath);

// Enable Write-Ahead Logging for better performance
db.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');

console.log('📁 Database file:', dbPath);

// ============ CREATE TABLES ============

// Servers table

db.exec(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS servers (
        server_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        config TEXT,
        dashboard_token TEXT,
        created_at INTEGER
    )
`);

// Economy table (UPDATED: added gems and daily_streak)
db.exec(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS economy (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        server_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        balance INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        gems INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        last_daily INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        daily_streak INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        UNIQUE(server_id, user_id)
    )
`);

///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//                    DATABASE MANAGER (UPDATED WITH GEMS & XP)      //
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

const db = require('./database');

class DatabaseManager {
    // ============ SERVER CONFIGURATION ============

    getServerConfig(serverId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT config FROM servers WHERE server_id = ?');
        const row = stmt.get(serverId);

        if (!row) {
            // Create default config
            const defaultConfig = {
                prefix: '%',
                xp_enabled: true,
                xp_multiplier: 1.0,
                created_at: Date.now()
            };
            this.saveServerConfig(serverId, defaultConfig);
            return defaultConfig;
        }

        return JSON.parse(row.config);
    }

    saveServerConfig(serverId, config) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO servers (server_id, config, created_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?)
        `);
        stmt.run(serverId, JSON.stringify(config), Date.now());
    }

    getDashboardToken(serverId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT dashboard_token FROM servers WHERE server_id = ?');
        const row = stmt.get(serverId);
        return row ? row.dashboard_token : null;
    }

    setDashboardToken(serverId, token) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO servers (server_id, dashboard_token, created_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?)
        `);
        stmt.run(serverId, token, Date.now());
    }

    // ============ GET DAILY CLAIM COOLDOWN ===
    getDailyCooldown(serverId, userId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT last_daily FROM economy WHERE server_id = ? AND user_id = ?');
        const row = stmt.get(serverId, userId);
        return row ? row.last_daily : 0;
    }

    setDailyCooldown(serverId, userId, timestamp) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO economy (server_id, user_id, last_daily)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?)
        ON CONFLICT(server_id, user_id) DO UPDATE SET last_daily = ?
    `);
        stmt.run(serverId, userId, timestamp, timestamp);
    }

    getDailyStreak(serverId, userId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT daily_streak FROM economy WHERE server_id = ? AND user_id = ?');
        const row = stmt.get(serverId, userId);
        return row ? row.daily_streak : 0;
    }

    updateDailyStreak(serverId, userId, streak) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO economy (server_id, user_id, daily_streak)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(server_id, user_id) DO UPDATE SET daily_streak = ?
        `);
        stmt.run(serverId, userId, streak, streak);
    }

    // ============ ECONOMY SYSTEM ============

    getEconomy(serverId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT user_id, balance FROM economy WHERE server_id = ?');
        const rows = stmt.all(serverId);

        const economy = {};
        for (const row of rows) {
            economy[row.user_id] = row.balance;
        }
        return economy;
    }

    getBalance(serverId, userId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT balance FROM economy WHERE server_id = ? AND user_id = ?');
        const row = stmt.get(serverId, userId);
        return row ? row.balance : 0;
    }

    setBalance(serverId, userId, amount) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO economy (server_id, user_id, balance)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?)
        ON CONFLICT(server_id, user_id) DO UPDATE SET balance = ?
    `);
        stmt.run(serverId, userId, amount, amount);
    }

    addUserBalance(serverId, userId, amount) {
        try {
            const currentBalance = this.getBalance(serverId, userId);
            const newBalance = currentBalance + amount;
            this.setBalance(serverId, userId, newBalance);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('❌ Error in addUserBalance:', error);
            throw error;
        }
    }

    removeBalance(serverId, userId, amount) {
        const currentBalance = this.getBalance(serverId, userId);
        this.setBalance(serverId, userId, Math.max(0, currentBalance - amount));
    }

    getTopBalances(serverId, limit = 10) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
        SELECT user_id, balance FROM economy 
        WHERE server_id = ? AND balance > 0
        ORDER BY balance DESC 
        LIMIT ?
    `);
        return stmt.all(serverId, limit);
    }

    getGlobalTopBalances(limit = 10) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
        SELECT user_id, SUM(balance) as total_balance 
        FROM economy 
        WHERE balance > 0
        GROUP BY user_id 
        ORDER BY total_balance DESC 
        LIMIT ?
    `);
        return stmt.all(limit);
    }

    // ============ GEMS SYSTEM (NEW!) ============

    getUserGems(serverId, userId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT gems FROM economy WHERE server_id = ? AND user_id = ?');
        const row = stmt.get(serverId, userId);
        return row ? (row.gems || 0) : 0;
    }

    addGems(serverId, userId, amount) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO economy (server_id, user_id, gems)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(server_id, user_id)
            DO UPDATE SET gems = gems + ?
        `);
        stmt.run(serverId, userId, amount, amount);
    }

    removeGems(serverId, userId, amount) {
        const current = this.getUserGems(serverId, userId);
        if (current >= amount) {
            const stmt = db.prepare(`
                UPDATE economy 
                SET gems = gems - ? 
                WHERE server_id = ? AND user_id = ?
            `);
            stmt.run(amount, serverId, userId);
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    setGems(serverId, userId, amount) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO economy (server_id, user_id, gems)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(server_id, user_id)
            DO UPDATE SET gems = ?
        `);
        stmt.run(serverId, userId, amount, amount);
    }

    // ============ XP/LEVEL SYSTEM (NEW!) ============

    getUserXP(serverId, userId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM user_xp WHERE server_id = ? AND user_id = ?');
        const row = stmt.get(serverId, userId);

        if (!row) {
            return {
                xp: 0,
                level: 0,
                total_xp: 0,
                profile_color: '#7289DA',
                last_xp_gain: 0
            };
        }

        return {
            xp: row.xp,
            level: row.level,
            total_xp: row.total_xp,
            profile_color: row.profile_color,
            last_xp_gain: row.last_xp_gain
        };
    }

    addXP(serverId, userId, xpAmount) {
        const current = this.getUserXP(serverId, userId);
        const newXP = current.xp + xpAmount;
        const newTotalXP = current.total_xp + xpAmount;

        // Calculate level up
        let level = current.level;
        let xpForNextLevel = this.getXPForLevel(level + 1);
        let leveledUp = false;

        let remainingXP = newXP;

        while (remainingXP >= xpForNextLevel) {
            remainingXP -= xpForNextLevel;
            level++;
            leveledUp = true;
            xpForNextLevel = this.getXPForLevel(level + 1);
        }

        // Update database
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO user_xp (server_id, user_id, xp, level, total_xp, last_xp_gain)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(server_id, user_id)
            DO UPDATE SET 
                xp = ?,
                level = ?,
                total_xp = ?,
                last_xp_gain = ?
        `);

        const now = Date.now();
        stmt.run(
            serverId, userId, remainingXP, level, newTotalXP, now,
            remainingXP, level, newTotalXP, now
        );

        if (leveledUp) {
            return {
                leveledUp: true,
                oldLevel: current.level,
                newLevel: level,
                xp: remainingXP,
                xpForNext: this.getXPForLevel(level + 1)
            };
        }

        return {
            leveledUp: false,
            level: level,
            xp: remainingXP,
            xpForNext: this.getXPForLevel(level + 1)
        };
    }

    getXPForLevel(level) {
        // Formula: 100 * level^1.5
        return Math.floor(100 * Math.pow(level, 1.5));
    }

    setProfileColor(serverId, userId, color) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO user_xp (server_id, user_id, profile_color)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(server_id, user_id)
            DO UPDATE SET profile_color = ?
        `);
        stmt.run(serverId, userId, color, color);
    }

    getXPLeaderboard(serverId, limit = 10) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            SELECT user_id, level, total_xp 
            FROM user_xp 
            WHERE server_id = ? AND level > 0
            ORDER BY level DESC, total_xp DESC
            LIMIT ?
        `);
        return stmt.all(serverId, limit);
    }

    getGlobalXPLeaderboard(limit = 10) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            SELECT user_id, SUM(total_xp) as global_xp, MAX(level) as max_level
            FROM user_xp
            WHERE level > 0
            GROUP BY user_id
            ORDER BY max_level DESC, global_xp DESC
            LIMIT ?
        `);
        return stmt.all(limit);
    }

    getServerRank(serverId, userId) {
        const userXP = this.getUserXP(serverId, userId);

        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            SELECT COUNT(*) + 1 as rank
            FROM user_xp
            WHERE server_id = ? AND (
                level > ? OR 
                (level = ? AND total_xp > ?)
            )
        `);

        const result = stmt.get(serverId, userXP.level, userXP.level, userXP.total_xp);
        return result ? result.rank : 1;
    }

    // ============ GEM SHOP PURCHASES ============

    purchaseItem(serverId, userId, itemId, expiresAt = null, data = null) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO gem_purchases (server_id, user_id, item_id, purchase_time, expires_at, data)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `);
        stmt.run(serverId, userId, itemId, Date.now(), expiresAt, data ? JSON.stringify(data) : null);
    }

    hasActiveBoost(serverId, userId, boostType) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            SELECT * FROM gem_purchases 
            WHERE server_id = ? AND user_id = ? AND item_id = ? 
            AND (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > ?)
        `);
        const row = stmt.get(serverId, userId, boostType, Date.now());
        return row !== undefined;
    }

    getActivePurchases(serverId, userId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            SELECT * FROM gem_purchases 
            WHERE server_id = ? AND user_id = ?
            AND (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > ?)
            ORDER BY purchase_time DESC
        `);
        return stmt.all(serverId, userId, Date.now());
    }

    cleanExpiredBoosts() {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            DELETE FROM gem_purchases 
            WHERE expires_at IS NOT NULL AND expires_at < ?
        `);
        stmt.run(Date.now());
    }

    // ============ GIVEAWAY SYSTEM ============

    getAllGiveaways(serverId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM giveaways WHERE server_id = ?');
        const rows = stmt.all(serverId);

        return rows.map(row => ({
            ...row,
            participants: JSON.parse(row.participants)
        }));
    }

    getActiveGiveaways(serverId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM giveaways WHERE server_id = ? AND active = 1');
        const rows = stmt.all(serverId);

        return rows.map(row => ({
            ...row,
            participants: JSON.parse(row.participants)
        }));
    }

    getGiveaway(messageId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM giveaways WHERE message_id = ?');
        const row = stmt.get(messageId);

        if (!row) return null;

        return {
            ...row,
            participants: JSON.parse(row.participants)
        };
    }

    createGiveaway(serverId, messageId, channelId, prize, winners, endTime, hostId, claimTime = 3600, requirements = {}, extraEntries = {}) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO giveaways (server_id, message_id, channel_id, prize, winners, end_time, host_id, claim_time, requirements, extra_entries, created_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `);
        stmt.run(serverId, messageId, channelId, prize, winners, endTime, hostId, claimTime, JSON.stringify(requirements), JSON.stringify(extraEntries), Date.now());
    }

    updateGiveaway(messageId, updates) {
        const fields = [];
        const values = [];

        if (updates.winners_list !== undefined) {
            fields.push('winners_list = ?');
            values.push(JSON.stringify(updates.winners_list));
        }
        if (updates.claimed !== undefined) {
            fields.push('claimed = ?');
            values.push(updates.claimed ? 1 : 0);
        }
        if (updates.claim_deadline !== undefined) {
            fields.push('claim_deadline = ?');
            values.push(updates.claim_deadline);
        }
        if (updates.reroll_enabled !== undefined) {
            fields.push('reroll_enabled = ?');
            values.push(updates.reroll_enabled ? 1 : 0);
        }
        if (updates.active !== undefined) {
            fields.push('active = ?');
            values.push(updates.active ? 1 : 0);
        }

        if (fields.length === 0) return;

        values.push(messageId);
        const stmt = db.prepare(`UPDATE giveaways SET ${fields.join(', ')} WHERE message_id = ?`);
        stmt.run(...values);
    }

    addGiveawayParticipant(messageId, userId) {
        const giveaway = this.getGiveaway(messageId);
        if (!giveaway) return;

        const participants = giveaway.participants;
        if (!participants.includes(userId)) {
            participants.push(userId);

            const stmt = db.prepare('UPDATE giveaways SET participants = ? WHERE message_id = ?');
            stmt.run(JSON.stringify(participants), messageId);
        }
    }

    removeGiveawayParticipant(messageId, userId) {
        const giveaway = this.getGiveaway(messageId);
        if (!giveaway) return;

        const participants = giveaway.participants.filter(id => id !== userId);

        const stmt = db.prepare('UPDATE giveaways SET participants = ? WHERE message_id = ?');
        stmt.run(JSON.stringify(participants), messageId);
    }

    endGiveaway(messageId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('UPDATE giveaways SET active = 0 WHERE message_id = ?');
        stmt.run(messageId);
    }

    deleteGiveaway(messageId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('DELETE FROM giveaways WHERE message_id = ?');
        stmt.run(messageId);
    }

    // ============ COMMAND STATES ============

    getCommandStates(serverId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT command_name, enabled FROM command_states WHERE server_id = ?');
        const rows = stmt.all(serverId);

        const states = {};
        for (const row of rows) {
            states[row.command_name] = row.enabled === 1;
        }
        return states;
    }

    getCommandState(serverId, commandName) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT enabled FROM command_states WHERE server_id = ? AND command_name = ?');
        const row = stmt.get(serverId, commandName);
        return row ? row.enabled === 1 : true; // Default enabled
    }

    setCommandState(serverId, commandName, enabled) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO command_states (server_id, command_name, enabled)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?)
        `);
        stmt.run(serverId, commandName, enabled ? 1 : 0);
    }

    // ============ CHESS SYSTEM ============

    getChessElo(serverId, userId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM chess_elo WHERE server_id = ? AND user_id = ?');
        const row = stmt.get(serverId, userId);

        if (!row) {
            return { elo: 1000, wins: 0, losses: 0, draws: 0 };
        }

        return {
            elo: row.elo,
            wins: row.wins,
            losses: row.losses,
            draws: row.draws
        };
    }

    setChessElo(serverId, userId, elo, wins, losses, draws) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO chess_elo (server_id, user_id, elo, wins, losses, draws)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `);
        stmt.run(serverId, userId, elo, wins, losses, draws);
    }

    getChessColor(serverId, userId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT preferred_color FROM chess_colors WHERE server_id = ? AND user_id = ?');
        const row = stmt.get(serverId, userId);
        return row ? row.preferred_color : 'random';
    }

    setChessColor(serverId, userId, color) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO chess_colors (server_id, user_id, preferred_color)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?)
        `);

    getGlobalChessElo(userId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM global_chess_elo WHERE user_id = ?');

            return { elo: 1000, wins: 0, losses: 0, draws: 0 };
        }

        return {
            elo: row.elo,
            wins: row.wins,
            losses: row.losses,
            draws: row.draws
        };
    }

    setGlobalChessElo(userId, elo, wins, losses, draws) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO global_chess_elo (user_id, elo, wins, losses, draws)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `);
        stmt.run(userId, elo, wins, losses, draws);
    }

    // ============ REMINDERS ============

    createReminder(serverId, channelId, userId, message, remindAt) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO reminders (server_id, channel_id, user_id, message, remind_at, created_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `);
        stmt.run(serverId, channelId, userId, message, remindAt, Date.now());
    }

    getActiveReminders() {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM reminders WHERE active = 1 AND remind_at <= ?');
        return stmt.all(Date.now());
    }

    getUserReminders(serverId, userId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM reminders WHERE server_id = ? AND user_id = ? AND active = 1');
        return stmt.all(serverId, userId);
    }

    completeReminder(id) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('UPDATE reminders SET active = 0 WHERE id = ?');
        stmt.run(id);
    }

        stmt.run(id);
    }

    // ============ POLLS ============

    createPoll(serverId, messageId, channelId, question, options, createdBy, endsAt) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO polls (server_id, message_id, channel_id, question, options, created_by, created_at, ends_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `);
        stmt.run(serverId, messageId, channelId, question, JSON.stringify(options), createdBy, Date.now(), endsAt);
    }

    getPoll(messageId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM polls WHERE message_id = ?');
        const row = stmt.get(messageId);

        if (!row) return null;

        return {
            ...row,
            options: JSON.parse(row.options),
            votes: JSON.parse(row.votes)
        };
    }

    addPollVote(messageId, userId, option) {
        const poll = this.getPoll(messageId);
        if (!poll) return;

        const votes = poll.votes;
        votes[userId] = option;

        const stmt = db.prepare('UPDATE polls SET votes = ? WHERE message_id = ?');
        stmt.run(JSON.stringify(votes), messageId);
    }

    endPoll(messageId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('UPDATE polls SET active = 0 WHERE message_id = ?');
        stmt.run(messageId);
    }

    // ============ MINECRAFT BOTS ============

    createMinecraftBot(serverId, userId, botName, botPassword, serverIp, serverPort, version) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`

            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `);
        const result = stmt.run(serverId, userId, botName, botPassword, serverIp, serverPort, version, Date.now());
        return result.lastInsertRowid;
    }

        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM minecraft_bots WHERE id = ?');
        return stmt.get(botId);
    }

    getUserMinecraftBots(serverId, userId) {

    }

    // ============ USER STATS ============

    getUserStats(serverId, userId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM user_stats WHERE server_id = ? AND user_id = ?');

            return { total_messages: 0, daily_messages: 0, math_points: 0 };
        }

        return {
            total_messages: row.total_messages,
            daily_messages: row.daily_messages,
            last_message: row.last_message,
            math_points: row.math_points || 0
        };
    }

    incrementMessageCount(serverId, userId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO user_stats (server_id, user_id, total_messages, daily_messages, last_message)
            VALUES (?, ?, 1, 1, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(server_id, user_id) DO UPDATE SET
                total_messages = total_messages + 1,
                daily_messages = daily_messages + 1,
                last_message = ?
        `);
        const now = Date.now();
        stmt.run(serverId, userId, now, now);
    }

    getTopMessageSenders(serverId, limit = 10) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            SELECT user_id, total_messages FROM user_stats 
            WHERE server_id = ? 
            ORDER BY total_messages DESC 
            LIMIT ?
        `);
        return stmt.all(serverId, limit);
    }

    getTopDailyMessageSenders(serverId, limit = 10) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            SELECT user_id, daily_messages FROM user_stats 
            WHERE server_id = ? AND daily_messages > 0
            ORDER BY daily_messages DESC 
            LIMIT ?
        `);
        return stmt.all(serverId, limit);
    }

    getGlobalTopMessageSenders(limit = 10) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
        SELECT
            user_id,
            SUM(total_messages) as total_messages
        FROM user_stats
        WHERE total_messages > 0
        GROUP BY user_id
        ORDER BY total_messages DESC
        LIMIT ?
    `);
        return stmt.all(limit);
    }

            VALUES (?, ?, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(server_id, user_id) DO UPDATE SET
                math_points = math_points + ?
        `);
        stmt.run(serverId, userId, points, points);
    }

    getTopMathPlayers(serverId, limit = 10) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            SELECT user_id, math_points FROM user_stats 
            WHERE server_id = ? AND math_points > 0
            ORDER BY math_points DESC 
            LIMIT ?
        `);
        return stmt.all(serverId, limit);
    }

    getGlobalTopMathPlayers(limit = 10) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
        SELECT 
            user_id, 
            SUM(math_points) as total_math_points,
            COUNT(DISTINCT server_id) as server_count
        FROM user_stats 
        WHERE math_points > 0
        GROUP BY user_id
        ORDER BY total_math_points DESC 
        LIMIT ?
    `);
        return stmt.all(limit);
    }

    // ============ UTILITY ============

    // Ensure server exists (for compatibility with old DataManager)
    ensureServer(serverId) {
        // Check if server exists, if not create default config
        const config = this.getServerConfig(serverId);
        return config; // getServerConfig already creates if not exists
    }

    // Check if command is enabled (for compatibility)
    isCommandEnabled(serverId, commandName) {
        return this.getCommandState(serverId, commandName);
    }

    // Load file (for compatibility - converts to database calls)
    loadFile(serverId, filename) {
        if (filename === 'server_config.json') {
            return this.getServerConfig(serverId);
        }
        if (filename === 'economy.json') {
            return this.getEconomy(serverId);
        }
        if (filename === 'command_states.json') {
            return this.getCommandStates(serverId);
        }
        if (filename === 'giveaway_data.json') {
            return { giveaways: {} }; // Return empty for now
        }
        return {};
    }

    // Save file (for compatibility - converts to database calls)
    saveFile(serverId, filename, data) {
        if (filename === 'server_config.json') {
            this.saveServerConfig(serverId, data);
        }
        if (filename === 'economy.json') {
            // Economy is saved per-user, not bulk
            for (const [userId, balance] of Object.entries(data)) {
                this.setBalance(serverId, userId, balance);
            }
        }
        if (filename === 'command_states.json') {
            for (const [command, enabled] of Object.entries(data)) {
                this.setCommandState(serverId, command, enabled);
            }
        }
    }

    // ============ USER STATS / MESSAGE TRACKING ============

    recordUserMessage(serverId, userId) {
        const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];

        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO user_stats (server_id, user_id, total_messages, daily_messages, last_message)
            VALUES (?, ?, 1, 1, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(server_id, user_id) DO UPDATE SET
                total_messages = total_messages + 1,
                daily_messages = CASE 
                    WHEN DATE(datetime(last_message / 1000, 'unixepoch')) = DATE('now') 
                    THEN daily_messages + 1 
                    ELSE 1 
                END,
                last_message = ?
        `);

        const now = Date.now();
        stmt.run(serverId, userId, now, now);
    }

    getServerUserStats(serverId, limit = 10) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            SELECT user_id, total_messages, daily_messages, last_message
            FROM user_stats
            WHERE server_id = ?
            ORDER BY total_messages DESC
            LIMIT ?
        `);
        return stmt.all(serverId, limit);
    }

    resetDailyMessages(serverId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('UPDATE user_stats SET daily_messages = 0 WHERE server_id = ?');
        stmt.run(serverId);
    }

    // Backup database to JSON (for migration back if needed)
    backupToJSON() {
        const backup = {
            servers: db.prepare('SELECT * FROM servers').all(),
            economy: db.prepare('SELECT * FROM economy').all(),
            giveaways: db.prepare('SELECT * FROM giveaways').all(),
            command_states: db.prepare('SELECT * FROM command_states').all(),
            chess_elo: db.prepare('SELECT * FROM chess_elo').all(),
            reminders: db.prepare('SELECT * FROM reminders').all(),
            polls: db.prepare('SELECT * FROM polls').all()
        };
        return backup;
    }

    // ============ SERVER ANALYTICS (JOIN/LEAVE) ============

    updateAnalytics(serverId, type, totalCount) {
        const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]; // Định dạng YYYY-MM-DD

        // Tạo bản ghi mới nếu ngày hôm nay chưa có
        const insertStmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT OR IGNORE INTO server_analytics (server_id, date, total_at_day) 
            VALUES (?, ?, ?)
        `);
        insertStmt.run(serverId, today, totalCount);

        // Cập nhật số liệu dựa trên loại sự kiện (join hoặc leave)
        if (type === 'join') {
            const updateJoin = db.prepare(`
                UPDATE server_analytics 
                SET joined = joined + 1, total_at_day = ? 
                WHERE server_id = ? AND date = ?
            `);
            updateJoin.run(totalCount, serverId, today);
        } else if (type === 'leave') {
            const updateLeave = db.prepare(`
                UPDATE server_analytics 
                SET left = left + 1, total_at_day = ? 
                WHERE server_id = ? AND date = ?
            `);
            updateLeave.run(totalCount, serverId, today);
        }
    }

    getRecentAnalytics(serverId, days = 7) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            SELECT * FROM server_analytics 
            WHERE server_id = ? 
            ORDER BY date DESC LIMIT ?
        `);
        return stmt.all(serverId, days).reverse();
    }

    // ============ AFK SYSTEM ============

    setAFK(serverId, userId, reason, timestamp) {
        const stmt = db.prepare(`
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO user_afk (server_id, user_id, reason, timestamp)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
        `);
        stmt.run(serverId, userId, reason, timestamp);
    }

    removeAFK(serverId, userId) {
        const stmt = db.prepare('DELETE FROM user_afk WHERE server_id = ? AND user_id = ?');
        stmt.run(serverId, userId);
    }

    getAllAFK() {
        // Lấy tất cả để load vào Map khi khởi động bot
        return db.prepare('SELECT * FROM user_afk').all();
    }

    // ============ GIVEAWAY CORE ============
    getMultipliers(guildId) {
        return db.prepare('SELECT role_id, multiplier FROM giveaway_multipliers WHERE guild_id = ?').all(guildId);
    }

    addParticipant(messageId, userId) {
        const row = db.prepare('SELECT participants FROM giveaways WHERE message_id = ?').get(messageId);
        if (!row) return false;
        let participants = JSON.parse(row.participants);
        if (participants.includes(userId)) return false;
        participants.push(userId);
        db.prepare('UPDATE giveaways SET participants = ? WHERE message_id = ?').run(JSON.stringify(participants), messageId);
        return true;
    }

}

console.log('✅ Database manager loaded (WITH GEMS & XP SYSTEM)');

module.exports = DatabaseManager;

///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//                         DATA MANAGER                               //
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

class DataManager {
    constructor() {
        this.dataFolder = path.join(process.cwd(), 'data');
        this.ensureDataFolder();
    }

    ensureDataFolder() {
        if (!fs.existsSync(this.dataFolder)) {
            fs.mkdirSync(this.dataFolder, { recursive: true });
        }
    }

    getServerPath(guildId) {
        return path.join(this.dataFolder, guildId.toString());
    }

    ensureServer(guildId) {
        const serverPath = this.getServerPath(guildId);
        if (!fs.existsSync(serverPath)) {
            fs.mkdirSync(serverPath, { recursive: true });
        }
        return serverPath;
    }

    loadFile(guildId, filename) {
        const serverPath = this.ensureServer(guildId);
        const filePath = path.join(serverPath, filename);

        if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
            fs.writeFileSync(filePath, JSON.stringify({}, null, 4));
            return {};
        }

        try {
            const data = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
            return JSON.parse(data);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(`Error loading ${filename}:`, error);
            return {};
        }
    }

    saveFile(guildId, filename, data) {
        const serverPath = this.ensureServer(guildId);
        const filePath = path.join(serverPath, filename);

        try {
            fs.writeFileSync(filePath, JSON.stringify(data, null, 4));
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(`Error saving ${filename}:`, error);
        }
    }

    deleteFile(guildId, filename) {
        const serverPath = this.getServerPath(guildId);
        const filePath = path.join(serverPath, filename);

        if (fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
            fs.unlinkSync(filePath);
        }
    }

    listServers() {
        if (!fs.existsSync(this.dataFolder)) return [];

        return fs.readdirSync(this.dataFolder)
            .filter(item => {
                const itemPath = path.join(this.dataFolder, item);
                return fs.statSync(itemPath).isDirectory() && /^\d+$/.test(item);
            })
            .map(id => parseInt(id));
    }

    getServerFiles(guildId) {
        const serverPath = this.getServerPath(guildId);
        if (!fs.existsSync(serverPath)) return [];

        return fs.readdirSync(serverPath).filter(f => f.endsWith('.json'));
    }

    // ============ ECONOMY DATA ============
    getEconomy(guildId) {
        return this.loadFile(guildId, 'economy.json');
    }

    saveEconomy(guildId, data) {
        this.saveFile(guildId, 'economy.json', data);
    }

    getUserBalance(guildId, userId) {
        const economy = this.getEconomy(guildId);
        return economy[userId.toString()] || 0;
    }

    setUserBalance(guildId, userId, amount) {
        const economy = this.getEconomy(guildId);
        economy[userId.toString()] = amount;
        this.saveEconomy(guildId, economy);
    }

    addUserBalance(guildId, userId, amount) {
        const current = this.getUserBalance(guildId, userId);
        this.setUserBalance(guildId, userId, current + amount);
    }

    // ============ GIVEAWAY DATA ============
    getGiveawayData(guildId) {
        return this.loadFile(guildId, 'giveaway_data.json');
    }

    saveGiveawayData(guildId, data) {
        this.saveFile(guildId, 'giveaway_data.json', data);
    }

    // ============ MESSAGE STATS ============
    incrementMessageCount(guildId, userId) {
        const data = this.loadFile(guildId, 'giveaway_data.json');

        if (!data.user_stats) data.user_stats = {};

        const userIdStr = userId.toString();
        const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];

        if (!data.user_stats[userIdStr]) {
            data.user_stats[userIdStr] = {
                total_messages: 0,
                daily_messages: 0,
                last_message_date: today
            };
        }

        const userStats = data.user_stats[userIdStr];

        // Reset daily if new day
        if (userStats.last_message_date !== today) {
            userStats.daily_messages = 0;
            userStats.last_message_date = today;
        }

        // Increment counts
        userStats.total_messages += 1;
        userStats.daily_messages += 1;

        data.user_stats[userIdStr] = userStats;
        this.saveFile(guildId, 'giveaway_data.json', data);
    }

    getUserMessageStats(guildId, userId) {
        const data = this.loadFile(guildId, 'giveaway_data.json');
        const userStats = data.user_stats?.[userId.toString()] || {
            total_messages: 0,
            daily_messages: 0
        };
        return {
            total: userStats.total_messages || 0,
            daily: userStats.daily_messages || 0
        };
    }

    // ============ COMMAND STATES ============
    getCommandStates(guildId) {
        return this.loadFile(guildId, 'command_states.json');
    }

    saveCommandStates(guildId, data) {
        this.saveFile(guildId, 'command_states.json', data);
    }

    isCommandEnabled(guildId, commandName) {
        const states = this.getCommandStates(guildId);
        return states[commandName] !== false; // Default: enabled
    }

    setCommandState(guildId, commandName, enabled) {
        const states = this.getCommandStates(guildId);
        states[commandName] = enabled;
        this.saveCommandStates(guildId, states);
    }

    // ============ SERVER CONFIG ============
    getConfig(guildId) {
        return this.loadFile(guildId, 'config.json');
    }

    saveConfig(guildId, config) {
        this.saveFile(guildId, 'config.json', config);
    }
}

console.log('✅ Data manager loaded');

module.exports = DataManager;

///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//                         HELPER FUNCTIONS                           //
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

// Parse time strings
function parseTime(timeStr) {
    const match = timeStr.toLowerCase().match(/^(\d+)(s|m|h|d|w|month|y)$/);
    if (!match) return null;

    const value = parseInt(match[1]);
    const unit = match[2];

    switch (unit) {
        case 's': return value * 1000;
        case 'm': return value * 60 * 1000;
        case 'h': return value * 60 * 60 * 1000;
        case 'd': return value * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
        case 'w': return value * 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
        case 'month': return value * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 * 30;
        case 'y': return value * 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
        default: return null;
    }
}

// Format milliseconds into readable time
function formatTime(ms) {
    const seconds = Math.floor(ms / 1000);
    
    if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds} seconds`;
    if (seconds < 3600) return `${Math.floor(seconds / 60)} minutes`;
    if (seconds < 86400) return `${Math.floor(seconds / 3600)} hours`;
    if (seconds < 604800) return `${Math.floor(seconds / 86400)} days`;
    if (seconds < 2592000) return `${Math.floor(seconds / 604800)} weeks`;
    if (seconds < 31536000) return `${Math.floor(seconds / 2592000)} months`;
    return `${Math.floor(seconds / 31536000)} years`;
}

// Format number with commas
function formatNumber(num) {
    return num.toString().replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ',');
}

// Generate random number between min and max
function randomInt(min, max) {
    return Math.floor(Math.random() * (max - min + 1)) + min;
}

// Weighted random selection
function weightedRandom(options) {
    const totalWeight = Object.values(options).reduce((sum, opt) => sum + opt.weight, 0);
    let random = Math.random() * totalWeight;

    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(options)) {
        random -= value.weight;
        if (random <= 0) return { key, ...value };
    }

    return Object.entries(options)[0]; // Fallback
}

// Ensure directory exists
function ensureDir(dirPath) {
    if (!fs.existsSync(dirPath)) {
        fs.mkdirSync(dirPath, { recursive: true });
    }
}

// Load JSON file
function loadJSON(filePath, defaultValue = {}) {
    if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
        saveJSON(filePath, defaultValue);
        return defaultValue;
    }

    try {
        const data = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
        return JSON.parse(data);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(`Error loading JSON from ${filePath}:`, error);
        return defaultValue;
    }
}

// Save JSON file
function saveJSON(filePath, data) {
    try {
        ensureDir(path.dirname(filePath));
        fs.writeFileSync(filePath, JSON.stringify(data, null, 4));
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(`Error saving JSON to ${filePath}:`, error);
    }
}

// Check if user has permission
function hasPermission(member, permission) {
    return member.permissions.has(permission);
}

// Check if user is owner
function isOwner(userId, ownerId) {
    return userId.toString() === ownerId.toString();
}

// Truncate string
function truncate(str, maxLength) {
    if (str.length <= maxLength) return str;
    return str.substring(0, maxLength) + '...';
}

// Sleep function
function sleep(ms) {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

// Get random element from array
function randomChoice(array) {
    return array[Math.floor(Math.random() * array.length)];
}

console.log('✅ Helper functions loaded');

module.exports = {
    parseTime,
    formatTime,
    formatNumber,
    randomInt,
    weightedRandom,
    ensureDir,
    loadJSON,
    saveJSON,
    hasPermission,
    isOwner,
    truncate,
    sleep,
    randomChoice
};

///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//                         SPAM DETECTOR                              //
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

const config = require('../config');
const SPAM_CONFIG = config.SPAM_CONFIG;

const userMessages = new Map();

async function checkSpam(message, client) {
    const userId = message.author.id;
    const now = Date.now();

    if (!userMessages.has(userId)) {
        userMessages.set(userId, []);
    }

    const messages = userMessages.get(userId);

    // Remove old messages outside the window
    const recentMessages = messages.filter(
        timestamp => now - timestamp < SPAM_CONFIG.WINDOW * 1000
    );

    recentMessages.push(now);
    userMessages.set(userId, recentMessages);

    // Check if spam threshold exceeded
    if (recentMessages.length > SPAM_CONFIG.THRESHOLD) {
        // Delete spam messages if enabled
        if (SPAM_CONFIG.DELETE_SPAM) {
            try {
                await message.delete();
            } catch (err) {
                // Ignore permission errors
            }
        }

        // Send warning
        const warning = await message.channel.send(
            `⚠️ ${message.author}, slow down! You're sending messages too quickly.`
        );

        // Delete warning after duration
        setTimeout(() => {
            warning.delete().catch(() => { });
        }, SPAM_CONFIG.WARNING_DURATION * 1000);

        // Auto-mute if enabled
        if (SPAM_CONFIG.AUTO_MUTE) {
            console.log("Checked spam!");
        }

        return true; // Is spam
    }

    return false; // Not spam
}

console.log('✅ Anti-spam system loaded');

module.exports = { checkSpam };

///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//                    TIMESTAMP HELPER UTILITIES                      //
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

/**
 * Discord timestamp format types
 */
const TimestampFormat = {
    SHORT_TIME: 't',        // 3:30 PM
    LONG_TIME: 'T',         // 3:30:45 PM
    SHORT_DATE: 'd',        // 12/31/2024
    LONG_DATE: 'D',         // December 31, 2024
    SHORT_DATETIME: 'f',    // December 31, 2024 3:30 PM (default)
    LONG_DATETIME: 'F',     // Sunday, December 31, 2024 3:30 PM
    RELATIVE: 'R'           // 2 hours ago / in 5 minutes
};

/**
 * Create Discord timestamp from Date or timestamp
 * @param {Date|number|string} time - Date object, timestamp (ms), or date string
 * @param {string} format - Format type (t, T, d, D, f, F, R)
 * @returns {string} Formatted Discord timestamp
 */
function createTimestamp(time, format = 'f') {
    let timestamp;

    if (time instanceof Date) {
        timestamp = Math.floor(time.getTime() / 1000);
    } else if (typeof time === 'number') {
        // If timestamp is in milliseconds, convert to seconds
        timestamp = time > 10000000000 ? Math.floor(time / 1000) : time;
    } else if (typeof time === 'string') {
        timestamp = Math.floor(new Date(time).getTime() / 1000);
    } else {
        throw new Error('Invalid time format');
    }

    return `<t:${timestamp}:${format}>`;
}

/**
 * Create multiple timestamp formats at once
 * @param {Date|number|string} time 
 * @returns {Object} Object with all timestamp formats
 */
function createAllTimestamps(time) {
    return {
        shortTime: createTimestamp(time, 't'),
        longTime: createTimestamp(time, 'T'),
        shortDate: createTimestamp(time, 'd'),
        longDate: createTimestamp(time, 'D'),
        shortDateTime: createTimestamp(time, 'f'),
        longDateTime: createTimestamp(time, 'F'),
        relative: createTimestamp(time, 'R')
    };
}

/**
 * Get timestamp for X time from now
 * @param {number} milliseconds - Milliseconds from now
 * @param {string} format - Format type
 * @returns {string} Discord timestamp
 */
function fromNow(milliseconds, format = 'R') {
    const futureTime = Date.now() + milliseconds;
    return createTimestamp(futureTime, format);
}

/**
 * Get timestamp for X time ago
 * @param {number} milliseconds - Milliseconds ago
 * @param {string} format - Format type
 * @returns {string} Discord timestamp
 */
function ago(milliseconds, format = 'R') {
    const pastTime = Date.now() - milliseconds;
    return createTimestamp(pastTime, format);
}

/**
 * Parse time string to milliseconds
 * @param {string} timeStr - Time string (10s, 5m, 2h, 1d)
 * @returns {number|null} Milliseconds or null if invalid
 */
function parseTime(timeStr) {
    if (!timeStr || typeof timeStr !== 'string') return null;

    const regex = /^(\d+)([smhdw])$/i;
    const match = timeStr.toLowerCase().match(regex);

    if (!match) return null;

    const value = parseInt(match[1]);
    const unit = match[2];

    const units = {
        's': 1000,           // seconds
        'm': 60000,          // minutes
        'h': 3600000,        // hours
        'd': 86400000,       // days
        'w': 604800000       // weeks
    };

    return value * (units[unit] || 0);
}

/**
 * Create timestamp from time string (10m, 2h, 1d)
 * @param {string} timeStr - Time string
 * @param {string} format - Format type
 * @returns {string} Discord timestamp
 */
function fromTimeString(timeStr, format = 'R') {
    const ms = parseTime(timeStr);
    if (!ms) throw new Error('Invalid time string format');
    return fromNow(ms, format);
}

/**
 * Format duration in milliseconds to readable string
 * @param {number} ms - Milliseconds
 * @returns {string} Formatted duration (e.g., "2h 30m 15s")
 */
function formatDuration(ms) {
    const seconds = Math.floor(ms / 1000);
    const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
    const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
    const days = Math.floor(hours / 24);

    const parts = [];

    if (days > 0) parts.push(`${days}d`);
    if (hours % 24 > 0) parts.push(`${hours % 24}h`);
    if (minutes % 60 > 0) parts.push(`${minutes % 60}m`);
    if (seconds % 60 > 0) parts.push(`${seconds % 60}s`);

    return parts.join(' ') || '0s';
}

/**
 * Get Unix timestamp in seconds
 * @param {Date|number} time - Date or timestamp
 * @returns {number} Unix timestamp in seconds
 */
function getUnixTimestamp(time = Date.now()) {
    if (time instanceof Date) {
        return Math.floor(time.getTime() / 1000);
    }
    return Math.floor(time / 1000);
}

/**
 * Check if time has passed
 * @param {number} timestamp - Unix timestamp in seconds or milliseconds
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function hasPassed(timestamp) {
    const ts = timestamp > 10000000000 ? timestamp : timestamp * 1000;
    return Date.now() >= ts;
}

/**
 * Get time remaining until timestamp
 * @param {number} timestamp - Unix timestamp in seconds or milliseconds
 * @returns {number} Milliseconds remaining (0 if passed)
 */
function getTimeRemaining(timestamp) {
    const ts = timestamp > 10000000000 ? timestamp : timestamp * 1000;
    const remaining = ts - Date.now();
    return Math.max(0, remaining);
}

module.exports = {
    // Constants
    TimestampFormat,

    // Main functions
    createTimestamp,
    createAllTimestamps,

    // Relative time
    fromNow,
    ago,
    fromTimeString,

    // Parsing
    parseTime,
    formatDuration,

    // Unix timestamps
    getUnixTimestamp,

    // Utilities
    hasPassed,
    getTimeRemaining
};
